### api-app/test/instagram-cp2-finalization.e2e-spec.ts — 19779 bytes SHA256 223739cd8cd4a529203cd8e686b297f2e51c53147be35b5916f1f353e99d3fe5

```text

import request from 'supertest';
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { INestApplication } from '@nestjs/common';
import { createHmac, randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import type { Prisma } from '@prisma/client';
import { PrismaService } from '../src/infra/prisma/prisma.service';
import { criarMantenedora, instagramFake, INSTAGRAM_WEBHOOK_SECRET, limparBanco, login, subirApp } from './helpers';

const awaitBarrier = async <T>(signal: Promise<T>, label: string, timeoutMs = 4_000): Promise<T> => {
  let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
  try {
    return await Promise.race([signal, new Promise<T>((_, reject) => {
      timer = setTimeout(() => reject(new Error(`Timed out waiting for ${label} after ${timeoutMs}ms`)), timeoutMs);
    })]);
  } finally { if (timer) clearTimeout(timer); }
};

describe('Instagram CP2 post-provider finalization and replay proofs', () => {
  let app: INestApplication;
  let prisma: PrismaService;

  beforeAll(async () => {
    ({ app, prisma } = await subirApp());
    await app.listen(0, '127.0.0.1');
    await limparBanco(prisma);
    instagramFake.afterRequestHook = null;
  });

  afterAll(async () => { await app?.close(); });

  const makeCandidate = async (label: string) => {
    const tenant = await criarMantenedora(app, `Instagram finalization ${label}`, `instagram-finalization-${label}@example.test`);
    const cookie = await login(app, `instagram-finalization-${label}@example.test`);
    const accountId = `synthetic-finalization-${label}`;
    instagramFake.subscribed = false;
    instagramFake.failAfterSubscription = false;
    instagramFake.subscribedEnvelope = null;
    instagramFake.meEnvelope = { user_id: accountId, username: `finalization_${label}` };
    instagramFake.subscribeEnvelope = { success: true };
    const created = await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/instagram-authorizations`)
      .set('cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').set('idempotency-key', randomUUID()).send({ operation: 'CONNECT' }).expect(201);
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).get('/api/bff/instagram-authorizations/callback').set('cookie', cookie)
      .query({ state: new URL(created.body.authorizationUrl).searchParams.get('state'), code: `synthetic-finalization-${label}-oauth-code` }).expect(303);
    return { tenant, cookie, accountId, attemptId: created.body.id as string };
  };

  it('matched no-fault control finalizes the settled POST once and releases its binding holder', async () => {
    const fixture = await makeCandidate('no-fault-control');
    const callsBefore = instagramFake.calls.length;
    const confirmation = await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/instagram-authorizations/${fixture.attemptId}/confirm`)
      .set('cookie', fixture.cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').set('idempotency-key', randomUUID()).send({}).expect(200);
    expect(confirmation.body.subscriptionState).toBe('SUBSCRIBED');
    const attempt = await prisma.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: fixture.attemptId } });
    expect(attempt.status).toBe('MAPPED_PENDING_RECEIPT');
    expect(attempt.subscriptionRequests).toBe(1);
    const binding = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: {
      provider_externalResourceId: { provider: 'INSTAGRAM', externalResourceId: fixture.accountId },
    } });
    expect(binding.instagramOperationLeaseToken).toBeNull();
    expect(instagramFake.calls.slice(callsBefore).filter((call) => call.method === 'POST' && call.url.endsWith('/subscribed_apps'))).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('settled successful POST finalization fault rolls back, releases its exact holder, and original-key recovery uses an independent signed receipt without another POST', async () => {
    const fixture = await makeCandidate('finalization-fault');
    const suffix = randomUUID().replace(/-/g, '');
    if (!/^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-f]{3}-[89ab][0-9a-f]{3}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i.test(fixture.attemptId)) {
      throw new Error('Expected generated authorization attempt UUID before trigger interpolation');
    }
    const triggerName = `instagram_test_finalization_fault_${suffix}`;
    const functionName = `instagram_test_finalization_function_${suffix}`;
    await prisma.$executeRawUnsafe(`CREATE FUNCTION public."${functionName}"() RETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $body$
      BEGIN RAISE EXCEPTION 'synthetic final subscription write fault'; END; $body$`);
    await prisma.$executeRawUnsafe(`CREATE TRIGGER "${triggerName}" BEFORE UPDATE ON instagram_authorization_attempts
      FOR EACH ROW WHEN (NEW.id = '${fixture.attemptId}' AND NEW.status = 'MAPPED_PENDING_RECEIPT' AND NEW.subscription_state = 'SUBSCRIBED')
      EXECUTE FUNCTION public."${functionName}"()`);
    const idempotencyKey = randomUUID();
    const callsBefore = instagramFake.calls.length;
    let holderReleasedBeforeReceipt!: boolean;
    let exactHolderAtPost: string | null = null;
    instagramFake.afterRequestHook = async (method, url) => {
      if (method === 'POST' && url.pathname.endsWith('/subscribed_apps')) {
        const binding = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: {
          provider_externalResourceId: { provider: 'INSTAGRAM', externalResourceId: fixture.accountId },
        } });
        exactHolderAtPost = binding.instagramOperationLeaseToken;
      }
    };
    try {
      const failedFinalization = await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/instagram-authorizations/${fixture.attemptId}/confirm`)
        .set('cookie', fixture.cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').set('idempotency-key', idempotencyKey).send({});
      expect(failedFinalization.status).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(500);
      expect(instagramFake.calls.slice(callsBefore).filter((call) => call.method === 'POST' && call.url.endsWith('/subscribed_apps'))).toHaveLength(1);
      const attemptAtFault = await prisma.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: fixture.attemptId } });
      expect(attemptAtFault.status).toBe('MAPPED_PENDING_SUBSCRIPTION');
      expect(attemptAtFault.subscriptionRequests).toBe(1);
      expect(attemptAtFault.confirmationState).toBe('IN_PROGRESS');
      const bindingAtFault = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: {
        provider_externalResourceId: { provider: 'INSTAGRAM', externalResourceId: fixture.accountId },
      } });
      expect(bindingAtFault.instagramLastSubscriptionAttemptAt).not.toBeNull();
      expect(bindingAtFault.instagramSubscriptionPostDispatchStartedAt).not.toBeNull();
      holderReleasedBeforeReceipt = bindingAtFault.instagramOperationLeaseToken === null
        && bindingAtFault.instagramOperationLeaseUntil === null;
      expect(exactHolderAtPost).not.toBeNull();
    } finally {
      await prisma.$executeRawUnsafe(`DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS "${triggerName}" ON instagram_authorization_attempts`);
      await prisma.$executeRawUnsafe(`DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS public."${functionName}"()`);
      instagramFake.afterRequestHook = null;
    }

    const rawReceipt = JSON.stringify({ object: 'instagram', entry: [{ id: fixture.accountId, messaging: [{
      sender: { id: `synthetic-finalization-sender-${suffix}` }, recipient: { id: fixture.accountId }, timestamp: Date.now(),
      message: { mid: `synthetic-finalization-receipt-${suffix}`, text: 'provider receipt proves subscription independently' },
    }] }] });
    const signature = `sha256=${createHmac('sha256', INSTAGRAM_WEBHOOK_SECRET).update(rawReceipt).digest('hex')}`;
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/webhooks/instagram').set('content-type', 'application/json')
      .set('x-hub-signature-256', signature).send(rawReceipt).expect(200);
    const receiptChannel = await prisma.buChannel.findFirstOrThrow({ where: {
      buId: fixture.tenant.businessUnit.id, type: 'INSTAGRAM', businessIdentity: { externalResourceId: fixture.accountId },
    } });
    expect(receiptChannel.receiptVerifiedAt).not.toBeNull();
    const postsBeforeReplay = instagramFake.calls.filter((call) => call.method === 'POST' && call.url.endsWith('/subscribed_apps')).length;
    const replay = await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/instagram-authorizations/${fixture.attemptId}/confirm`)
      .set('cookie', fixture.cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').set('idempotency-key', idempotencyKey).send({}).expect(200);
    expect(replay.body).toMatchObject({ status: 'MAPPED_PENDING_RECEIPT', channelId: receiptChannel.id, subscriptionState: 'SUBSCRIBED' });
    expect(instagramFake.calls.filter((call) => call.method === 'POST' && call.url.endsWith('/subscribed_apps'))).toHaveLength(postsBeforeReplay);
    expect((await prisma.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: fixture.attemptId } })).subscriptionRequests).toBe(1);
    const bindingAfterRecovery = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: {
      provider_externalResourceId: { provider: 'INSTAGRAM', externalResourceId: fixture.accountId },
    } });
    expect(bindingAfterRecovery.instagramLastSubscriptionAttemptAt).not.toBeNull();
    expect(bindingAfterRecovery.instagramSubscriptionPostDispatchStartedAt).not.toBeNull();
    expect(holderReleasedBeforeReceipt).toBe(true);
  });

  it('successful GET/null continuation control preserves UNKNOWN and the durable no-repeat POST budget', async () => {
    const fixture = await makeCandidate('get-null-control');
    instagramFake.failAfterSubscription = true;
    const originalKey = randomUUID();
    const first = await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/instagram-authorizations/${fixture.attemptId}/confirm`)
      .set('cookie', fixture.cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').set('idempotency-key', originalKey).send({}).expect(200);
    expect(first.body.subscriptionState).toBe('UNCERTAIN');
    const afterPost = await prisma.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: fixture.attemptId } });
    expect(afterPost.subscriptionRequests).toBe(1);
    expect(afterPost.subscriptionState).toBe('UNCERTAIN');
    await prisma.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.update({ where: { id: fixture.attemptId }, data: { lastSubscriptionAttemptAt: new Date(Date.now() - 10_001) } });
    await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.update({ where: {
      provider_externalResourceId: { provider: 'INSTAGRAM', externalResourceId: fixture.accountId },
    }, data: { instagramLastSubscriptionAttemptAt: new Date(Date.now() - 10_001) } });
    const callsBeforeAdvance = instagramFake.calls.length;
    const advanced = await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/instagram-authorizations/${fixture.attemptId}/advance`)
      .set('cookie', fixture.cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').send({}).expect(200);
    expect(advanced.body.subscriptionState).toBe('UNCERTAIN');
    expect(instagramFake.calls.slice(callsBeforeAdvance).filter((call) => call.method === 'GET' && call.url.endsWith('/subscribed_apps'))).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(instagramFake.calls.slice(callsBeforeAdvance).filter((call) => call.method === 'POST' && call.url.endsWith('/subscribed_apps'))).toHaveLength(0);
    const finalAttempt = await prisma.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: fixture.attemptId } });
    expect(finalAttempt.subscriptionRequests).toBe(afterPost.subscriptionRequests + 1);
    expect(finalAttempt.subscriptionRequests).toBe(2);
    expect(finalAttempt.subscriptionState).toBe('UNCERTAIN');
  });

  it('successful GET/null continuation denies a takeover queued between owner postcheck and observation finalization', async () => {
    const fixture = await makeCandidate('get-null-fifo-takeover');
    instagramFake.failAfterSubscription = true;
    const first = await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/instagram-authorizations/${fixture.attemptId}/confirm`)
      .set('cookie', fixture.cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').set('idempotency-key', randomUUID()).send({}).expect(200);
    expect(first.body.subscriptionState).toBe('UNCERTAIN');
    const beforeAdvance = await prisma.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: fixture.attemptId } });
    expect(beforeAdvance.subscriptionRequests).toBe(1);
    const retryAt = new Date(Date.now() - 10_001);
    await prisma.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.update({ where: { id: fixture.attemptId }, data: { lastSubscriptionAttemptAt: retryAt } });
    await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.update({ where: {
      provider_externalResourceId: { provider: 'INSTAGRAM', externalResourceId: fixture.accountId },
    }, data: { instagramLastSubscriptionAttemptAt: retryAt } });

    const lockKey = `instagram-resource:${fixture.accountId}`;
    const startHolder = (afterAdvisory?: (tx: Prisma.TransactionClient) => Promise<void>) => {
      let pidReady!: (pid: number) => void;
      let acquired!: () => void;
      let release!: () => void;
      const pidPromise = new Promise<number>((resolve) => { pidReady = resolve; });
      const acquiredPromise = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { acquired = resolve; });
      const released = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { release = resolve; });
      const transaction = prisma.$transaction(async (tx) => {
        const [row] = await tx.$queryRaw<Array<{ pid: number }>>`SELECT pg_backend_pid() AS pid`;
        pidReady(row.pid);
        await tx.$queryRaw`SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended(${lockKey}, 0))::text AS locked`;
        await afterAdvisory?.(tx);
        acquired();
        await released;
      });
      return { pidPromise, acquiredPromise, transaction, release: () => release() };
    };
    const waitBlockedPid = async (pid: number, blockerPid?: number) => {
      const deadline = Date.now() + 3_000;
      while (Date.now() < deadline) {
        const rows = blockerPid === undefined
          ? await prisma.$queryRaw<Array<{ pid: number }>>`SELECT activity.pid FROM pg_stat_activity AS activity
              WHERE activity.pid = ${pid} AND activity.wait_event_type = 'Lock' AND activity.query ILIKE '%pg_advisory_xact_lock%'`
          : await prisma.$queryRaw<Array<{ pid: number }>>`SELECT activity.pid FROM pg_stat_activity AS activity
              WHERE activity.pid = ${pid} AND activity.wait_event_type = 'Lock' AND ${blockerPid} = ANY(pg_blocking_pids(activity.pid))
                AND activity.query ILIKE '%pg_advisory_xact_lock%'`;
        if (rows.length > 0) return true;
        await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 10));
      }
      return false;
    };
    const waitBlockedQuery = async (blockerPid: number) => {
      const deadline = Date.now() + 3_000;
      while (Date.now() < deadline) {
        const rows = await prisma.$queryRaw<Array<{ pid: number }>>`SELECT activity.pid FROM pg_stat_activity AS activity
          WHERE activity.wait_event_type = 'Lock' AND ${blockerPid} = ANY(pg_blocking_pids(activity.pid))
            AND activity.query ILIKE '%pg_advisory_xact_lock%' ORDER BY activity.query_start LIMIT 1`;
        if (rows.length > 0) return rows[0].pid;
        await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 10));
      }
      return null;
    };

    let holderA: ReturnType<typeof startHolder> | undefined;
    let holderB: ReturnType<typeof startHolder> | undefined;
    let holderC: ReturnType<typeof startHolder> | undefined;
    let advance: Promise<{ status: number; body: { code?: string; subscriptionState?: string } }> | undefined;
    let attemptAtHook: typeof beforeAdvance | undefined;
    let getHookEntered!: () => void;
    const getHookReached = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { getHookEntered = resolve; });
    instagramFake.subscribedEnvelope = { data: [] };
    instagramFake.afterRequestHook = async (method, url) => {
      if (method === 'GET' && url.pathname.endsWith('/subscribed_apps')) {
        holderA = startHolder();
        await holderA.acquiredPromise;
        attemptAtHook = await prisma.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: fixture.attemptId } });
        getHookEntered();
      }
    };
    const callsBeforeAdvance = instagramFake.calls.length;
    try {
      advance = request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/instagram-authorizations/${fixture.attemptId}/advance`)
        .set('cookie', fixture.cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').send({})
        .then((response) => ({ status: response.status, body: response.body }));
      await awaitBarrier(getHookReached, 'successful GET response barrier');
      const pidA = await holderA!.pidPromise;
      const postResponseGuardPid = await waitBlockedQuery(pidA);
      expect(postResponseGuardPid).not.toBeNull();

      holderB = startHolder();
      const pidB = await holderB.pidPromise;
      expect(await waitBlockedPid(pidB)).toBe(true);
      holderA!.release();
      await holderA!.transaction;
      await holderB!.acquiredPromise;

      const providerCallPostcheckPid = await waitBlockedQuery(pidB);
      expect(providerCallPostcheckPid).not.toBeNull();
      const successorToken = `synthetic-successor-${randomUUID()}`;
      holderC = startHolder(async (tx) => {
        await tx.$queryRaw`SELECT id FROM provider_resource_identities WHERE provider = 'INSTAGRAM'
          AND external_resource_id = ${fixture.accountId} FOR UPDATE`;
        await tx.providerResourceIdentity.update({ where: {
          provider_externalResourceId: { provider: 'INSTAGRAM', externalResourceId: fixture.accountId },
        }, data: { instagramOperationLeaseToken: successorToken, instagramOperationLeaseUntil: new Date(Date.now() + 60_000) } });
      });
      const pidC = await holderC.pidPromise;
      expect(await waitBlockedPid(pidC)).toBe(true);
      holderB!.release();
      await holderB!.transaction;
      // C's acquired signal follows its same-transaction successor mutation.
      await holderC!.acquiredPromise;

      const observationFinalizationPid = await waitBlockedQuery(pidC);
      expect(observationFinalizationPid).not.toBeNull();
      holderC!.release();
      await holderC!.transaction;

      const response = await advance;
      expect(response.status).toBe(409);
      expect(response.body.code).toBe('SUBSCRIPTION_CONTEXT_STALE');
      expect(instagramFake.calls.slice(callsBeforeAdvance).filter((call) => call.method === 'GET' && call.url.endsWith('/subscribed_apps'))).toHaveLength(1);
      expect(instagramFake.calls.slice(callsBeforeAdvance).filter((call) => call.method === 'POST' && call.url.endsWith('/subscribed_apps'))).toHaveLength(0);
      const attemptAfter = await prisma.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: fixture.attemptId } });
      expect(attemptAfter).toMatchObject({
        status: attemptAtHook!.status, subscriptionState: attemptAtHook!.subscriptionState,
        subscriptionRequests: attemptAtHook!.subscriptionRequests, operationLeaseToken: attemptAtHook!.operationLeaseToken,
      });
      const bindingAfter = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: {
        provider_externalResourceId: { provider: 'INSTAGRAM', externalResourceId: fixture.accountId },
      } });
      expect(bindingAfter.instagramOperationLeaseToken).toBe(successorToken);
      expect(bindingAfter.instagramOperationLeaseUntil!.getTime()).toBeGreaterThan(Date.now());
    } finally {
      instagramFake.afterRequestHook = null;
      holderA?.release(); holderB?.release(); holderC?.release();
      await Promise.allSettled([...(holderA ? [holderA.transaction] : []), ...(holderB ? [holderB.transaction] : []), ...(holderC ? [holderC.transaction] : []), ...(advance ? [advance] : [])]);
      instagramFake.subscribedEnvelope = null;
    }
  });
});
```

### api-app/test/instagram-cp2-recovery.e2e-spec.ts — 8663 bytes SHA256 a75d9687f300486c9acf9ed38587f9fa507fcaeecd11588af5530e631cb1f856

```text
import request from 'supertest';
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { INestApplication } from '@nestjs/common';
import { createHmac, randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import { PrismaService } from '../src/infra/prisma/prisma.service';
import { criarMantenedora, instagramFake, INSTAGRAM_WEBHOOK_SECRET, limparBanco, login, subirApp } from './helpers';

describe('Instagram CP2 committed writer recovery', () => {
  let app: INestApplication;
  let prisma: PrismaService;

  beforeAll(async () => {
    ({ app, prisma } = await subirApp());
    await app.listen(0, '127.0.0.1');
    await limparBanco(prisma);
    instagramFake.calls.length = 0;
    instagramFake.afterRequestHook = null;
    instagramFake.failNext = false;
    instagramFake.redirectNext = false;
    instagramFake.shortEnvelope = null;
    instagramFake.longEnvelope = null;
    instagramFake.meEnvelope = null;
    instagramFake.subscribedEnvelope = null;
    instagramFake.subscribeEnvelope = null;
    instagramFake.failAfterSubscription = false;
    instagramFake.subscribed = false;
  });

  afterAll(async () => { await app?.close(); });

  it('replays the original confirmation after a real PostgreSQL fault between mapping commit and result commit', async () => {
    const tenant = await criarMantenedora(app, 'Instagram committed writer recovery', 'instagram-committed-recovery@example.test');
    const cookie = await login(app, 'instagram-committed-recovery@example.test');
    const accountId = 'synthetic-committed-writer-recovery-account';
    instagramFake.meEnvelope = { user_id: accountId, username: 'committed_recovery' };
    instagramFake.subscribed = false;
    const created = await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/instagram-authorizations`)
      .set('cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').set('idempotency-key', randomUUID()).send({ operation: 'CONNECT' }).expect(201);
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).get('/api/bff/instagram-authorizations/callback').set('cookie', cookie)
      .query({ state: new URL(created.body.authorizationUrl).searchParams.get('state'), code: 'synthetic-committed-recovery-code' }).expect(303);

    const attemptId = String(created.body.id);
    if (!/^[0-9a-f-]{36}$/i.test(attemptId)) throw new Error('Generated attempt ID was not a UUID.');
    const trigger = 'ig_cp2_recovery_exact_attempt';
    const functionName = 'ig_cp2_recovery_fault';
    await prisma.$executeRawUnsafe(`CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION ${functionName}() RETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$
      BEGIN
        IF NEW.id = '${attemptId}' AND NEW.confirmation_state = 'COMPLETED' THEN
          RAISE EXCEPTION 'synthetic committed-writer result fault';
        END IF;
        RETURN NEW;
      END; $$`);
    await prisma.$executeRawUnsafe(`CREATE TRIGGER ${trigger} BEFORE UPDATE ON instagram_authorization_attempts
      FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION ${functionName}()`);

    const receiptBody = JSON.stringify({ object: 'instagram', entry: [{ id: accountId, messaging: [{
      sender: { id: 'opaque-committed-recovery-peer' }, recipient: { id: accountId }, timestamp: Date.now(),
      message: { mid: 'synthetic-committed-recovery-receipt', text: 'signed receipt is independent of confirmation result storage' },
    }] }] });
    const receiptSignature = `sha256=${createHmac('sha256', INSTAGRAM_WEBHOOK_SECRET).update(receiptBody).digest('hex')}`;
    instagramFake.afterRequestHook = async (method, url) => {
      if (method === 'POST' && url.pathname.endsWith('/subscribed_apps')) {
        await request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/webhooks/instagram').set('content-type', 'application/json')
          .set('x-hub-signature-256', receiptSignature).send(receiptBody).expect(200);
      }
    };

    const confirmationKey = randomUUID();
    const postsBefore = instagramFake.calls.filter((call) => call.method === 'POST' && call.url.endsWith('/subscribed_apps')).length;
    try {
      const interrupted = await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/instagram-authorizations/${attemptId}/confirm`)
        .set('cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').set('idempotency-key', confirmationKey).send({});
      expect(interrupted.status).toBe(500);

      const mapped = await prisma.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: attemptId } });
      const channel = await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: mapped.channelId! }, include: { credential: true } });
      expect(mapped.status).toBe('MAPPED_PENDING_RECEIPT');
      expect(mapped.confirmationState).toBe('IN_PROGRESS');
      expect(mapped.confirmationIdempotencyKey).toBe(confirmationKey);
      expect(mapped.confirmationRequestHash).toBeTruthy();
      expect(mapped.expectedGeneration).toBeNull();
      expect(mapped.mappedCredentialVersion).toBe(1);
      expect(channel.credential?.version).toBe(1);
      expect(channel.credential?.generation).toBe(channel.generation);
      expect(channel.credential?.ciphertext).toBeTruthy();
      const resource = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { provider: 'INSTAGRAM', externalResourceId: accountId } });
      expect(resource.currentChannelId).toBe(channel.id);
      expect(instagramFake.calls.filter((call) => call.method === 'POST' && call.url.endsWith('/subscribed_apps'))).toHaveLength(postsBefore + 1);
      const ciphertext = Buffer.from(channel.credential!.ciphertext).toString('hex');
      const nonce = Buffer.from(channel.credential!.nonce).toString('hex');
      const tag = Buffer.from(channel.credential!.tag).toString('hex');
      const generation = channel.credential!.generation;
      const acquiredAt = channel.credential!.acquiredAt;
      const expiresAt = channel.credential!.expiresAt;

      // The synthetic fault targets only the original result commit. Remove it
      // after that committed-writer boundary is proven so replay can complete.
      await prisma.$executeRawUnsafe(`DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS ${trigger} ON instagram_authorization_attempts`);
      await prisma.$executeRawUnsafe(`DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS ${functionName}()`);

      const replay = await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/instagram-authorizations/${attemptId}/confirm`)
        .set('cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').set('idempotency-key', confirmationKey).send({}).expect(200);
      expect(replay.body).toMatchObject({ id: attemptId, status: 'MAPPED_PENDING_RECEIPT', channelId: channel.id, subscriptionState: 'SUBSCRIBED' });
      const completed = await prisma.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: attemptId } });
      const after = await prisma.channelCredential.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { channelId: channel.id } });
      expect(completed.confirmationState).toBe('COMPLETED');
      expect(completed.confirmationIdempotencyKey).toBe(confirmationKey);
      expect(completed.confirmationRequestHash).toBe(mapped.confirmationRequestHash);
      expect(completed.expectedGeneration).toBe(mapped.expectedGeneration);
      expect(after.version).toBe(1);
      expect(after.generation).toBe(generation);
      expect(Buffer.from(after.nonce).toString('hex')).toBe(nonce);
      expect(Buffer.from(after.tag).toString('hex')).toBe(tag);
      expect(Buffer.from(after.ciphertext).toString('hex')).toBe(ciphertext);
      expect(after.acquiredAt).toEqual(acquiredAt);
      expect(after.expiresAt).toEqual(expiresAt);
      expect(instagramFake.calls.filter((call) => call.method === 'POST' && call.url.endsWith('/subscribed_apps'))).toHaveLength(postsBefore + 1);

      await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/instagram-authorizations/${attemptId}/confirm`)
        .set('cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').set('idempotency-key', confirmationKey).send({ expectedGeneration: 1 }).expect(409);
      const completedReplay = await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/instagram-authorizations/${attemptId}/confirm`)
        .set('cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').set('idempotency-key', confirmationKey).send({}).expect(200);
      expect(completedReplay.body).toEqual(replay.body);
      expect(instagramFake.calls.filter((call) => call.method === 'POST' && call.url.endsWith('/subscribed_apps'))).toHaveLength(postsBefore + 1);
    } finally {
      await prisma.$executeRawUnsafe(`DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS ${trigger} ON instagram_authorization_attempts`);
      await prisma.$executeRawUnsafe(`DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS ${functionName}()`);
      instagramFake.afterRequestHook = null;
      instagramFake.meEnvelope = null;
    }
  });
});
```
