### api-app/test/instagram-cp4-connections.e2e-spec.ts — 69247 bytes SHA256 4295a591e926a7e0e21d177387f1b9d06e12a1e4ae35ff92ad5dfccf6b55b947

```text

import type { INestApplication } from '@nestjs/common';
import request from 'supertest';
import { createHmac, randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import { afterAll, beforeAll, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { PrismaService } from '../src/infra/prisma/prisma.service';
import { ChannelCredentialResolver } from '../src/domain/channels/channel-credential-resolver';
import { ChannelsService } from '../src/domain/channels/channels.service';
import { SESSION_COOKIE, SessionService } from '../src/infra/session/session.service';
import {
  criarCanalVerificado, criarMantenedora, criarUsuario, evolutionFake, instagramFake,
  INSTAGRAM_WEBHOOK_SECRET, limparBanco, login, metaFake, subirApp, tetoComCanalNaoOficial,
} from './helpers';

const waitFor = async <T>(signal: Promise<T>, label: string, timeoutMs = 8_000): Promise<T> => {
  let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
  try {
    return await Promise.race([signal, new Promise<T>((_, reject) => {
      timer = setTimeout(() => reject(new Error(`Timed out waiting for ${label} after ${timeoutMs}ms`)), timeoutMs);
    })]);
  } finally { if (timer) clearTimeout(timer); }
};

const domainStatements = (events: Array<{ query: string; params: string }>) => events.filter(({ query }) =>
  !/^\s*(?:BEGIN|START\s+TRANSACTION|COMMIT|ROLLBACK|SAVEPOINT|RELEASE|SET\s+TRANSACTION)\b/i.test(query));
const assertBoundedReadOnly = (events: Array<{ query: string; params: string }>, maximum = 8) => {
  const statements = domainStatements(events);
  expect(statements.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(maximum);
  expect(statements.some(({ query }) => /\b(?:INSERT|UPDATE|DELETE|MERGE|TRUNCATE)\b/i.test(query))).toBe(false);
};

describe('Instagram CP4 connections and current UUID lifecycle (real Nest/PostgreSQL)', () => {
  let app: INestApplication;
  let prisma: PrismaService;
  let tenant: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof criarMantenedora>>;
  let cookie = '';
  let sessions: SessionService;
  const queryEvents: Array<{ query: string; params: string }> = [];

  beforeAll(async () => {
    tetoComCanalNaoOficial();
    ({ app, prisma } = await subirApp(undefined, true));
    sessions = app.get(SessionService);
    prisma.$on('query', (event) => queryEvents.push(event));
    await app.listen(0, '127.0.0.1');
  });

  beforeEach(async () => {
    await limparBanco(prisma);
    queryEvents.length = 0;
    tenant = await criarMantenedora(app, 'Instagram CP4 connections', `instagram-cp4-${randomUUID()}@example.test`);
    cookie = await login(app, tenant.owner.email);
    instagramFake.meEnvelope = null;
    instagramFake.subscribedEnvelope = null;
    instagramFake.subscribed = false;
    metaFake.confirmHook = null;
    evolutionFake.inventory.clear();
    evolutionFake.ownerPhone = null;
  });

  afterAll(async () => { if (prisma) await limparBanco(prisma); await app?.close(); });

  const bff = (method: 'get' | 'post', path: string, session = cookie) => {
    const req = request(app.getHttpServer())[method](path).set('cookie', session);
    return method === 'post' ? req.set('x-requested-with', 'teste') : req;
  };

  const pause = (ms: number) => new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
  const waitForResourceWaiter = async (pid: number, query: 'COMMIT' | 'UPDATE') => {
    const deadline = performance.now() + 3_000;
    while (performance.now() < deadline) {
      const rows = query === 'COMMIT'
        ? await prisma.$queryRaw<Array<{ pid: number }>>`SELECT a.pid FROM pg_stat_activity a WHERE a.wait_event_type = 'Lock' AND ${pid} = ANY(pg_blocking_pids(a.pid)) AND a.query ILIKE 'COMMIT%'`
        : await prisma.$queryRaw<Array<{ pid: number }>>`SELECT a.pid FROM pg_stat_activity a WHERE a.wait_event_type = 'Lock'
          AND a.query ILIKE '%UPDATE provider_resource_identities%'
          AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_stat_activity b WHERE b.pid = ANY(pg_blocking_pids(a.pid))
            AND ${pid} = ANY(pg_blocking_pids(b.pid)))`;
      if (rows.length) return true;
      await pause(10);
    }
    return false;
  };
  const resourceCommitBarrier = async (identityId: string) => {
    if (!/^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-f]{3}-[89ab][0-9a-f]{3}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i.test(identityId)) {
      throw new Error('Expected generated text UUID before PostgreSQL trigger interpolation');
    }
    const suffix = randomUUID().replace(/-/g, '');
    const trigger = `instagram_verify_trigger_${suffix}`;
    const fn = `instagram_verify_function_${suffix}`;
    const key = `instagram-verify:${suffix}`;
    const lockExpr = `pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended('${key}', 0))`;
    let functionCreated = false;
    let triggerCreated = false;
    let releaseGate!: () => void;
    let acquireHolder!: () => void;
    const gate = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { releaseGate = resolve; });
    const held = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { acquireHolder = resolve; });
    let holderPid = 0;
    let holderOutcome: Promise<void> | undefined;
    let released = false;
    let cleanupTask: Promise<void> | undefined;
    const release = () => { if (!released) { released = true; releaseGate(); } };
    const dropArtifacts = async () => {
      try {
        if (triggerCreated) await prisma.$executeRawUnsafe(`DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS "${trigger}" ON provider_resource_identities`);
      } finally {
        if (functionCreated) await prisma.$executeRawUnsafe(`DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS public."${fn}"()`);
      }
    };
    try {
      await prisma.$executeRawUnsafe(`CREATE FUNCTION public."${fn}"() RETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $body$ BEGIN PERFORM ${lockExpr}; RETURN NEW; END; $body$`);
      functionCreated = true;
      await prisma.$executeRawUnsafe(`CREATE CONSTRAINT TRIGGER "${trigger}" AFTER UPDATE ON provider_resource_identities DEFERRABLE INITIALLY DEFERRED FOR EACH ROW WHEN (OLD.id = '${identityId}' AND OLD.instagram_operation_lease_token IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.instagram_operation_lease_token AND NEW.instagram_operation_lease_token IS NOT NULL) EXECUTE FUNCTION public."${fn}"()`);
      triggerCreated = true;
      const holder = prisma.$transaction(async (tx) => {
        const [row] = await tx.$queryRaw<Array<{ pid: number }>>`SELECT pg_backend_pid() AS pid`;
        holderPid = row.pid;
        await tx.$queryRaw`SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended(${key}, 0))::text AS locked`;
        acquireHolder();
        await gate;
      });
      holderOutcome = holder;
      await waitFor(held, 'provider-resource commit barrier holder');
      return {
        holderPid,
        release,
        cleanup: () => cleanupTask ??= (async () => {
          release();
          try { await waitFor(holderOutcome!, 'provider-resource commit barrier completion'); }
          finally { await dropArtifacts(); }
        })(),
      };
    } catch (setupError) {
      release();
      try { if (holderOutcome) await waitFor(holderOutcome, 'failed provider-resource barrier cleanup'); }
      finally { await dropArtifacts(); }
      throw setupError;
    }
  };

  const authorize = async (
    session: string, buId: string, accountId: string, username: string,
    operation: 'CONNECT' | 'REAUTHORIZE' = 'CONNECT', channelId?: string, expectedGeneration?: number,
  ) => {
    instagramFake.meEnvelope = { user_id: accountId, username };
    const payload = operation === 'CONNECT' ? { operation } : { operation, channelId, expectedGeneration };
    const reserved = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${buId}/instagram-authorizations`, session)
      .set('idempotency-key', randomUUID()).send(payload).expect(201);
    const state = new URL(reserved.body.authorizationUrl).searchParams.get('state');
    expect(state).toBeTruthy();
    const callbackCode = `synthetic-cp4-code-${accountId}-${randomUUID()}`;
    await bff('get', '/api/bff/instagram-authorizations/callback', session)
      .query({ state, code: callbackCode }).expect(303);
    const preconfirm = await bff('get', `/api/bff/instagram-authorizations/${reserved.body.id}`, session).expect(200);
    expect(preconfirm.body).toMatchObject({ id: reserved.body.id, buId, status: 'CANDIDATE_READY', operation,
      channelId: operation === 'REAUTHORIZE' ? channelId : null,
      expectedGeneration: operation === 'REAUTHORIZE' ? expectedGeneration : null,
      account: { username }, expiresAt: expect.any(String), subscriptionState: expect.any(String) });
    const safeAttempt = JSON.stringify(preconfirm.body);
    expect(safeAttempt).not.toContain(accountId);
    expect(safeAttempt).not.toContain(callbackCode);
    expect(safeAttempt).not.toContain(state);
    expect(safeAttempt).not.toContain('synthetic-long-lived-token');
    expect(safeAttempt).not.toContain('synthetic-short-token');
    for (const field of ['authorizationReference', 'professionalUserId', 'externalResourceId', 'candidateKeyId', 'candidateNonce',
      'candidateCiphertext', 'candidateTag', 'accessToken', 'token', 'credential', 'sessionHash', 'state', 'code',
      'operationLeaseToken', 'callbackLeaseToken']) expect(preconfirm.body).not.toHaveProperty(field);
    const confirmBody = operation === 'CONNECT' ? {} : { expectedGeneration };
    const confirmed = await bff('post', `/api/bff/instagram-authorizations/${reserved.body.id}/confirm`, session)
      .set('idempotency-key', randomUUID()).send(confirmBody).expect(200);
    const attempt = await prisma.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: reserved.body.id } });
    const channel = attempt.channelId
      ? await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: attempt.channelId }, include: { credential: true, businessIdentity: true } })
      : null;
    return { reserved: reserved.body, confirmed: confirmed.body, attempt, channel };
  };

  const ingress = async (accountId: string, peerId: string, mid = `synthetic-cp4-${randomUUID()}`, text = 'synthetic accepted Instagram message') => {
    const body = JSON.stringify({ object: 'instagram', entry: [{ id: accountId, messaging: [{
      sender: { id: peerId }, recipient: { id: accountId }, timestamp: Date.now(), message: { mid, text },
    }] }] });
    const signature = `sha256=${createHmac('sha256', INSTAGRAM_WEBHOOK_SECRET).update(body).digest('hex')}`;
    return request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/webhooks/instagram').set('content-type', 'application/json')
      .set('x-hub-signature-256', signature).send(body).expect(200);
  };

  const countFacts = async (buId: string) => ({
    channels: await prisma.buChannel.count({ where: { buId } }),
    attempts: await prisma.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.count({ where: { buId } }),
    credentials: await prisma.channelCredential.count({ where: { buId } }),
    businessIdentities: await prisma.businessChannelIdentity.count({ where: { buId } }),
    providerIdentities: await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.count({ where: { buId } }),
    contacts: await prisma.contact.count({ where: { buId } }),
    conversations: await prisma.conversation.count({ where: { buId } }),
    routes: await prisma.conversationRoute.count({ where: { buId } }),
    messages: await prisma.message.count({ where: { buId } }),
    audits: await prisma.activityLog.count({ where: { buId } }),
  });

  it('first exposes the approved local cursor catalog contract; the missing route is a causal RED', async () => {
    const buId = tenant.businessUnit.id;
    const adminIds: string[] = [];
    const admins = await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: 3 }, async (_, index) => {
      const email = `instagram-cp4-page-admin-${index}-${randomUUID()}@example.test`;
      adminIds[index] = await criarUsuario(prisma, tenant.mantenedora.id, buId, email, 'ADMIN');
      return login(app, email);
    }));
    const accounts: Array<{ accountId: string; username: string; actor: number; channelId: string }> = [];
    for (let index = 0; index < 21; index += 1) {
      const actor = Math.floor(index / 7);
      const accountId = `synthetic-cp4-catalog-account-${index}-${randomUUID()}`;
      const username = `catalog_fixture_${index}`;
      const created = await authorize(admins[actor]!, buId, accountId, username);
      expect(created.channel?.type).toBe('INSTAGRAM');
      accounts.push({ accountId, username, actor, channelId: created.channel!.id });
    }
    expect(await prisma.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.count({ where: { buId } })).toBe(21);
    for (let index = 0; index < 3; index += 1) {
      expect(await prisma.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.count({ where: { buId, userId: adminIds[index]! } })).toBe(7);
    }

    // Equal creation instants exercise the UUID tie-breaker on the real keyset.
    const tiedAt = new Date('2026-10-23T12:00:00.000Z');
    await prisma.buChannel.updateMany({ where: { id: { in: accounts.map(({ channelId }) => channelId) } }, data: { createdAt: tiedAt } });
    const beforeFacts = await countFacts(buId);
    const providerCallsBefore = instagramFake.calls.length;
    const queryStart = queryEvents.length;
    // This approved 200 assertion intentionally observes today's missing-route 404.
    const first = await bff('get', `/api/bff/business-units/${buId}/channel-connections`).expect(200);
    assertBoundedReadOnly(queryEvents.slice(queryStart));
    expect(first.body.connections).toHaveLength(20);
    const pageConnections = first.body.connections as Array<{ channelId: string; type: string; generation: number; status: string;
      display: { platform: string; accountLabel: string; whatsappPhone: string | null; instagramUsername: string | null };
      identityVerified: boolean; providerConnectionState: string; receiptVerified: boolean }>;
    expect(pageConnections.every((connection) =>
      typeof connection.channelId === 'string' && typeof connection.type === 'string' && typeof connection.generation === 'number'
      && typeof connection.status === 'string' && typeof connection.display.platform === 'string'
      && typeof connection.display.accountLabel === 'string' && typeof connection.display.instagramUsername === 'string'
      && connection.display.whatsappPhone === null
      && typeof connection.identityVerified === 'boolean' && typeof connection.providerConnectionState === 'string'
      && typeof connection.receiptVerified === 'boolean')).toBe(true);
    expect(pageConnections.every((connection) => connection.display.instagramUsername
      === accounts.find((account) => account.channelId === connection.channelId)?.username)).toBe(true);
    const returnedIds = first.body.connections.map((connection: { channelId: string }) => connection.channelId);
    expect(new Set(returnedIds).size).toBe(returnedIds.length);
    expect(returnedIds.every((id: string) => accounts.some((account) => account.channelId === id))).toBe(true);
    expect(first.body.nextCursor).toEqual(expect.any(String));
    const serialized = JSON.stringify(first.body);
    expect(serialized).not.toMatch(/synthetic-(?:long-lived|short)-token|ciphertext|candidateNonce|accessToken|externalResourceId/i);
    expect(accounts.every(({ accountId }) => !serialized.includes(accountId))).toBe(true);
    const repeatStart = queryEvents.length;
    const repeat = await bff('get', `/api/bff/business-units/${buId}/channel-connections`).expect(200);
    assertBoundedReadOnly(queryEvents.slice(repeatStart));
    expect(repeat.body.connections.map((connection: { channelId: string }) => connection.channelId)).toEqual(returnedIds);
    const continuationStart = queryEvents.length;
    const continuation = await bff('get', `/api/bff/business-units/${buId}/channel-connections`)
      .query({ cursor: first.body.nextCursor }).expect(200);
    assertBoundedReadOnly(queryEvents.slice(continuationStart));
    expect(continuation.body.connections).toHaveLength(1);
    const allIds = [...returnedIds, ...continuation.body.connections.map((connection: { channelId: string }) => connection.channelId)];
    expect(new Set(allIds).size).toBe(21);
    expect(new Set(allIds)).toEqual(new Set(accounts.map(({ channelId }) => channelId)));
    expect(continuation.body.nextCursor).toBeNull();
    const oneStart = queryEvents.length;
    expect((await bff('get', `/api/bff/business-units/${buId}/channel-connections`).query({ limit: 1 }).expect(200)).body.connections).toHaveLength(1);
    assertBoundedReadOnly(queryEvents.slice(oneStart));
    const fiftyStart = queryEvents.length;
    expect((await bff('get', `/api/bff/business-units/${buId}/channel-connections`).query({ limit: 50 }).expect(200)).body.connections).toHaveLength(21);
    assertBoundedReadOnly(queryEvents.slice(fiftyStart));
    expect((await bff('get', `/api/bff/business-units/${buId}/channel-connections`).query({ cursor: 'malformed-cursor' })).status).toBe(400);

    const secondBuResponse = await bff('post', '/api/bff/business-units').send({ nome: 'CP4 cursor scope BU' }).expect(201);
    const secondBuId = String(secondBuResponse.body.id);
    await prisma.userBuRole.create({ data: { userId: adminIds[0]!, buId: secondBuId, role: 'ADMIN', grantedViaTipo: 'DIRETO' } });
    const scopedCursor = await bff('get', `/api/bff/business-units/${secondBuId}/channel-connections`, admins[0]!)
      .query({ cursor: first.body.nextCursor });
    expect(scopedCursor.status).toBe(400);
    const lowRoleEmail = `instagram-cp4-low-role-${randomUUID()}@example.test`;
    await criarUsuario(prisma, tenant.mantenedora.id, buId, lowRoleEmail, 'ATENDENTE');
    const lowRoleCookie = await login(app, lowRoleEmail);
    await bff('get', `/api/bff/business-units/${buId}/channel-connections`, lowRoleCookie).expect(200);
    expect((await bff('get', `/api/bff/business-units/${buId}/channel-connections`, lowRoleCookie)
      .query({ cursor: 'malformed-cursor' })).status).toBe(400);
    const foreign = await criarMantenedora(app, 'Instagram CP4 foreign BU', `instagram-cp4-foreign-${randomUUID()}@example.test`);
    const foreignCookie = await login(app, foreign.owner.email);
    expect((await bff('get', `/api/bff/business-units/${buId}/channel-connections`, foreignCookie).query({ cursor: 'malformed-cursor' })).status).toBe(404);
    const visibleAttempt = await prisma.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { buId, userId: adminIds[0]! } });
    expect((await bff('get', `/api/bff/instagram-authorizations/${visibleAttempt.id}`, admins[1]!)).status).toBe(404);
    expect((await bff('get', `/api/bff/instagram-authorizations/${visibleAttempt.id}`, foreignCookie)).status).toBe(404);
    expect((await request(app.getHttpServer()).get(`/api/bff/instagram-authorizations/${visibleAttempt.id}`)).status).toBe(401);
    expect((await request(app.getHttpServer()).get(`/api/bff/business-units/${buId}/channel-connections`).query({ cursor: 'malformed-cursor' })).status).toBe(401);
    expect(await countFacts(buId)).toEqual(beforeFacts);
    expect(instagramFake.calls).toHaveLength(providerCallsBefore);
  });

  it('routes exact WhatsApp UUID operations to the selected adapter and preserves the sibling binding', async () => {
    const buId = tenant.businessUnit.id;
    const phone = '5584998880000';
    metaFake.wabaRecursos = [{ wabaId: 'synthetic-cp4-waba', phoneNumberId: 'synthetic-cp4-phone-id', telefone: phone }];
    const official = await criarCanalVerificado(prisma, buId, 'OFICIAL', 'synthetic-cp4-phone-id', {
      provedBusinessPhone: phone, provedWabaId: 'synthetic-cp4-waba',
    });
    const evolution = await criarCanalVerificado(prisma, buId, 'NAO_OFICIAL', `synthetic-cp4-evolution-${randomUUID()}`, { provedBusinessPhone: phone });
    evolutionFake.ownerPhone = phone;
    evolutionFake.inventory.set(evolution.channel.evolutionInstanceName!, {
      name: evolution.channel.evolutionInstanceName!, ownerJid: `${phone}@s.whatsapp.net`, connectionStatus: 'open',
    });
    const beforeMeta = metaFake.confirmCalls;
    const beforeEvolution = evolutionFake.providerCalls;
    const stale = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${buId}/channel-connections/${official.channel.id}/verify`)
      .send({ expectedGeneration: official.channel.generation + 1 });
    expect(stale.status).toBe(409);
    expect(metaFake.confirmCalls).toBe(beforeMeta);
    expect(evolutionFake.providerCalls).toBe(beforeEvolution);

    let releaseProvider!: () => void;
    let enterProvider!: () => void;
    const entered = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { enterProvider = resolve; });
    const held = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { releaseProvider = resolve; });
    metaFake.confirmHook = async () => { enterProvider(); await held; };
    const verifying = bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${buId}/channel-connections/${official.channel.id}/verify`)
      .send({ expectedGeneration: official.channel.generation }).then((response) => response);
    try {
      await waitFor(entered, 'exact official UUID verification provider call');
      const competingDisconnect = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${buId}/channel-connections/${official.channel.id}/disconnect`)
        .send({ expectedGeneration: official.channel.generation });
      expect(competingDisconnect.status).toBe(409);
      releaseProvider();
      const verified = await verifying;
      expect(verified.status).toBe(200);
    } finally {
      releaseProvider();
      metaFake.confirmHook = null;
      await Promise.allSettled([verifying]);
    }
    const beforeEvolutionVerify = evolutionFake.providerCalls;
    const evolutionVerified = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${buId}/channel-connections/${evolution.channel.id}/verify`)
      .send({ expectedGeneration: evolution.channel.generation }).expect(200);
    expect(evolutionFake.providerCalls).toBeGreaterThan(beforeEvolutionVerify);
    expect(evolutionVerified.body.channelId).toBe(evolution.channel.id);
    const siblingBeforeRemoval = await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: evolution.channel.id } });
    const officialProvider = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { provider_externalResourceId: { provider: 'OFICIAL', externalResourceId: 'synthetic-cp4-phone-id' } } });
    const removed = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${buId}/channel-connections/${official.channel.id}/disconnect`)
      .send({ expectedGeneration: official.channel.generation }).expect(200);
    expect(removed.body).toMatchObject({ channelId: official.channel.id, connection: null, remoteCleanup: 'PENDING' });
    expect(await prisma.buChannel.findUnique({ where: { id: official.channel.id } })).toBeNull();
    expect(await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUnique({ where: { id: officialProvider.id } }))
      .toMatchObject({ currentChannelId: null, businessIdentityId: officialProvider.businessIdentityId });
    expect(await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: evolution.channel.id } }))
      .toMatchObject({ id: siblingBeforeRemoval.id, businessIdentityId: siblingBeforeRemoval.businessIdentityId, generation: siblingBeforeRemoval.generation });

    const foreign = await criarMantenedora(app, 'Instagram CP4 foreign WA', `instagram-cp4-foreign-wa-${randomUUID()}@example.test`);
    const beforeForeign = metaFake.confirmCalls + evolutionFake.providerCalls;
    const foreignResult = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${foreign.businessUnit.id}/channel-connections/${evolution.channel.id}/verify`)
      .send({ expectedGeneration: evolution.channel.generation });
    expect(foreignResult.status).toBe(404);
    expect(metaFake.confirmCalls + evolutionFake.providerCalls).toBe(beforeForeign);
  });

  it('gates Evolution verification by the tenant module on both legacy and selected UUID routes', async () => {
    const buId = tenant.businessUnit.id;
    const phone = '5584998877665';
    await prisma.mantenedoraSettings.update({ where: { mantenedoraId: tenant.mantenedora.id }, data: { modulos: { canalNaoOficial: true } } });
    const evolution = await criarCanalVerificado(prisma, buId, 'NAO_OFICIAL', `synthetic-cp4-module-${randomUUID()}`, { provedBusinessPhone: phone });
    evolutionFake.ownerPhone = phone;
    evolutionFake.inventory.set(evolution.channel.evolutionInstanceName!, {
      name: evolution.channel.evolutionInstanceName!, ownerJid: `${phone}@s.whatsapp.net`, connectionStatus: 'open',
    });
    const identityBefore = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { currentChannelId: evolution.channel.id } });
    const channelBefore = await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: evolution.channel.id } });
    await prisma.mantenedoraSettings.update({ where: { mantenedoraId: tenant.mantenedora.id }, data: { modulos: { canalNaoOficial: false } } });
    const callsWhileDisabled = evolutionFake.providerCalls;
    for (const route of [
      { path: `/api/bff/business-units/${buId}/channels/NAO_OFICIAL/verify`, body: {} },
      { path: `/api/bff/business-units/${buId}/channel-connections/${evolution.channel.id}/verify`, body: { expectedGeneration: evolution.channel.generation } },
    ]) {
      const response = await bff('post', route.path).send(route.body);
      expect(response.status).toBe(404);
      expect(response.body).toMatchObject({ code: 'MODULO_DESLIGADO' });
    }
    expect(evolutionFake.providerCalls).toBe(callsWhileDisabled);
    expect(await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: evolution.channel.id } }))
      .toMatchObject({ generation: channelBefore.generation, ownNumberVerified: channelBefore.ownNumberVerified,
        verifiedEpoch: channelBefore.verifiedEpoch, providerConnectionState: channelBefore.providerConnectionState });
    expect(await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: identityBefore.id } }))
      .toMatchObject({ currentChannelId: identityBefore.currentChannelId, businessIdentityId: identityBefore.businessIdentityId,
        externalResourceId: identityBefore.externalResourceId });

    await prisma.mantenedoraSettings.update({ where: { mantenedoraId: tenant.mantenedora.id }, data: { modulos: { canalNaoOficial: true } } });
    for (const route of [
      { path: `/api/bff/business-units/${buId}/channels/NAO_OFICIAL/verify`, body: {} },
      { path: `/api/bff/business-units/${buId}/channel-connections/${evolution.channel.id}/verify`, body: { expectedGeneration: evolution.channel.generation } },
    ]) {
      const before = evolutionFake.providerCalls;
      const response = await bff('post', route.path).send(route.body);
      expect(response.status).toBe(200);
      expect(evolutionFake.providerCalls).toBeGreaterThan(before);
    }
  });

  it('rejects a captured selected UUID after legacy disconnect and same-type generation-one replacement', async () => {
    const buId = tenant.businessUnit.id;
    const phone = '5584998877000';
    const originalPhoneNumberId = `synthetic-cp4-race-original-${randomUUID()}`;
    const replacementPhoneNumberId = `synthetic-cp4-race-replacement-${randomUUID()}`;
    const wabaId = `synthetic-cp4-race-waba-${randomUUID()}`;
    metaFake.wabaRecursos = [{ wabaId, phoneNumberId: replacementPhoneNumberId, telefone: phone }];
    const original = await criarCanalVerificado(prisma, buId, 'OFICIAL', originalPhoneNumberId, {
      provedBusinessPhone: phone, provedWabaId: wabaId,
    });
    expect(original.channel.generation).toBe(1);
    expect(await prisma.channelCredential.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { channelId: original.channel.id } }))
      .toMatchObject({ generation: original.channel.generation });

    const service = app.get(ChannelsService);
    const realVerificar = service.verificar.bind(service);
    let release!: () => void;
    let enter!: () => void;
    const held = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { release = resolve; });
    const entered = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { enter = resolve; });
    const spy = vi.spyOn(service, 'verificar').mockImplementation(async (...args: Parameters<typeof service.verificar>) => {
      enter();
      await held;
      return realVerificar(...args);
    });
    const verifyCallsBefore = metaFake.confirmCalls;
    const verification = bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${buId}/channel-connections/${original.channel.id}/verify`)
      .send({ expectedGeneration: 1 }).then((response) => response);
    try {
      const boundary = await Promise.race([
        entered.then(() => ({ kind: 'entered' as const })),
        verification.then((response) => ({ kind: 'response' as const, response })),
      ]);
      expect(boundary.kind, boundary.kind === 'response' ? `Native selected-UUID verify returned ${boundary.response.status} before the service boundary.` : undefined)
        .toBe('entered');

      await request(app.getHttpServer()).delete(`/api/bff/business-units/${buId}/channels/oficial`)
        .set('cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').expect(204);
      const replacement = await criarCanalVerificado(prisma, buId, 'OFICIAL', replacementPhoneNumberId, {
        provedBusinessPhone: phone, provedWabaId: wabaId,
      });
      expect(replacement.channel.generation).toBe(1);
      expect(replacement.channel.type).toBe(original.channel.type);
      expect(replacement.channel.businessIdentityId).toBe(original.channel.businessIdentityId);
      expect(replacement.channel.phoneNumberId).toBe(replacementPhoneNumberId);
      expect(replacement.channel.phoneNumberId).not.toBe(original.channel.phoneNumberId);
      expect(await prisma.channelCredential.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { channelId: replacement.channel.id } }))
        .toMatchObject({ generation: replacement.channel.generation });
      expect(await prisma.businessChannelIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: replacement.channel.businessIdentityId! } }))
        .toMatchObject({ buId, externalResourceId: phone });
      const replacementBeforeResume = await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: replacement.channel.id },
        include: { credential: true, businessIdentity: true } });
      const replacementIdentityBeforeResume = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: replacement.identity.id } });
      const providerCallsBeforeResume = metaFake.providerCalls;
      release();
      const stale = await verification;
      expect(stale.status).toBe(409);
      expect(metaFake.providerCalls).toBe(providerCallsBeforeResume);
      expect(metaFake.confirmCalls).toBe(verifyCallsBefore);
      expect(await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: replacement.channel.id },
        include: { credential: true, businessIdentity: true } }))
        .toEqual(replacementBeforeResume);
      expect(await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: replacement.identity.id } }))
        .toEqual(replacementIdentityBeforeResume);
    } finally {
      release();
      spy.mockRestore();
      await Promise.allSettled([verification]);
    }
  });

  it('makes a legacy type-only WhatsApp operation conflict when more than two compatible rows exist', async () => {
    const buId = tenant.businessUnit.id;
    const phone = '5584998880000';
    metaFake.wabaRecursos = [
      { wabaId: 'synthetic-cp4-waba', phoneNumberId: 'synthetic-cp4-phone-a', telefone: phone },
      { wabaId: 'synthetic-cp4-waba', phoneNumberId: 'synthetic-cp4-phone-b', telefone: phone },
      { wabaId: 'synthetic-cp4-waba', phoneNumberId: 'synthetic-cp4-phone-c', telefone: phone },
    ];
    await criarCanalVerificado(prisma, buId, 'OFICIAL', 'synthetic-cp4-phone-a', {
      provedBusinessPhone: phone, provedWabaId: 'synthetic-cp4-waba',
    });
    await prisma.buChannel.createMany({ data: [
      { buId, type: 'OFICIAL' as const, phoneNumberId: 'synthetic-cp4-legacy-official', businessIdentityId: null },
      { buId, type: 'NAO_OFICIAL' as const, evolutionInstanceName: 'synthetic-cp4-legacy-evolution', businessIdentityId: null },
    ] });
    const before = metaFake.confirmCalls;
    const response = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${buId}/channels/OFICIAL/verify`).send({});
    expect(response.status).toBe(409);
    expect(metaFake.confirmCalls).toBe(before);
  });

  it('verifies a durable binding after OAuth expiry, preserves known receipt, and fences UNKNOWN without POST', async () => {
    const buId = tenant.businessUnit.id;
    const accountId = `synthetic-cp4-verify-${randomUUID()}`;
    const connected = await authorize(cookie, buId, accountId, 'verify_fixture');
    const channel = connected.channel!;
    await ingress(accountId, `synthetic-cp4-peer-${randomUUID()}`);
    const connectedResource = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { currentChannelId: channel.id } });
    await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.update({ where: { id: connectedResource.id },
      data: { instagramLastSubscriptionAttemptAt: new Date(Date.now() - 11_000) } });
    const acceptedReceipt = await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: channel.id }, select: { receiptVerifiedAt: true, generation: true } });
    expect(acceptedReceipt.receiptVerifiedAt).not.toBeNull();
    await prisma.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.update({ where: { id: connected.attempt.id }, data: { expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() - 60_000) } });
    const callsBefore = instagramFake.calls.length;
    const knownGetCount = instagramFake.calls.filter((call) => call.method === 'GET' && call.url.endsWith('/subscribed_apps')).length;
    const knownPostCount = instagramFake.calls.filter((call) => call.method === 'POST' && call.url.endsWith('/subscribed_apps')).length;
    const verified = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${buId}/channel-connections/${channel.id}/verify`)
      .send({ expectedGeneration: channel.generation });
    expect(verified.status).toBe(503);
    expect(verified.body).toMatchObject({ code: 'SUBSCRIPTION_OBSERVATION_UNCONFIRMED', observation: 'UNKNOWN' });
    expect(instagramFake.calls.filter((call) => call.method === 'GET' && call.url.endsWith('/subscribed_apps'))).toHaveLength(knownGetCount + 1);
    expect(instagramFake.calls.filter((call) => call.method === 'POST' && call.url.endsWith('/subscribed_apps'))).toHaveLength(knownPostCount);
    expect((await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: channel.id } })).receiptVerifiedAt).toEqual(acceptedReceipt.receiptVerifiedAt);
    expect(instagramFake.calls.length).toBeGreaterThan(callsBefore);

    const unresolved = await authorize(cookie, buId, `synthetic-cp4-verify-unknown-${randomUUID()}`, 'verify_unknown');
    const unresolvedChannel = unresolved.channel!;
    const unresolvedResource = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { currentChannelId: unresolvedChannel.id } });
    await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.update({ where: { id: unresolvedResource.id },
      data: { instagramLastSubscriptionAttemptAt: new Date(Date.now() - 11_000) } });
    await prisma.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.update({ where: { id: unresolved.attempt.id }, data: { expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() - 60_000) } });
    const beforeUnknown = instagramFake.calls.length;
    const postCount = instagramFake.calls.filter((call) => call.method === 'POST' && call.url.endsWith('/subscribed_apps')).length;
    const unknown = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${buId}/channel-connections/${unresolvedChannel.id}/verify`)
      .send({ expectedGeneration: unresolvedChannel.generation });
    expect(unknown.status).toBe(503);
    expect(unknown.body).toMatchObject({ code: 'SUBSCRIPTION_OBSERVATION_UNCONFIRMED', observation: 'UNKNOWN' });
    expect(unknown.body.connection).toMatchObject({ channelId: unresolvedChannel.id, generation: unresolvedChannel.generation });
    const calls = instagramFake.calls.slice(beforeUnknown);
    expect(calls.filter((call) => call.method === 'GET' && call.url.endsWith('/subscribed_apps'))).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(instagramFake.calls.filter((call) => call.method === 'POST' && call.url.endsWith('/subscribed_apps'))).toHaveLength(postCount);
    expect((await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: unresolvedChannel.id } })).receiptVerifiedAt).toBeNull();
    const cooled = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${buId}/channel-connections/${unresolvedChannel.id}/verify`)
      .send({ expectedGeneration: unresolvedChannel.generation });
    expect(cooled.status).toBe(429);
    expect(Number(cooled.headers['retry-after'])).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    const staleCalls = instagramFake.calls.length;
    const staleGeneration = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${buId}/channel-connections/${unresolvedChannel.id}/verify`)
      .send({ expectedGeneration: unresolvedChannel.generation + 1 });
    expect(staleGeneration.status).toBe(409);
    expect(instagramFake.calls).toHaveLength(staleCalls);

    const beforeAuthFailures = instagramFake.calls.length;
    const invalidSession = await request(app.getHttpServer())
      .post(`/api/bff/business-units/${buId}/channel-connections/${unresolvedChannel.id}/verify`)
      .set('cookie', `${SESSION_COOKIE}=synthetic-invalid-session`).set('x-requested-with', 'teste')
      .send({ expectedGeneration: unresolvedChannel.generation });
    expect(invalidSession.status).toBe(401);
    await prisma.userBuRole.updateMany({ where: { userId: tenant.owner.id, buId }, data: { role: 'ATENDENTE' } });
    const missingGrant = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${buId}/channel-connections/${unresolvedChannel.id}/verify`)
      .send({ expectedGeneration: unresolvedChannel.generation });
    expect(missingGrant.status).toBe(403);
    await prisma.userBuRole.deleteMany({ where: { userId: tenant.owner.id, buId } });
    const noGrant = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${buId}/channel-connections/${unresolvedChannel.id}/verify`)
      .send({ expectedGeneration: unresolvedChannel.generation });
    expect(noGrant.status).toBe(404);
    expect(instagramFake.calls).toHaveLength(beforeAuthFailures);
  });

  it('cleans a verification lease after post-COMMIT session expiry, preserves a successor lease, and reaches GET for a live control', async () => {
    const buId = tenant.businessUnit.id;
    const fixtures: Array<{ channelId: string; generation: number; identityId: string; attemptId: string }> = [];
    for (const label of ['verify-expired-cleanup', 'verify-expired-successor', 'verify-live-control']) {
      const accountId = `synthetic-cp4-${label}-${randomUUID()}`;
      const connected = await authorize(cookie, buId, accountId, label.replaceAll('-', '_'));
      const channel = connected.channel!;
      const owner = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { currentChannelId: channel.id } });
      fixtures.push({ channelId: channel.id, generation: channel.generation, identityId: owner.id, attemptId: connected.attempt.id });
    }
    const owners = await Promise.all(fixtures.map(({ identityId }) => prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: identityId } })));
    const cooldownUntil = Math.max(...owners.map((owner) => (owner.instagramLastSubscriptionAttemptAt?.getTime() ?? 0) + 10_000));
    await pause(Math.max(0, cooldownUntil - Date.now() + 50));

    const runExpired = async (fixture: typeof fixtures[number], preserveSuccessor: boolean) => {
      const ownerBefore = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: fixture.identityId } });
      const attemptBefore = await prisma.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: fixture.attemptId } });
      const historyBefore = { postDispatchStartedAt: ownerBefore.instagramSubscriptionPostDispatchStartedAt,
        attemptStatus: attemptBefore.status, subscriptionState: attemptBefore.subscriptionState,
        subscriptionRequests: attemptBefore.subscriptionRequests };
      const token = sessions.assinar(tenant.owner.id, Date.now() - 24 * 60 * 60_000 + 3_000);
      const claims = sessions.verificar(token);
      if (!claims) throw new Error('Expected a real signed session before its natural expiration');
      const barrier = await resourceCommitBarrier(fixture.identityId);
      const callsBefore = instagramFake.calls.length;
      const responsePromise = bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${buId}/channel-connections/${fixture.channelId}/verify`, `${SESSION_COOKIE}=${token}`)
        .send({ expectedGeneration: fixture.generation }).then((response) => response);
      let successor = '';
      let successorUpdate: Promise<{ count: number } | { error: unknown }> | undefined;
      try {
        expect(await waitForResourceWaiter(barrier.holderPid, 'COMMIT')).toBe(true);
        if (preserveSuccessor) {
          successor = `successor-${randomUUID()}`;
          successorUpdate = prisma.$executeRaw`UPDATE provider_resource_identities
            SET instagram_operation_lease_token = ${successor},
              instagram_operation_lease_until = clock_timestamp() + interval '60 seconds'
            WHERE id = ${fixture.identityId} AND instagram_operation_lease_token IS NOT NULL`
            .then((count) => ({ count }), (error: unknown) => ({ error }));
          expect(await waitForResourceWaiter(barrier.holderPid, 'UPDATE')).toBe(true);
        }
        await pause(Math.max(0, claims.exp - Date.now() + 50));
        expect(sessions.verificar(token)).toBeNull();
        barrier.release();
        const response = await waitFor(responsePromise, 'expired-session verification response');
        expect(response.status).toBe(401);
        if (successorUpdate) {
          const outcome = await waitFor(successorUpdate, 'queued successor lease update');
          if ('error' in outcome) throw outcome.error;
          expect(outcome.count).toBe(1);
        }
        expect(instagramFake.calls).toHaveLength(callsBefore);
        const ownerAfter = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: fixture.identityId } });
        const attemptAfter = await prisma.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: fixture.attemptId } });
        expect(ownerAfter.instagramOperationLeaseToken).toBe(preserveSuccessor ? successor : null);
        expect(ownerAfter.instagramSubscriptionPostDispatchStartedAt).toEqual(historyBefore.postDispatchStartedAt);
        expect(attemptAfter).toMatchObject({ status: historyBefore.attemptStatus,
          subscriptionState: historyBefore.subscriptionState, subscriptionRequests: historyBefore.subscriptionRequests });
      } finally {
        barrier.release();
        await Promise.allSettled([responsePromise, ...(successorUpdate ? [successorUpdate] : [])]);
        await barrier.cleanup();
        if (successor) await prisma.$executeRaw`UPDATE provider_resource_identities SET instagram_operation_lease_token = NULL,
          instagram_operation_lease_until = NULL WHERE id = ${fixture.identityId} AND instagram_operation_lease_token = ${successor}`;
      }
    };

    await runExpired(fixtures[0]!, false);
    await runExpired(fixtures[1]!, true);

    const live = fixtures[2]!;
    const ownerBeforeLive = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: live.identityId } });
    const attemptBeforeLive = await prisma.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: live.attemptId } });
    const liveHistory = { postDispatchStartedAt: ownerBeforeLive.instagramSubscriptionPostDispatchStartedAt,
      status: attemptBeforeLive.status, subscriptionState: attemptBeforeLive.subscriptionState,
      subscriptionRequests: attemptBeforeLive.subscriptionRequests };
    const liveBarrier = await resourceCommitBarrier(live.identityId);
    const liveCallsBefore = instagramFake.calls.length;
    const liveResponsePromise = bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${buId}/channel-connections/${live.channelId}/verify`)
      .send({ expectedGeneration: live.generation }).then((response) => response);
    try {
      expect(await waitForResourceWaiter(liveBarrier.holderPid, 'COMMIT')).toBe(true);
      await pause(3_050);
      expect(sessions.verificar(sessions.extrairDoCabecalho(cookie))).not.toBeNull();
      liveBarrier.release();
      const response = await waitFor(liveResponsePromise, 'live-session verification response');
      expect(response.status).toBe(503);
      expect(response.body).toMatchObject({ code: 'SUBSCRIPTION_OBSERVATION_UNCONFIRMED', observation: 'UNKNOWN' });
      const calls = instagramFake.calls.slice(liveCallsBefore);
      expect(calls.filter((call) => call.method === 'GET' && call.url.endsWith('/subscribed_apps'))).toHaveLength(1);
      expect(calls.filter((call) => call.method === 'POST' && call.url.endsWith('/subscribed_apps'))).toHaveLength(0);
      const ownerAfter = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: live.identityId } });
      const attemptAfter = await prisma.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: live.attemptId } });
      expect(ownerAfter.instagramOperationLeaseToken).toBeNull();
      expect(ownerAfter.instagramSubscriptionPostDispatchStartedAt).toEqual(liveHistory.postDispatchStartedAt);
      expect(attemptAfter).toMatchObject({ status: liveHistory.status,
        subscriptionState: liveHistory.subscriptionState, subscriptionRequests: liveHistory.subscriptionRequests });
    } finally {
      liveBarrier.release();
      await Promise.allSettled([liveResponsePromise]);
      await liveBarrier.cleanup();
    }
  }, 45_000);

  it('commits local disconnect while a real reauthorization callback is held and preserves completed history', async () => {
    const buId = tenant.businessUnit.id;
    const accountId = `synthetic-cp4-disconnect-${randomUUID()}`;
    const peerId = `synthetic-cp4-disconnect-peer-${randomUUID()}`;
    const initial = await authorize(cookie, buId, accountId, 'disconnect_fixture');
    await ingress(accountId, peerId, `synthetic-cp4-history-${randomUUID()}`);
    const channel = await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: initial.channel!.id }, include: { credential: true } });
    const provider = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { currentChannelId: channel.id } });
    const business = await prisma.businessChannelIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: channel.businessIdentityId! } });
    const route = await prisma.conversationRoute.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { buId, businessIdentityId: business.id } });
    const completedBefore = await prisma.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: initial.attempt.id } });
    const completedFacts = {
      id: completedBefore.id, status: completedBefore.status, channelId: completedBefore.channelId,
      professionalUserId: completedBefore.professionalUserId, mappedCredentialVersion: completedBefore.mappedCredentialVersion,
      confirmationState: completedBefore.confirmationState, confirmationIdempotencyKey: completedBefore.confirmationIdempotencyKey,
      confirmationRequestHash: completedBefore.confirmationRequestHash,
    };
    const historyCount = await prisma.message.count({ where: { buId, channelOrigin: 'INSTAGRAM' } });
    const pending = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${buId}/instagram-authorizations`)
      .set('idempotency-key', randomUUID()).send({ operation: 'REAUTHORIZE', channelId: channel.id, expectedGeneration: channel.generation }).expect(201);

    let release!: () => void;
    let enter!: () => void;
    const held = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { release = resolve; });
    const entered = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { enter = resolve; });
    instagramFake.meEnvelope = { user_id: accountId, username: 'disconnect_fixture_reauthorized' };
    instagramFake.afterRequestHook = async (method, url) => {
      if (method === 'GET' && url.pathname === '/access_token') { enter(); await held; }
    };
    const requestsBeforeCallback = instagramFake.calls.length;
    const callback = bff('get', '/api/bff/instagram-authorizations/callback')
      .query({ state: new URL(pending.body.authorizationUrl).searchParams.get('state'), code: `synthetic-cp4-held-${randomUUID()}` })
      .then((response) => response);
    try {
      await waitFor(entered, 'reauthorization token exchange HTTP barrier');
      const disconnect = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${buId}/channel-connections/${channel.id}/disconnect`)
        .send({ expectedGeneration: channel.generation }).expect(200);
      expect(disconnect.body).toMatchObject({ channelId: channel.id, remoteCleanup: 'PENDING' });
      expect(disconnect.body.connection).toMatchObject({ channelId: channel.id, generation: channel.generation + 1 });
      const committed = await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: channel.id }, include: { credential: true } });
      expect(committed).toMatchObject({ generation: channel.generation + 1, receiptVerifiedAt: null, credential: null });
      expect(await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: provider.id } }))
        .toMatchObject({ currentChannelId: channel.id, businessIdentityId: business.id, retiredAt: null });
      expect(await prisma.businessChannelIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: business.id } })).toEqual(business);
      expect(await prisma.conversationRoute.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: route.id } })).toEqual(route);
      expect(await prisma.message.count({ where: { buId, channelOrigin: 'INSTAGRAM' } })).toBe(historyCount);
      release();
      const callbackResponse = await callback;
      expect(callbackResponse.status).toBe(303);
      expect(callbackResponse.headers['cache-control']).toContain('no-store');
      expect(callbackResponse.headers['referrer-policy']).toBe('no-referrer');
      expect(callbackResponse.headers.location).not.toContain('synthetic-cp4-held-');
    } finally {
      release();
      instagramFake.afterRequestHook = null;
      await Promise.allSettled([callback]);
    }
    const after = await prisma.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: pending.body.id } });
    expect(after.status).toBe('CANCELLED');
    expect(after).toMatchObject({ candidateCiphertext: null, candidateNonce: null, candidateTag: null, mappedCredentialVersion: null });
    expect(await prisma.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: initial.attempt.id } }))
      .toMatchObject(completedFacts);
    expect(await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: channel.id } }))
      .toMatchObject({ id: channel.id, generation: channel.generation + 1, receiptVerifiedAt: null });
    expect(await prisma.channelCredential.count({ where: { channelId: channel.id } })).toBe(0);
    const callbackHttp = instagramFake.calls.slice(requestsBeforeCallback);
    expect(callbackHttp.map((call) => call.url)).toEqual([
      'https://api.instagram.com/oauth/access_token', 'https://graph.instagram.com/access_token',
    ]);
  });

  it('does not let an already-dispatched subscription response restore a channel disconnected in flight', async () => {
    const buId = tenant.businessUnit.id;
    const accountId = `synthetic-cp4-confirm-race-${randomUUID()}`;
    instagramFake.meEnvelope = { user_id: accountId, username: 'confirm_race' };
    const reserved = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${buId}/instagram-authorizations`)
      .set('idempotency-key', randomUUID()).send({ operation: 'CONNECT' }).expect(201);
    await bff('get', '/api/bff/instagram-authorizations/callback')
      .query({ state: new URL(reserved.body.authorizationUrl).searchParams.get('state'), code: `synthetic-cp4-confirm-${randomUUID()}` }).expect(303);
    let release!: () => void;
    let enter!: () => void;
    const held = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { release = resolve; });
    const entered = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { enter = resolve; });
    instagramFake.afterRequestHook = async (method, url) => {
      if (method === 'POST' && url.pathname.endsWith('/subscribed_apps')) { enter(); await held; }
    };
    const postsBefore = instagramFake.calls.filter((call) => call.method === 'POST' && call.url.endsWith('/subscribed_apps')).length;
    const confirming = bff('post', `/api/bff/instagram-authorizations/${reserved.body.id}/confirm`)
      .set('idempotency-key', randomUUID()).send({}).then((response) => response);
    let channelId: string;
    let generation: number;
    try {
      await waitFor(entered, 'actual Instagram subscription POST barrier');
      const inFlightAttempt = await prisma.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: reserved.body.id } });
      channelId = inFlightAttempt.channelId!;
      generation = (await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: channelId } })).generation;
      const disconnected = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${buId}/channel-connections/${channelId}/disconnect`)
        .send({ expectedGeneration: generation }).expect(200);
      expect(disconnected.body.connection).toMatchObject({ channelId, generation: generation + 1 });
      expect(await prisma.channelCredential.count({ where: { channelId } })).toBe(0);
      release();
      const result = await confirming;
      expect(result.status).toBe(409);
    } finally {
      release();
      instagramFake.afterRequestHook = null;
      await Promise.allSettled([confirming]);
    }
    expect(instagramFake.calls.filter((call) => call.method === 'POST' && call.url.endsWith('/subscribed_apps'))).toHaveLength(postsBefore + 1);
    expect(await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: channelId } }))
      .toMatchObject({ generation: generation + 1, receiptVerifiedAt: null });
    expect(await prisma.channelCredential.count({ where: { channelId } })).toBe(0);
    expect((await prisma.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: reserved.body.id } })).status).toBe('CANCELLED');
  });

  it('reattaches an absent-credential same-account channel with a mapped-history version and requires a new-generation receipt', async () => {
    const buId = tenant.businessUnit.id;
    const accountId = `synthetic-cp4-reconnect-${randomUUID()}`;
    const peerId = `synthetic-cp4-reconnect-peer-${randomUUID()}`;
    const original = await authorize(cookie, buId, accountId, 'reconnect_fixture');
    await ingress(accountId, peerId, `synthetic-cp4-before-disconnect-${randomUUID()}`);
    const oldChannel = await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: original.channel!.id }, include: { credential: true } });
    const oldResource = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { currentChannelId: oldChannel.id } });
    const oldBusiness = await prisma.businessChannelIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: oldChannel.businessIdentityId! } });
    const oldRoute = await prisma.conversationRoute.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { buId, businessIdentityId: oldBusiness.id } });
    const conversation = await prisma.conversation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: oldRoute.conversationId } });
    const options = await bff('get', `/api/bff/conversations/${conversation.id}/reply-options`).expect(200);
    const option = options.body.options.find((candidate: { routeId: string; channelId: string }) =>
      candidate.routeId === oldRoute.id && candidate.channelId === oldChannel.id);
    if (!option) throw new Error('Expected the actual qualified Instagram V2 reply option before disconnect.');
    const replyText = 'completed ordinary reply before Instagram disconnect';
    const postsBeforeReply = instagramFake.calls.filter((call) => call.method === 'POST' && /\/messages$/.test(call.url)).length;
    const reply = await bff('post', `/api/bff/conversations/${conversation.id}/messages`)
      .send({ texto: replyText, schemaVersion: 2, routeId: option.routeId, channelId: option.channelId,
        expectedGeneration: option.generation, contextFingerprint: option.contextFingerprint }).expect(201);
    expect(instagramFake.calls.filter((call) => call.method === 'POST' && /\/messages$/.test(call.url))).toHaveLength(postsBeforeReply + 1);
    const completedMessage = await prisma.message.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: reply.body.id } });
    expect(completedMessage).toMatchObject({ buId, conversationId: conversation.id, direction: 'OUT', channelOrigin: 'INSTAGRAM', content: replyText, status: 'ENVIADA' });
    const acceptedAdmissions = await prisma.dispatchAdmission.findMany({
      where: { buId, conversationId: conversation.id, caller: 'BFF', actorId: tenant.owner.id, state: 'SETTLED', releasedAt: { not: null } },
    });
    expect(acceptedAdmissions).toHaveLength(1);
    const acceptedAdmission = acceptedAdmissions[0];
    if (!acceptedAdmission) throw new Error('Expected the one settled ordinary BFF admission for this reply.');
    expect(acceptedAdmission.selectedContext).toMatchObject({
      buId, conversationId: conversation.id, routeId: oldRoute.id, channelId: oldChannel.id,
      generation: oldChannel.generation, providerResourceIdentityId: oldResource.id,
    });
    const acceptedFacts = {
      id: acceptedAdmission.id, buId: acceptedAdmission.buId, caller: acceptedAdmission.caller,
      selectedContext: acceptedAdmission.selectedContext, state: acceptedAdmission.state,
      expiresAt: acceptedAdmission.expiresAt, dispatchDeadlineAt: acceptedAdmission.dispatchDeadlineAt,
      releasedAt: acceptedAdmission.releasedAt, ownerNonce: acceptedAdmission.ownerNonce,
    };
    const oldMessageCount = await prisma.message.count({ where: { buId, channelOrigin: 'INSTAGRAM' } });
    await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${buId}/channel-connections/${oldChannel.id}/disconnect`)
      .send({ expectedGeneration: oldChannel.generation }).expect(200);
    const retained = await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: oldChannel.id } });
    expect(retained).toMatchObject({ generation: oldChannel.generation + 1, receiptVerifiedAt: null });
    expect(await prisma.channelCredential.findUnique({ where: { channelId: oldChannel.id } })).toBeNull();
    expect(await prisma.message.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: completedMessage.id } })).toEqual(completedMessage);
    expect(await prisma.dispatchAdmission.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: acceptedAdmission.id } })).toMatchObject(acceptedFacts);
    expect(instagramFake.calls.filter((call) => call.method === 'POST' && /\/messages$/.test(call.url)))
      .toHaveLength(postsBeforeReply + 1);
    const mappedVersions = (await prisma.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.findMany({
      where: { channelId: oldChannel.id, professionalUserId: accountId, mappedCredentialVersion: { not: null } },
      select: { mappedCredentialVersion: true },
    })).map(({ mappedCredentialVersion }) => mappedCredentialVersion!);
    const maximumMappedVersion = Math.max(...mappedVersions);

    const reauthorization = await authorize(cookie, buId, accountId, 'reconnect_fixture_v2', 'REAUTHORIZE', oldChannel.id, retained.generation);
    expect(reauthorization.attempt.operation).toBe('REAUTHORIZE');
    expect(reauthorization.channel).toMatchObject({ id: oldChannel.id, generation: retained.generation });
    expect(reauthorization.attempt.mappedCredentialVersion).toBeGreaterThan(maximumMappedVersion);
    expect(reauthorization.channel!.credential!.version).toBeGreaterThan(maximumMappedVersion);
    expect(reauthorization.channel!.credential!.generation).toBe(retained.generation);
    expect(await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: oldResource.id } }))
      .toMatchObject({ id: oldResource.id, currentChannelId: oldChannel.id, businessIdentityId: oldBusiness.id, retiredAt: null });
    expect(await prisma.businessChannelIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: oldBusiness.id } })).toEqual(oldBusiness);
    expect(await prisma.conversationRoute.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: oldRoute.id } })).toEqual(oldRoute);
    expect(await prisma.message.count({ where: { buId, channelOrigin: 'INSTAGRAM' } })).toBe(oldMessageCount);
    expect(await prisma.message.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: completedMessage.id } })).toEqual(completedMessage);
    expect(await prisma.dispatchAdmission.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: acceptedAdmission.id } })).toMatchObject(acceptedFacts);
    expect(instagramFake.calls.filter((call) => call.method === 'POST' && /\/messages$/.test(call.url)))
      .toHaveLength(postsBeforeReply + 1);
    expect(reauthorization.channel!.receiptVerifiedAt).toBeNull();
    await expect(app.get(ChannelCredentialResolver).resolve(oldChannel.id, 'send')).rejects.toMatchObject({ response: { code: 'CHANNEL_NOT_READY' } });
    const decrypted = await app.get(ChannelCredentialResolver).resolve(oldChannel.id, 'verify');
    expect(decrypted).toMatchObject({ channelId: oldChannel.id, buId, generation: retained.generation, instagramAccountId: accountId, activeCredentialVersion: reauthorization.attempt.mappedCredentialVersion });
    expect(decrypted.accessToken).toBe('synthetic-long-lived-token');

    await ingress(accountId, peerId, `synthetic-cp4-after-reconnect-${randomUUID()}`, 'fresh generation receipt');
    const fresh = await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: oldChannel.id } });
    expect(fresh).toMatchObject({ generation: retained.generation, receiptVerifiedAt: expect.any(Date) });
    expect(await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.count({ where: { buId, provider: 'INSTAGRAM', externalResourceId: accountId } })).toBe(1);
    expect(await prisma.buChannel.count({ where: { buId, type: 'INSTAGRAM' } })).toBe(1);
    expect(await prisma.conversationRoute.count({ where: { id: oldRoute.id } })).toBe(1);
  });

  it('keeps the accepted receipt on a live same-generation reauthorization', async () => {
    const buId = tenant.businessUnit.id;
    const accountId = `synthetic-cp4-live-reauth-${randomUUID()}`;
    const connected = await authorize(cookie, buId, accountId, 'live_reauth_fixture');
    await ingress(accountId, `synthetic-cp4-live-peer-${randomUUID()}`);
    const current = await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: connected.channel!.id }, include: { credential: true } });
    const receiptAt = current.receiptVerifiedAt;
    const resource = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { currentChannelId: current.id } });
    const postsBefore = instagramFake.calls.filter((call) => call.method === 'POST' && call.url.endsWith('/subscribed_apps')).length;
    const reauthorized = await authorize(cookie, buId, accountId, 'live_reauth_fixture_v2', 'REAUTHORIZE', current.id, current.generation);
    expect(reauthorized.channel).toMatchObject({ id: current.id, generation: current.generation, receiptVerifiedAt: receiptAt });
    expect(reauthorized.channel!.credential!.version).toBeGreaterThan(current.credential!.version);
    expect(await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: resource.id } }))
      .toMatchObject({ id: resource.id, currentChannelId: current.id, externalResourceId: accountId });
    expect(await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.count({ where: { buId, provider: 'INSTAGRAM', externalResourceId: accountId } })).toBe(1);
    expect(instagramFake.calls.filter((call) => call.method === 'POST' && call.url.endsWith('/subscribed_apps'))).toHaveLength(postsBefore);
  });

  it('rejects wrong-account reauthorization before changing owner, display, or credential', async () => {
    const buId = tenant.businessUnit.id;
    const accountId = `synthetic-cp4-owner-${randomUUID()}`;
    const initial = await authorize(cookie, buId, accountId, 'owner_fixture');
    const channelId = initial.channel!.id;
    const credentialBefore = await prisma.channelCredential.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { channelId } });
    const ownerBefore = await prisma.businessChannelIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: initial.channel!.businessIdentityId! } });
    const reauthorization = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${buId}/instagram-authorizations`)
      .set('idempotency-key', randomUUID()).send({ operation: 'REAUTHORIZE', channelId, expectedGeneration: initial.channel!.generation }).expect(201);
    instagramFake.meEnvelope = { user_id: `synthetic-cp4-wrong-${randomUUID()}`, username: 'wrong_account_fixture' };
    await bff('get', '/api/bff/instagram-authorizations/callback')
      .query({ state: new URL(reauthorization.body.authorizationUrl).searchParams.get('state'), code: `synthetic-cp4-wrong-account-${randomUUID()}` }).expect(303);
    const confirmation = await bff('post', `/api/bff/instagram-authorizations/${reauthorization.body.id}/confirm`)
      .set('idempotency-key', randomUUID()).send({ expectedGeneration: initial.channel!.generation });
    expect(confirmation.status).toBe(409);
    expect(await prisma.businessChannelIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: ownerBefore.id } })).toEqual(ownerBefore);
    expect(await prisma.channelCredential.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { channelId } })).toMatchObject({
      version: credentialBefore.version, generation: credentialBefore.generation, ciphertext: credentialBefore.ciphertext,
    });
    expect(await prisma.buChannel.findMany({ where: { buId, type: 'INSTAGRAM' } })).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findMany({ where: { buId, provider: 'INSTAGRAM' } })).toHaveLength(1);
    expect((await prisma.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: reauthorization.body.id } })).professionalUserId)
      .not.toBe(ownerBefore.externalResourceId);
  });

  it('keeps the global prior-POST uncertainty fence across disconnect and reauthorization', async () => {
    const buId = tenant.businessUnit.id;
    const accountId = `synthetic-cp4-uncertain-${randomUUID()}`;
    instagramFake.meEnvelope = { user_id: accountId, username: 'uncertain_fixture' };
    instagramFake.failAfterSubscription = true;
    const reserved = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${buId}/instagram-authorizations`)
      .set('idempotency-key', randomUUID()).send({ operation: 'CONNECT' }).expect(201);
    await bff('get', '/api/bff/instagram-authorizations/callback')
      .query({ state: new URL(reserved.body.authorizationUrl).searchParams.get('state'), code: `synthetic-cp4-uncertain-${randomUUID()}` }).expect(303);
    const first = await bff('post', `/api/bff/instagram-authorizations/${reserved.body.id}/confirm`)
      .set('idempotency-key', randomUUID()).send({}).expect(200);
    expect(first.body.subscriptionState).toBe('UNCERTAIN');
    const channel = await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: (await prisma.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: reserved.body.id } })).channelId! } });
    const resource = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { currentChannelId: channel.id } });
    expect(resource.instagramSubscriptionPostDispatchStartedAt).not.toBeNull();
    const dispatched = instagramFake.calls.filter((call) => call.method === 'POST' && call.url.endsWith('/subscribed_apps')).length;
    await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${buId}/channel-connections/${channel.id}/disconnect`)
      .send({ expectedGeneration: channel.generation }).expect(200);
    const reconnectedGeneration = channel.generation + 1;
    const next = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${buId}/instagram-authorizations`)
      .set('idempotency-key', randomUUID()).send({ operation: 'REAUTHORIZE', channelId: channel.id, expectedGeneration: reconnectedGeneration }).expect(201);
    instagramFake.meEnvelope = { user_id: accountId, username: 'uncertain_fixture' };
    await bff('get', '/api/bff/instagram-authorizations/callback')
      .query({ state: new URL(next.body.authorizationUrl).searchParams.get('state'), code: `synthetic-cp4-uncertain-reconnect-${randomUUID()}` }).expect(303);
    const [cooldown] = await prisma.$queryRaw<Array<{ waitMs: number }>>`SELECT GREATEST(
      0, CEIL(10025 - EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (clock_timestamp() - instagram_last_subscription_attempt_at)) * 1000)::int
    ) AS "waitMs" FROM provider_resource_identities WHERE id = ${resource.id}`;
    if (!cooldown) throw new Error('Expected the durable resource cooldown timestamp before reconnect confirmation.');
    if (cooldown.waitMs > 10_500) throw new Error('The durable resource cooldown exceeds the bounded native wait.');
    if (cooldown.waitMs > 0) {
      await waitFor(new Promise<void>((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, cooldown.waitMs)), 'actual Instagram resource cooldown', 11_000);
    }
    const nextConfirm = await bff('post', `/api/bff/instagram-authorizations/${next.body.id}/confirm`)
      .set('idempotency-key', randomUUID()).send({ expectedGeneration: reconnectedGeneration }).expect(200);
    expect(nextConfirm.body.subscriptionState).toBe('UNCERTAIN');
    expect(instagramFake.calls.filter((call) => call.method === 'POST' && call.url.endsWith('/subscribed_apps'))).toHaveLength(dispatched);
    expect(instagramFake.calls.some((call) => call.method === 'GET' && call.url.endsWith('/subscribed_apps'))).toBe(true);
    expect(await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: resource.id } }))
      .toMatchObject({ id: resource.id, currentChannelId: channel.id });
    expect((await prisma.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: next.body.id } })).subscriptionState).toBe('UNCERTAIN');
  });
});
```
