```text

import { INestApplication } from '@nestjs/common';
import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import { afterAll, beforeAll, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { PrismaService } from '../src/infra/prisma/prisma.service';
import { resolveOrCreateContactIdentity, whatsappPeerAddress } from '../src/domain/inbox/contact-identity';
import { claimWhatsAppBusinessResource } from '../src/domain/channels/business-resource-ownership';
import { criarMantenedora, limparBanco, subirApp } from './helpers';

describe('Instagram identity and immutable route constraints (PostgreSQL)', () => {
  let app: INestApplication;
  let prisma: PrismaService;
  let ownerBuId: string;

  beforeAll(async () => {
    ({ app, prisma } = await subirApp());
    await app.listen(0, '127.0.0.1');
  });
  afterAll(async () => {
    if (prisma) await limparBanco(prisma);
    await app?.close();
  });
  beforeEach(async () => {
    await limparBanco(prisma);
    const tenant = await criarMantenedora(app, 'Instagram identity', 'instagram-identity@example.test');
    ownerBuId = tenant.businessUnit.id;
  });

  async function businessIdentity(input: {
    platform: 'WHATSAPP' | 'INSTAGRAM';
    externalResourceId: string;
    scopeReference?: string;
  }) {
    const scopeReference = input.scopeReference ?? (input.platform === 'WHATSAPP' ? 'BU' : input.externalResourceId);
    return prisma.businessChannelIdentity.create({ data: {
      buId: ownerBuId,
      platform: input.platform,
      namespace: input.platform === 'WHATSAPP' ? 'WHATSAPP_RESOURCE' : 'INSTAGRAM_LOGIN',
      scopeReference,
      externalResourceId: input.externalResourceId,
    } });
  }

  async function peer(input: {
    platform: 'WHATSAPP' | 'INSTAGRAM';
    externalPeerId: string;
    contactId?: string;
    scopeReference?: string;
    namespace?: string;
  }) {
    const contact = await prisma.contact.create({ data: { buId: ownerBuId, id: input.contactId ?? randomUUID() } });
    const identity = await prisma.contactIdentity.create({ data: {
      buId: ownerBuId,
      contactId: contact.id,
      platform: input.platform,
      namespace: input.namespace ?? (input.platform === 'WHATSAPP' ? 'PERSON' : 'INSTAGRAM_PEER'),
      scopeReference: input.scopeReference ?? (input.platform === 'WHATSAPP' ? 'BU' : 'ig-account-1'),
      externalPeerId: input.externalPeerId,
    } });
    const conversation = await prisma.conversation.create({ data: {
      buId: ownerBuId,
      contactId: contact.id,
      contact: input.externalPeerId,
      contactType: 'PESSOA',
    } });
    return { contact, identity, conversation };
  }

  it('allows one durable WhatsApp identity and multiple independent Instagram accounts per BU', async () => {
    const whatsapp = await businessIdentity({ platform: 'WHATSAPP', externalResourceId: 'wa-business-1' });
    await expect(businessIdentity({ platform: 'WHATSAPP', externalResourceId: 'wa-business-2' })).rejects.toThrow();

    const first = await businessIdentity({ platform: 'INSTAGRAM', externalResourceId: 'ig-account-1' });
    const second = await businessIdentity({ platform: 'INSTAGRAM', externalResourceId: 'ig-account-2' });
    expect(first.buId).toBe(whatsapp.buId);
    expect(second.buId).toBe(whatsapp.buId);
    expect(await prisma.businessChannelIdentity.count({ where: { buId: ownerBuId, platform: 'INSTAGRAM' } })).toBe(2);
  });

  it('keeps legacy adapter aliases unambiguous within a BU and adapter type', async () => {
    await prisma.buChannel.create({ data: { buId: ownerBuId, type: 'OFICIAL' } });
    await expect(prisma.buChannel.create({ data: { buId: ownerBuId, type: 'OFICIAL' } })).rejects.toThrow();
  });

  it('separates equal external peer strings by platform and account scope', async () => {
    const whatsapp = await peer({ platform: 'WHATSAPP', externalPeerId: '5511999990000' });
    const instagram = await peer({ platform: 'INSTAGRAM', externalPeerId: '5511999990000', scopeReference: 'ig-account-1' });
    const otherAccount = await peer({ platform: 'INSTAGRAM', externalPeerId: '5511999990000', scopeReference: 'ig-account-2' });
    expect(new Set([whatsapp.contact.id, instagram.contact.id, otherAccount.contact.id]).size).toBe(3);
    await expect(prisma.contactIdentity.create({ data: {
      buId: ownerBuId, contactId: otherAccount.contact.id, platform: 'INSTAGRAM', namespace: 'INSTAGRAM_PEER',
      scopeReference: 'ig-account-2', externalPeerId: '5511999990000',
    } })).rejects.toThrow();
  });

  it('reuses one local WhatsApp conversation across manual start and verified-resource ingress without inventing a route', async () => {
    const manual = await prisma.$transaction((tx) => resolveOrCreateContactIdentity(tx, whatsappPeerAddress({
      buId: ownerBuId, externalPeerId: '+55 (11) 98765-4321', contactType: 'PESSOA',
    })));
    const conversation = await prisma.conversation.create({ data: {
      buId: ownerBuId, contactId: manual.contactId, contact: '5511987654321', contactType: 'PESSOA',
    } });
    const ingress = await prisma.$transaction((tx) => resolveOrCreateContactIdentity(tx, whatsappPeerAddress({
      buId: ownerBuId, externalPeerId: '5511987654321', contactType: 'PESSOA', groupScope: 'verified-resource-2',
    })));
    const reused = await prisma.conversation.findFirst({ where: { buId: ownerBuId, contactId: ingress.contactId } });
    expect(ingress.contactId).toBe(manual.contactId);
    expect(reused?.id).toBe(conversation.id);
    expect(await prisma.conversationRoute.count({ where: { conversationId: conversation.id } })).toBe(0);
  });

  it('does not invent a route when an old local conversation has no proved resource association', async () => {
    const legacy = await peer({ platform: 'WHATSAPP', externalPeerId: '5511888880000' });
    expect(await prisma.conversationRoute.count({ where: { conversationId: legacy.conversation.id } })).toBe(0);
  });

  it('requires the route to match the conversation contact and business resource and prevents mutation', async () => {
    const resource = await businessIdentity({ platform: 'INSTAGRAM', externalResourceId: 'ig-account-1' });
    const selected = await peer({ platform: 'INSTAGRAM', externalPeerId: 'peer-1', scopeReference: resource.externalResourceId });
    const wrongPeer = await peer({ platform: 'INSTAGRAM', externalPeerId: 'peer-2', scopeReference: resource.externalResourceId });
    const route = await prisma.conversationRoute.create({ data: {
      buId: ownerBuId,
      conversationId: selected.conversation.id,
      contactId: selected.contact.id,
      contactIdentityId: selected.identity.id,
      platform: 'INSTAGRAM',
      contactScopeReference: selected.identity.scopeReference,
      businessScopeReference: resource.scopeReference,
      businessIdentityId: resource.id,
    } });
    expect(route.id).toEqual(expect.any(String));

    await expect(prisma.conversationRoute.create({ data: {
      buId: ownerBuId,
      conversationId: selected.conversation.id,
      contactId: selected.contact.id,
      contactIdentityId: wrongPeer.identity.id,
      platform: 'INSTAGRAM',
      contactScopeReference: wrongPeer.identity.scopeReference,
      businessScopeReference: resource.scopeReference,
      businessIdentityId: resource.id,
    } })).rejects.toThrow();

    await expect(prisma.conversationRoute.update({ where: { id: route.id }, data: { contactIdentityId: wrongPeer.identity.id } })).rejects.toThrow();
    await expect(prisma.conversationRoute.delete({ where: { id: route.id } })).rejects.toThrow();
    await expect(prisma.contactIdentity.update({ where: { id: selected.identity.id }, data: { externalPeerId: 'different-peer' } })).rejects.toThrow();
    await expect(prisma.contactIdentity.update({ where: { id: selected.identity.id }, data: { namespace: 'different-namespace' } })).rejects.toThrow();
    await expect(prisma.contactIdentity.update({ where: { id: selected.identity.id }, data: { contactId: wrongPeer.contact.id } })).rejects.toThrow();
  });

  it('rejects an Instagram peer from account A routed through account B at the database constraint', async () => {
    const accountA = await businessIdentity({ platform: 'INSTAGRAM', externalResourceId: 'ig-account-a' });
    const accountB = await businessIdentity({ platform: 'INSTAGRAM', externalResourceId: 'ig-account-b' });
    const selected = await peer({ platform: 'INSTAGRAM', externalPeerId: 'peer-a', scopeReference: accountA.scopeReference });

    await expect(prisma.conversationRoute.create({ data: {
      buId: ownerBuId,
      conversationId: selected.conversation.id,
      contactId: selected.contact.id,
      contactIdentityId: selected.identity.id,
      platform: 'INSTAGRAM',
      contactScopeReference: accountA.scopeReference,
      businessScopeReference: accountB.scopeReference,
      businessIdentityId: accountB.id,
    } })).rejects.toThrow();
  });

  it('enforces current provider attachment BU, business resource and adapter equality in PostgreSQL', async () => {
    const resourceA = await businessIdentity({ platform: 'INSTAGRAM', externalResourceId: 'ig-resource-a' });
    const resourceB = await businessIdentity({ platform: 'INSTAGRAM', externalResourceId: 'ig-resource-b' });
    const channelA = await prisma.buChannel.create({ data: { buId: ownerBuId, type: 'INSTAGRAM', businessIdentityId: resourceA.id } });
    const channelB = await prisma.buChannel.create({ data: { buId: ownerBuId, type: 'INSTAGRAM', businessIdentityId: resourceB.id } });
    await expect(prisma.buChannel.create({ data: { buId: ownerBuId, type: 'OFICIAL', businessIdentityId: resourceA.id } })).rejects.toThrow();
    await expect(prisma.providerResourceIdentity.create({ data: {
      provider: 'INSTAGRAM', externalResourceId: 'ig-provider-wrong-resource', buId: ownerBuId,
      currentChannelId: channelB.id, businessIdentityId: resourceA.id,
    } })).rejects.toThrow();

    const other = await criarMantenedora(app, 'Other Instagram identity', 'other-instagram-identity@example.test');
    const foreignChannel = await prisma.buChannel.create({ data: { buId: other.businessUnit.id, type: 'INSTAGRAM' } });
    await expect(prisma.providerResourceIdentity.create({ data: {
      provider: 'INSTAGRAM', externalResourceId: 'ig-provider-cross-bu', buId: ownerBuId,
      currentChannelId: foreignChannel.id, businessIdentityId: resourceA.id,
    } })).rejects.toThrow();

    const whatsapp = await businessIdentity({ platform: 'WHATSAPP', externalResourceId: 'wa-business-owner' });
    const evolutionChannel = await prisma.buChannel.create({ data: { buId: ownerBuId, type: 'NAO_OFICIAL', businessIdentityId: whatsapp.id } });
    await expect(prisma.providerResourceIdentity.create({ data: {
      provider: 'OFICIAL', externalResourceId: 'wa-provider-wrong-adapter', buId: ownerBuId,
      currentChannelId: evolutionChannel.id, businessIdentityId: whatsapp.id,
    } })).rejects.toThrow();
    expect(channelA.businessIdentityId).toBe(resourceA.id);
  });

  it('retains business ownership when disconnected and rejects deletion or cross-BU reclaim', async () => {
    const owner = await businessIdentity({ platform: 'WHATSAPP', externalResourceId: 'wa-retained-owner' });
    await expect(prisma.businessChannelIdentity.delete({ where: { id: owner.id } })).rejects.toThrow();
    const other = await criarMantenedora(app, 'Retained owner claimant', 'retained-owner-claimant@example.test');
    await expect(prisma.businessChannelIdentity.create({ data: {
      buId: other.businessUnit.id, platform: 'WHATSAPP', namespace: owner.namespace,
      scopeReference: owner.scopeReference, externalResourceId: owner.externalResourceId,
    } })).rejects.toThrow();
    await expect(businessIdentity({ platform: 'WHATSAPP', externalResourceId: 'wa-second-owner' })).rejects.toThrow();
  });

  it('serializes provider-proved WhatsApp ownership claims across adapters and BUs', async () => {
    const other = await criarMantenedora(app, 'Concurrent WA claimant', 'concurrent-wa-claimant@example.test');
    const official = await prisma.buChannel.create({ data: { buId: ownerBuId, type: 'OFICIAL', status: 'CONECTADO' } });
    const evolution = await prisma.buChannel.create({ data: { buId: other.businessUnit.id, type: 'NAO_OFICIAL', status: 'CONECTADO' } });
    const officialProvider = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.create({ data: {
      buId: ownerBuId, provider: 'OFICIAL', externalResourceId: `official-${randomUUID()}`, currentChannelId: official.id,
    } });
    const evolutionProvider = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.create({ data: {
      buId: other.businessUnit.id, provider: 'NAO_OFICIAL', externalResourceId: `evolution-${randomUUID()}`, currentChannelId: evolution.id,
    } });

    const contenders = [
      { buId: ownerBuId, channelId: official.id, providerId: officialProvider.id, claim: () => prisma.$transaction((tx) => claimWhatsAppBusinessResource(tx, {
        buId: ownerBuId, channelId: official.id, providerResourceIdentityId: officialProvider.id,
        adapter: 'OFICIAL', provedPhone: '+55 (11) 98888-0000',
      })) },
      { buId: other.businessUnit.id, channelId: evolution.id, providerId: evolutionProvider.id, claim: () => prisma.$transaction((tx) => claimWhatsAppBusinessResource(tx, {
        buId: other.businessUnit.id, channelId: evolution.id, providerResourceIdentityId: evolutionProvider.id,
        adapter: 'NAO_OFICIAL', provedPhone: '5511988880000',
      })) },
    ];
    const outcomes = await Promise.allSettled(contenders.map((contender) => contender.claim()));
    expect(outcomes.filter((outcome) => outcome.status === 'fulfilled')).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(outcomes.filter((outcome) => outcome.status === 'rejected')).toHaveLength(1);
    const winnerIndex = outcomes.findIndex((outcome) => outcome.status === 'fulfilled');
    const winner = contenders[winnerIndex]!;
    const loser = contenders[1 - winnerIndex];
    const owner = await prisma.businessChannelIdentity.findUnique({
      where: { platform_namespace_externalResourceId: { platform: 'WHATSAPP', namespace: 'WHATSAPP_RESOURCE', externalResourceId: '5511988880000' } },
    });
    expect(owner?.buId).toBe(winner.buId);
    expect(await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: winner.channelId } })).toMatchObject({ businessIdentityId: owner?.id });
    expect(await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: winner.providerId } })).toMatchObject({ currentChannelId: winner.channelId, businessIdentityId: owner?.id });
    expect(await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: loser.channelId } })).toMatchObject({ businessIdentityId: null });
    expect(await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: loser.providerId } })).toMatchObject({ currentChannelId: loser.channelId, businessIdentityId: null });
  });
});
```

```text
import { INestApplication } from '@nestjs/common';
import request from 'supertest';
import { afterAll, beforeAll, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { PrismaService } from '../src/infra/prisma/prisma.service';
import { claimWhatsAppBusinessResource } from '../src/domain/channels/business-resource-ownership';
import * as businessResourceOwnership from '../src/domain/channels/business-resource-ownership';
import { resolveOrCreateContactIdentity, whatsappPeerAddress } from '../src/domain/inbox/contact-identity';
import { EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_SECRET, contratarCanalNaoOficial, criarMantenedora, limparBanco, login, metaFake, evolutionFake, subirApp, tetoComCanalNaoOficial, typebotFake } from './helpers';
import { RealtimeService } from '../src/realtime/realtime.service';
import { EvolutionHttpClient } from '../src/integrations/evolution/evolution.client';

describe('Instagram CP1 WhatsApp ownership lifecycle (PostgreSQL)', () => {
  let app: INestApplication;
  let prisma: PrismaService;
  let tenant: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof criarMantenedora>>;
  let cookie: string;

  beforeAll(async () => {
    tetoComCanalNaoOficial();
    process.env.EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_URL = 'https://example.test/webhooks/evolution';
    ({ app, prisma } = await subirApp());
  });
  afterAll(async () => { if (prisma) await limparBanco(prisma); await app?.close(); });
  beforeEach(async () => {
    await limparBanco(prisma);
    tenant = await criarMantenedora(app, 'Instagram lifecycle', 'instagram-lifecycle@example.test');
    cookie = await login(app, 'instagram-lifecycle@example.test');
    metaFake.wabaRecusada = null;
    metaFake.wabaRecursos = [
      { wabaId: '123456789', phoneNumberId: '111111111', telefone: '5584998880000' },
      { wabaId: '123456789', phoneNumberId: '987654321', telefone: '5584999990000' },
    ];
    metaFake.registrosNumero = 0;
    metaFake.subscriptionHook = null;
    evolutionFake.createdState = 'connecting';
    evolutionFake.ownerPhone = null;
    evolutionFake.inventory.clear();
  });

  const bff = (verb: 'get' | 'post', path: string) => {
    const req = request(app.getHttpServer())[verb](path).set('Cookie', cookie);
    return verb === 'post' ? req.set('x-requested-with', 'teste') : req;
  };

  const webhook = (instance: string, key: string, text = 'synthetic lifecycle message') => request(app.getHttpServer())
    .post('/webhooks/evolution').set('x-leadshug-webhook-secret', EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_SECRET)
    .send({ event: 'messages.upsert', instance, data: { key: { remoteJid: '5584997776666@s.whatsapp.net', fromMe: false, id: key }, pushName: 'Synthetic peer', message: { conversation: text } } });

  async function seedRenewalOwner() {
    const identity = await prisma.businessChannelIdentity.create({ data: {
      buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, platform: 'WHATSAPP', namespace: 'WHATSAPP_RESOURCE',
      scopeReference: 'BU', externalResourceId: '5584999990000',
    } });
    const channel = await prisma.buChannel.create({ data: {
      buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, type: 'NAO_OFICIAL', status: 'CONECTADO', evolutionInstanceName: 'synthetic-renew-old',
      ownNumberVerified: true, businessIdentityId: identity.id,
    } });
    await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.create({ data: {
      buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, provider: 'NAO_OFICIAL', externalResourceId: 'synthetic-renew-old', currentChannelId: channel.id,
      businessIdentityId: identity.id, managedLifecycle: true, cleanupState: 'ACTIVE',
    } });
    evolutionFake.inventory.set('synthetic-renew-old', { name: 'synthetic-renew-old', ownerJid: '5584999990000@s.whatsapp.net', connectionStatus: 'open' });
    evolutionFake.createdState = 'open';
    evolutionFake.ownerPhone = '5584999990000';
    return { identity, channel };
  }

  it('claims the freshly confirmed official number before credential promotion', async () => {
    const created = await bff('post', '/api/bff/number-registrations').set('Idempotency-Key', 'aaaaaaaa-aaaa-4aaa-8aaa-aaaaaaaaaaaa')
      .send({ buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, tipo: 'OFICIAL', operation: 'NOVO' }).expect(201);
    const id = created.body.attempt.id as string;
    await bff('post', `/api/bff/number-registrations/${id}/authorize`).send({ nonce: created.body.nonce, code: 'fixture-oauth-code' }).expect(200);
    await bff('get', `/api/bff/number-registrations/${id}/options?wabaId=123456789&after=fixture-page-1`).expect(200);
    const confirmed = await bff('post', `/api/bff/number-registrations/${id}/confirm`)
      .send({ wabaId: '123456789', phoneNumberId: '987654321', modoRegistro: 'JA_REGISTRADO', after: 'fixture-page-1' });
    expect(confirmed.status, JSON.stringify(confirmed.body)).toBe(200);
    const channel = await prisma.buChannel.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, type: 'OFICIAL' } });
    const provider = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { currentChannelId: channel.id } });
    const owner = await prisma.businessChannelIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({
      where: { platform_namespace_externalResourceId: { platform: 'WHATSAPP', namespace: 'WHATSAPP_RESOURCE', externalResourceId: '5584999990000' } },
    });
    expect(channel.businessIdentityId).toBe(owner.id);
    expect(provider.businessIdentityId).toBe(owner.id);
    expect(await prisma.channelCredential.count({ where: { channelId: channel.id } })).toBe(1);
  });

  it('claims a pending managed Evolution pairing only after current provider proof', async () => {
    await contratarCanalNaoOficial(prisma, tenant.mantenedora.id);
    const paired = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/nao-oficial/pair`).send({ intent: 'INITIAL' }).expect(200);
    const channel = await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: paired.body.channelId } });
    const name = channel.evolutionInstanceName!;
    expect(channel.ownNumberVerified).toBe(false);
    evolutionFake.inventory.set(name, { name, ownerJid: '5584999990000@s.whatsapp.net', connectionStatus: 'open' });
    await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/NAO_OFICIAL/verify`).send({}).expect(200);
    const [updated, provider] = await Promise.all([
      prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: channel.id } }),
      prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { currentChannelId: channel.id } }),
    ]);
    expect(updated.ownNumberVerified).toBe(true);
    expect(updated.businessIdentityId).toBeTruthy();
    expect(provider.businessIdentityId).toBe(updated.businessIdentityId);
  });

  it('claims a migrated managed unmapped provider only after provider proof', async () => {
    await contratarCanalNaoOficial(prisma, tenant.mantenedora.id);
    const channel = await prisma.buChannel.create({ data: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, type: 'NAO_OFICIAL', status: 'CONECTANDO', evolutionInstanceName: 'synthetic-migrated-managed' } });
    const provider = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.create({ data: { provider: 'NAO_OFICIAL', externalResourceId: 'synthetic-migrated-managed', buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, currentChannelId: channel.id, managedLifecycle: true, cleanupState: 'ACTIVE' } });
    evolutionFake.inventory.set(provider.externalResourceId, { name: provider.externalResourceId, ownerJid: '5584999990000@s.whatsapp.net', connectionStatus: 'open' });
    await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/NAO_OFICIAL/verify`).send({}).expect(200);
    expect((await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: provider.id } })).businessIdentityId).toBeTruthy();

  });

  it('rejects a foreign retained owner before trust or link mutation', async () => {
    const foreign = await criarMantenedora(app, 'Foreign retained owner', 'instagram-foreign-owner@example.test');
    await contratarCanalNaoOficial(prisma, tenant.mantenedora.id);
    await prisma.businessUnit.update({ where: { id: tenant.businessUnit.id }, data: { phone: null } });
    const owner = await prisma.businessChannelIdentity.create({ data: { buId: foreign.businessUnit.id, platform: 'WHATSAPP', namespace: 'WHATSAPP_RESOURCE', scopeReference: 'BU', externalResourceId: '5584990000111' } });
    const targetChannel = await prisma.buChannel.create({ data: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, type: 'NAO_OFICIAL', status: 'CONECTANDO', evolutionInstanceName: 'synthetic-foreign-owner' } });
    const targetProvider = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.create({ data: { provider: 'NAO_OFICIAL', externalResourceId: 'synthetic-foreign-owner', buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, currentChannelId: targetChannel.id, managedLifecycle: true, cleanupState: 'ACTIVE' } });
    evolutionFake.inventory.set(targetProvider.externalResourceId, { name: targetProvider.externalResourceId, ownerJid: `${owner.externalResourceId}@s.whatsapp.net`, connectionStatus: 'open' });
    const grantsBefore = await prisma.setorGrant.findMany({ where: { setorId: tenant.businessUnit.setorId }, orderBy: { id: 'asc' } });
    const credentialsBefore = await prisma.channelCredential.findMany({ where: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id }, orderBy: { id: 'asc' }, select: { id: true, provider: true, generation: true, version: true } });
    const result = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/NAO_OFICIAL/verify`).send({});
    expect(result.status, JSON.stringify(result.body)).toBe(409);
    expect(await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: targetChannel.id } })).toMatchObject({ ownNumberVerified: false, businessIdentityId: null });
    expect(await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: targetProvider.id } })).toMatchObject({ businessIdentityId: null });
    expect(await prisma.businessChannelIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: owner.id } })).toMatchObject({ buId: foreign.businessUnit.id });
    expect(await prisma.setorGrant.findMany({ where: { setorId: tenant.businessUnit.setorId }, orderBy: { id: 'asc' } })).toEqual(grantsBefore);
    expect(await prisma.channelCredential.findMany({ where: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id }, orderBy: { id: 'asc' }, select: { id: true, provider: true, generation: true, version: true } })).toEqual(credentialsBefore);
  });

  it('creates a WhatsApp route from an actual claimed owner and canonical PERSON identity', async () => {
    const channel = await prisma.buChannel.create({ data: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, type: 'OFICIAL' } });
    const provider = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.create({ data: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, provider: 'OFICIAL', externalResourceId: 'synthetic-route-phone', currentChannelId: channel.id } });
    const owner = await prisma.$transaction((tx) => claimWhatsAppBusinessResource(tx, {
      buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, channelId: channel.id, providerResourceIdentityId: provider.id, adapter: 'OFICIAL', provedPhone: '5584999990000',
    }));
    const identity = await prisma.$transaction((tx) => resolveOrCreateContactIdentity(tx, whatsappPeerAddress({
      buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, externalPeerId: '5584998880000', contactType: 'PESSOA',
    })));
    const conversation = await prisma.conversation.create({ data: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, contactId: identity.contactId, contact: '5584998880000', contactType: 'PESSOA' } });
    await prisma.conversationRoute.create({ data: {
      buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, conversationId: conversation.id, contactId: identity.contactId, contactIdentityId: identity.id,
      platform: 'WHATSAPP', contactScopeReference: 'BU', businessScopeReference: owner.scopeReference, businessIdentityId: owner.id,
    } });
    expect(await prisma.conversationRoute.count({ where: { conversationId: conversation.id, businessIdentityId: owner.id } })).toBe(1);
  });

  it('completes a managed RENEW only after the replacement instance proves the retained number', async () => {
    await contratarCanalNaoOficial(prisma, tenant.mantenedora.id);
    const { channel, identity } = await seedRenewalOwner();
    const retired = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/nao-oficial/pair`)
      .send({ intent: 'RENEW', expectedGeneration: channel.generation }).expect(200);
    expect(retired.body.state).toBe('CLEANUP_COMPLETED_RETRY_PAIR');
    const staged = await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: channel.id } });
    expect(staged.businessIdentityId).toBe(identity.id);
    expect(await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { provider_externalResourceId: { provider: 'NAO_OFICIAL', externalResourceId: staged.evolutionInstanceName! } } }))
      .toMatchObject({ currentChannelId: null, businessIdentityId: null, unverifiedStaging: true });
    const renewed = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/nao-oficial/pair`)
      .send({ intent: 'INITIAL', expectedGeneration: staged.generation });
    expect(renewed.status, JSON.stringify(renewed.body)).toBe(200);
    expect(renewed.body.state).toBe('PENDING_RECEIPT');
    const finalChannel = await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: channel.id } });
    const currentProvider = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { currentChannelId: channel.id } });
    expect(finalChannel).toMatchObject({ ownNumberVerified: true, businessIdentityId: identity.id });
    expect(currentProvider).toMatchObject({ businessIdentityId: identity.id, externalResourceId: finalChannel.evolutionInstanceName });
  });

  it('resumes the same retained RENEW reservation after actual provider cleanup observation becomes conclusive', async () => {
    await contratarCanalNaoOficial(prisma, tenant.mantenedora.id);
    const { channel } = await seedRenewalOwner();
    evolutionFake.createdState = 'connecting';
    evolutionFake.ownerPhone = null;

    const baseUrl = 'https://evolution.synthetic.invalid';
    const oldInstanceName = channel.evolutionInstanceName!;
    const previousFetch = globalThis.fetch;
    const previousObservation = evolutionFake.consultarInstancia.bind(evolutionFake);
    const requestedInstances: string[] = [];
    const requestMethods: string[] = [];
    const requestKeys: Array<string | null> = [];
    const responseStatuses: number[] = [];
    const fetchStub = vi.fn(async (input: Parameters<typeof fetch>[0], init?: Parameters<typeof fetch>[1]): Promise<Response> => {
      const url = new URL(input instanceof Request ? input.url : String(input));
      requestedInstances.push(url.searchParams.get('instanceName') ?? '');
      requestMethods.push(init?.method ?? 'GET');
      requestKeys.push(new Headers(init?.headers).get('apikey'));
      const isExpectedObservation = url.origin === baseUrl
        && url.pathname === '/instance/fetchInstances'
        && url.searchParams.has('instanceName');
      const status = !isExpectedObservation ? 400 : responseStatuses.length === 0 ? 503 : 200;
      responseStatuses.push(status);
      const instanceName = url.searchParams.get('instanceName') ?? '';
      if (status === 200 && instanceName === oldInstanceName) evolutionFake.inventory.delete(oldInstanceName);
      const observed = evolutionFake.inventory.get(instanceName);
      const body = status === 503
        ? JSON.stringify({ message: 'synthetic provider unavailable' })
        : JSON.stringify(observed ? [{ name: observed.name, ownerJid: observed.ownerJid, connectionStatus: observed.connectionStatus }] : []);
      return new Response(body, { status, headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' } });
    });

    vi.stubEnv('EVOLUTION_API_URL', baseUrl);
    vi.stubEnv('EVOLUTION_API_KEY', 'synthetic-evolution-key');
    globalThis.fetch = fetchStub;
    try {
      const actualEvolution = new EvolutionHttpClient();
      evolutionFake.consultarInstancia = (name) => actualEvolution.consultarInstancia(name);

      const initialCreates = evolutionFake.createCalls;
      const renewed = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/nao-oficial/pair`)
        .send({ intent: 'RENEW', expectedGeneration: channel.generation }).expect(200);
      expect(renewed.body.state).toBe('CLEANUP_AMBIGUOUS');
      const ambiguous = await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: channel.id } });
      const attemptId = ambiguous.currentAttemptId!;
      const priorIdentityId = ambiguous.pendingCleanupIdentityId!;
      const reservedTuple = {
        generation: ambiguous.generation,
        pairingEpoch: ambiguous.pairingEpoch,
        currentAttemptId: attemptId,
        evolutionInstanceName: ambiguous.evolutionInstanceName,
      };
      const attemptFence = {
        id: attemptId,
        channelId: channel.id,
        buId: tenant.businessUnit.id,
        type: 'NAO_OFICIAL',
        generation: reservedTuple.generation,
        pairingEpoch: reservedTuple.pairingEpoch,
      };
      expect(ambiguous).toMatchObject({ ...reservedTuple, pendingCleanupInstanceName: channel.evolutionInstanceName, pendingCleanupIdentityId: expect.any(String) });
      expect(ambiguous.generation).toBeGreaterThan(channel.generation);
      expect(ambiguous.evolutionInstanceName).not.toBe(channel.evolutionInstanceName);
      expect(await prisma.channelRegistrationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: attemptId } }))
        .toMatchObject({ ...attemptFence, state: 'ERROR', stage: 'CLEANUP_AMBIGUOUS', safeErrorCode: 'CLEANUP_AMBIGUOUS' });
      expect(await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: priorIdentityId } }))
        .toMatchObject({ externalResourceId: channel.evolutionInstanceName, cleanupState: 'CLEANUP_PENDING', currentChannelId: null });
      expect(evolutionFake.createCalls).toBe(initialCreates);
      expect(requestedInstances).toEqual([oldInstanceName]);
      expect(requestMethods).toEqual(['GET']);
      expect(requestKeys).toEqual(['synthetic-evolution-key']);
      expect(responseStatuses).toEqual([503]);

      const reconciled = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/nao-oficial/pair`)
        .send({ intent: 'INITIAL', expectedGeneration: reservedTuple.generation });
      expect(reconciled.status).toBe(409);
      expect(reconciled.body.code).toBe('CLEANUP_COMPLETED_RETRY_PAIR');
      expect(await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: priorIdentityId } }))
        .toMatchObject({ cleanupState: 'CLEANED', cleanupLeaseToken: null, cleanupLeaseUntil: null });
      const stillAmbiguous = await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: channel.id } });
      expect(stillAmbiguous).toMatchObject({ ...reservedTuple, pendingCleanupInstanceName: channel.evolutionInstanceName, pendingCleanupIdentityId: priorIdentityId });
      expect(await prisma.channelRegistrationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: attemptId } }))
        .toMatchObject({ ...attemptFence, state: 'ERROR', stage: 'CLEANUP_AMBIGUOUS', safeErrorCode: 'CLEANUP_AMBIGUOUS' });
      expect(requestedInstances).toEqual([oldInstanceName, oldInstanceName]);
      expect(responseStatuses).toEqual([503, 200]);
      expect(evolutionFake.createCalls).toBe(initialCreates);

      const prepared = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/nao-oficial/pair`)
        .send({ intent: 'INITIAL', expectedGeneration: reservedTuple.generation });
      expect(prepared.status, JSON.stringify(prepared.body)).toBe(200);
      expect(prepared.body.state).toBe('CLEANUP_COMPLETED_RETRY_PAIR');
      const reserved = await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: channel.id } });
      expect(reserved).toMatchObject({ ...reservedTuple, pendingCleanupInstanceName: null, pendingCleanupIdentityId: null, ownNumberVerified: false, verifiedEpoch: null });
      expect(await prisma.channelRegistrationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: attemptId } }))
        .toMatchObject({ ...attemptFence, state: 'PENDING_NUMBER', stage: 'RESERVED_NEVER_CREATED', safeErrorCode: null });
      expect(evolutionFake.createCalls).toBe(initialCreates);
      expect(requestedInstances).toEqual([oldInstanceName, oldInstanceName, oldInstanceName]);
      expect(responseStatuses).toEqual([503, 200, 200]);

      const createsBeforeReservedCreate = evolutionFake.createCalls;
      const created = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/nao-oficial/pair`)
        .send({ intent: 'INITIAL', expectedGeneration: reservedTuple.generation });
      expect(created.status, JSON.stringify(created.body)).toBe(200);
      expect(created.body.state).toBe('PENDING_IDENTITY');
      expect(evolutionFake.createCalls).toBe(createsBeforeReservedCreate + 1);
      const afterCreate = await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: channel.id } });
      expect(afterCreate).toMatchObject({ ...reservedTuple, pendingCleanupInstanceName: null, pendingCleanupIdentityId: null, ownNumberVerified: false, verifiedEpoch: null });
      const replacementIdentity = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({
        where: { provider_externalResourceId: { provider: 'NAO_OFICIAL', externalResourceId: reservedTuple.evolutionInstanceName! } },
      });
      expect(replacementIdentity).toMatchObject({ currentChannelId: null, unverifiedStaging: true, businessIdentityId: null, cleanupState: 'ACTIVE' });
      expect(requestedInstances).toEqual([oldInstanceName, oldInstanceName, oldInstanceName, reservedTuple.evolutionInstanceName]);
      expect(requestMethods).toEqual(['GET', 'GET', 'GET', 'GET']);
      expect(requestKeys).toEqual(Array(4).fill('synthetic-evolution-key'));
      expect(responseStatuses).toEqual([503, 200, 200, 200]);

      const publish = vi.spyOn(app.get(RealtimeService), 'publicarNaBu');
      try {
        const messageCountBefore = await prisma.message.count({ where: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id } });
        const conversationsBefore = await prisma.conversation.count({ where: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id } });
        const botStartsBefore = typebotFake.starts;
        const ingress = await webhook(reservedTuple.evolutionInstanceName!, 'renewal-cleanup-unproved-ingress');
        expect(ingress.status).toBe(503);
        expect(ingress.body.code).toBe('PENDING_IDENTITY');
        expect(await prisma.message.count({ where: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id } })).toBe(messageCountBefore);
        expect(await prisma.conversation.count({ where: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id } })).toBe(conversationsBefore);
        expect(typebotFake.starts).toBe(botStartsBefore);
        expect(publish).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      } finally {
        publish.mockRestore();
      }
    } finally {
      await prisma.buChannel.updateMany({
        where: { id: channel.id },
        data: { pendingCleanupInstanceName: null, pendingCleanupIdentityId: null },
      });
      evolutionFake.consultarInstancia = previousObservation;
      globalThis.fetch = previousFetch;
      vi.unstubAllEnvs();
    }
  });

  it.each([
    { label: 'object-shaped 200', body: JSON.stringify({ instances: [] }) },
    { label: 'invalid JSON 200', body: '{' },
  ])('keeps retained-renewal cleanup pending after $label from the actual Evolution client', async ({ body: malformedBody }) => {
    await contratarCanalNaoOficial(prisma, tenant.mantenedora.id);
    const { channel } = await seedRenewalOwner();
    evolutionFake.createdState = 'connecting';
    evolutionFake.ownerPhone = null;
    const baseUrl = 'https://evolution.synthetic.invalid';
    const oldName = channel.evolutionInstanceName!;
    const previousFetch = globalThis.fetch;
    const previousObservation = evolutionFake.consultarInstancia.bind(evolutionFake);
    const requestedNames: string[] = [];
    let observation = 0;
    const fetchStub = vi.fn(async (input: Parameters<typeof fetch>[0], init?: Parameters<typeof fetch>[1]): Promise<Response> => {
      const url = new URL(input instanceof Request ? input.url : String(input));
      const isExpected = url.origin === baseUrl && url.pathname === '/instance/fetchInstances'
        && (init?.method ?? 'GET') === 'GET' && url.searchParams.has('instanceName');
      if (!isExpected) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ message: 'unexpected synthetic request' }), { status: 400 });
      const name = url.searchParams.get('instanceName')!;
      requestedNames.push(name);
      observation += 1;
      if (observation === 1) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ message: 'synthetic initial timeout' }), { status: 503 });
      if (observation === 2) return new Response(malformedBody, { status: 200, headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' } });
      // The subsequent explicit reconciliation observes a real, valid empty inventory.
      if (name === oldName) {
        evolutionFake.inventory.delete(name);
        return new Response('[]', { status: 200, headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' } });
      }
      const observed = evolutionFake.inventory.get(name);
      return new Response(JSON.stringify(observed ? [observed] : []), { status: 200, headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' } });
    });
    vi.stubEnv('EVOLUTION_API_URL', baseUrl);
    vi.stubEnv('EVOLUTION_API_KEY', 'synthetic-evolution-key');
    globalThis.fetch = fetchStub;
    try {
      const actualEvolution = new EvolutionHttpClient();
      evolutionFake.consultarInstancia = (name) => actualEvolution.consultarInstancia(name);
      const createsBefore = evolutionFake.createCalls;
      const first = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/nao-oficial/pair`)
        .send({ intent: 'RENEW', expectedGeneration: channel.generation });
      const reserved = await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: channel.id } });
      expect(first.body.state).toBe('CLEANUP_AMBIGUOUS');
      expect(reserved).toMatchObject({
        generation: expect.any(Number), pairingEpoch: expect.any(Number), currentAttemptId: expect.any(String),
        pendingCleanupInstanceName: oldName, pendingCleanupIdentityId: expect.any(String),
      });
      const tuple = { generation: reserved.generation, pairingEpoch: reserved.pairingEpoch, currentAttemptId: reserved.currentAttemptId, evolutionInstanceName: reserved.evolutionInstanceName };
      const attemptId = reserved.currentAttemptId!;
      const cleanupId = reserved.pendingCleanupIdentityId!;
      const beforeBadObservationCreates = evolutionFake.createCalls;
      const beforeBadObservationProviderCalls = evolutionFake.providerCalls;
      const deletion = vi.spyOn(evolutionFake, 'apagarInstancia');
      let malformed: request.Response;
      let deletionCalls = -1;
      try {
        malformed = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/nao-oficial/pair`)
          .send({ intent: 'INITIAL', expectedGeneration: reserved.generation });
        deletionCalls = deletion.mock.calls.length;
      } finally {
        deletion.mockRestore();
      }

      // Check persisted safety before checking the response: the old client mistakes malformed JSON for absence.
      expect(await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: cleanupId } }))
        .toMatchObject({ externalResourceId: oldName, cleanupState: 'CLEANUP_PENDING', currentChannelId: null });
      expect(await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: channel.id } }))
        .toMatchObject({ ...tuple, pendingCleanupInstanceName: oldName, pendingCleanupIdentityId: cleanupId });
      expect(await prisma.channelRegistrationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: attemptId } }))
        .toMatchObject({ state: 'ERROR', stage: 'CLEANUP_AMBIGUOUS', safeErrorCode: 'CLEANUP_AMBIGUOUS' });
      expect(evolutionFake.createCalls).toBe(beforeBadObservationCreates);
      expect(evolutionFake.providerCalls).toBe(beforeBadObservationProviderCalls);
      expect(deletionCalls).toBe(0);
      expect(evolutionFake.inventory.get(oldName)).toEqual({ name: oldName, ownerJid: '5584999990000@s.whatsapp.net', connectionStatus: 'open' });
      expect(malformed.status).toBe(409);
      expect(malformed.body.code).toBe('CLEANUP_AMBIGUOUS');

      const reconciled = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/nao-oficial/pair`)
        .send({ intent: 'INITIAL', expectedGeneration: reserved.generation });
      expect(reconciled.status).toBe(409);
      expect(reconciled.body.code).toBe('CLEANUP_COMPLETED_RETRY_PAIR');
      expect(await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: cleanupId } }))
        .toMatchObject({ cleanupState: 'CLEANED', cleanupLeaseToken: null, cleanupLeaseUntil: null });
      expect(await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: channel.id } }))
        .toMatchObject({ ...tuple, pendingCleanupInstanceName: oldName, pendingCleanupIdentityId: cleanupId });
      const prepared = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/nao-oficial/pair`)
        .send({ intent: 'INITIAL', expectedGeneration: reserved.generation });
      expect(prepared.status, JSON.stringify(prepared.body)).toBe(200);
      expect(prepared.body.state).toBe('CLEANUP_COMPLETED_RETRY_PAIR');
      expect(await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: channel.id } }))
        .toMatchObject({ ...tuple, pendingCleanupInstanceName: null, pendingCleanupIdentityId: null });
      expect(await prisma.channelRegistrationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: attemptId } }))
        .toMatchObject({ state: 'PENDING_NUMBER', stage: 'RESERVED_NEVER_CREATED', safeErrorCode: null });
      const created = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/nao-oficial/pair`)
        .send({ intent: 'INITIAL', expectedGeneration: reserved.generation });
      expect(created.status, JSON.stringify(created.body)).toBe(200);
      expect(created.body.state).toBe('PENDING_IDENTITY');
      expect(evolutionFake.createCalls).toBe(createsBefore + 1);
      const afterCreate = await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: channel.id } });
      const replacement = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({
        where: { provider_externalResourceId: { provider: 'NAO_OFICIAL', externalResourceId: afterCreate.evolutionInstanceName! } },
      });
      expect(afterCreate).toMatchObject({ ...tuple, pendingCleanupInstanceName: null, pendingCleanupIdentityId: null, ownNumberVerified: false });
      expect(replacement).toMatchObject({ currentChannelId: null, unverifiedStaging: true, businessIdentityId: null });
      expect(requestedNames).toEqual([oldName, oldName, oldName, oldName, afterCreate.evolutionInstanceName]);
    } finally {
      await prisma.buChannel.updateMany({ where: { id: channel.id }, data: { pendingCleanupInstanceName: null, pendingCleanupIdentityId: null } });
      evolutionFake.consultarInstancia = previousObservation;
      globalThis.fetch = previousFetch;
      vi.unstubAllEnvs();
    }
  });

  it.each([
    { label: 'object-shaped 200', body: JSON.stringify({ instances: [] }) },
    { label: 'invalid JSON 200', body: '{' },
  ])('keeps detached post-unlink cleanup pending after $label from the actual Evolution client', async ({ body: malformedBody }) => {
    await contratarCanalNaoOficial(prisma, tenant.mantenedora.id);
    const { channel } = await seedRenewalOwner();
    evolutionFake.createdState = 'connecting';
    evolutionFake.ownerPhone = null;
    const renewed = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/nao-oficial/pair`)
      .send({ intent: 'RENEW', expectedGeneration: channel.generation }).expect(200);
    evolutionFake.createHook = async () => { throw new Error('synthetic ambiguous create acknowledgement'); };
    const stagedResponse = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/nao-oficial/pair`)
      .send({ intent: 'INITIAL', expectedGeneration: renewed.body.generation }).expect(200);
    evolutionFake.createHook = null;
    expect(stagedResponse.body.state).toBe('PENDING_IDENTITY');
    const stagedChannel = await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: channel.id } });
    const stagedAttemptId = stagedChannel.currentAttemptId!;
    const stagedName = stagedChannel.evolutionInstanceName!;
    const staged = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({
      where: { provider_externalResourceId: { provider: 'NAO_OFICIAL', externalResourceId: stagedName } },
    });
    evolutionFake.deleteFails = true;
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).delete(`/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/NAO_OFICIAL`)
      .set('Cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').expect(204);
    evolutionFake.deleteFails = false;
    await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.update({ where: { id: staged.id }, data: { cleanupLeaseUntil: new Date(Date.now() - 1_000) } });

    const baseUrl = 'https://evolution.synthetic.invalid';
    const previousFetch = globalThis.fetch;
    const previousObservation = evolutionFake.consultarInstancia.bind(evolutionFake);
    const requestedNames: string[] = [];
    let malformedSeen = false;
    const fetchStub = vi.fn(async (input: Parameters<typeof fetch>[0], init?: Parameters<typeof fetch>[1]): Promise<Response> => {
      const url = new URL(input instanceof Request ? input.url : String(input));
      const isExpected = url.origin === baseUrl && url.pathname === '/instance/fetchInstances'
        && (init?.method ?? 'GET') === 'GET' && url.searchParams.has('instanceName');
      if (!isExpected) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ message: 'unexpected synthetic request' }), { status: 400 });
      const name = url.searchParams.get('instanceName')!;
      requestedNames.push(name);
      if (!malformedSeen) {
        malformedSeen = true;
        return new Response(malformedBody, { status: 200, headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' } });
      }
      // A valid empty inventory is the later conclusive absence for this detached resource.
      if (name === stagedName) {
        evolutionFake.inventory.delete(name);
        return new Response('[]', { status: 200, headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' } });
      }
      const observed = evolutionFake.inventory.get(name);
      return new Response(JSON.stringify(observed ? [observed] : []), { status: 200, headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' } });
    });
    vi.stubEnv('EVOLUTION_API_URL', baseUrl);
    vi.stubEnv('EVOLUTION_API_KEY', 'synthetic-evolution-key');
    globalThis.fetch = fetchStub;
    try {
      const actualEvolution = new EvolutionHttpClient();
      evolutionFake.consultarInstancia = (name) => actualEvolution.consultarInstancia(name);
      const createsBefore = evolutionFake.createCalls;
      const providerCallsBeforeMalformed = evolutionFake.providerCalls;
      const deletion = vi.spyOn(evolutionFake, 'apagarInstancia');
      let malformed: request.Response;
      let deletionCalls = -1;
      try {
        malformed = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/nao-oficial/pair`)
          .send({ intent: 'INITIAL' });
        deletionCalls = deletion.mock.calls.length;
      } finally {
        deletion.mockRestore();
      }
      // Verify the detached provider owner and cancelled attempt before response assertions.
      expect(await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: staged.id } }))
        .toMatchObject({ externalResourceId: stagedName, currentChannelId: null, unverifiedStaging: true, cleanupState: 'CLEANUP_PENDING' });
      expect(await prisma.channelRegistrationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: stagedAttemptId } }))
        .toMatchObject({ state: 'CANCELLED' });
      expect(await prisma.buChannel.findUnique({ where: { id: channel.id } })).toBeNull();
      expect(evolutionFake.createCalls).toBe(createsBefore);
      expect(evolutionFake.providerCalls).toBe(providerCallsBeforeMalformed);
      expect(deletionCalls).toBe(0);
      expect(evolutionFake.inventory.get(stagedName)).toEqual({ name: stagedName, ownerJid: null, connectionStatus: 'connecting' });
      expect(malformed.status).toBe(409);
      expect(malformed.body.code).toBe('CLEANUP_AMBIGUOUS');

      const reconciled = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/nao-oficial/pair`).send({ intent: 'INITIAL' });
      expect(reconciled.status).toBe(409);
      expect(reconciled.body.code).toBe('CLEANUP_COMPLETED_RETRY_PAIR');
      expect(await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: staged.id } }))
        .toMatchObject({ cleanupState: 'CLEANED', currentChannelId: null });
      expect(await prisma.channelRegistrationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: stagedAttemptId } }))
        .toMatchObject({ state: 'CANCELLED' });
      expect(evolutionFake.createCalls).toBe(createsBefore);

      const newPairing = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/nao-oficial/pair`).send({ intent: 'INITIAL' });
      expect(newPairing.status, JSON.stringify(newPairing.body)).toBe(200);
      expect(newPairing.body.state).toBe('PENDING_IDENTITY');
      expect(evolutionFake.createCalls).toBe(createsBefore + 1);
      const newChannel = await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: newPairing.body.channelId as string } });
      expect(newChannel.id).not.toBe(channel.id);
      expect(newChannel.currentAttemptId).not.toBe(stagedAttemptId);
      expect(await prisma.channelRegistrationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: stagedAttemptId } })).toMatchObject({ state: 'CANCELLED' });
      const newProvider = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({
        where: { provider_externalResourceId: { provider: 'NAO_OFICIAL', externalResourceId: newChannel.evolutionInstanceName! } },
      });
      expect(newChannel).toMatchObject({ ownNumberVerified: false, businessIdentityId: null });
      expect(newProvider).toMatchObject({ currentChannelId: newChannel.id, unverifiedStaging: false, businessIdentityId: null });
      const messageCountBefore = await prisma.message.count({ where: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id } });
      const conversationCountBefore = await prisma.conversation.count({ where: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id } });
      const botStartsBefore = typebotFake.starts;
      const publish = vi.spyOn(app.get(RealtimeService), 'publicarNaBu');
      try {
        const ingress = await webhook(newChannel.evolutionInstanceName!, 'unlinked-recovery-unproved-ingress');
        expect(ingress.status).toBe(503);
        expect(ingress.body.code).toBe('PENDING_IDENTITY');
        expect(await prisma.message.count({ where: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id } })).toBe(messageCountBefore);
        expect(await prisma.conversation.count({ where: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id } })).toBe(conversationCountBefore);
        expect(typebotFake.starts).toBe(botStartsBefore);
        expect(publish).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      } finally {
        publish.mockRestore();
      }
      expect(requestedNames).toEqual([stagedName, stagedName, newChannel.evolutionInstanceName]);
    } finally {
      evolutionFake.createHook = null;
      evolutionFake.deleteFails = false;
      evolutionFake.consultarInstancia = previousObservation;
      globalThis.fetch = previousFetch;
      vi.unstubAllEnvs();
    }
  });

  it('does not let a late cleanup failure overwrite a reservation recovered by the next lease owner', async () => {
    await contratarCanalNaoOficial(prisma, tenant.mantenedora.id);
    const { channel } = await seedRenewalOwner();
    evolutionFake.createdState = 'connecting';
    evolutionFake.ownerPhone = null;

    const baseUrl = 'https://evolution.synthetic.invalid';
    const oldInstanceName = channel.evolutionInstanceName!;
    const previousFetch = globalThis.fetch;
    const previousObservation = evolutionFake.consultarInstancia.bind(evolutionFake);
    const requestedInstances: string[] = [];
    const responseStatuses: number[] = [];
    let releaseLateObservation: (() => void) | undefined;
    let lateObservationEntered: (() => void) | undefined;
    const lateObservation = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { lateObservationEntered = resolve; });
    const release = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { releaseLateObservation = resolve; });
    let lateRequestPromise: Promise<request.Response> | undefined;
    const fetchStub = vi.fn(async (input: Parameters<typeof fetch>[0], init?: Parameters<typeof fetch>[1]): Promise<Response> => {
      const url = new URL(input instanceof Request ? input.url : String(input));
      const instanceName = url.searchParams.get('instanceName') ?? '';
      requestedInstances.push(instanceName);
      const isExpectedObservation = url.origin === baseUrl
        && url.pathname === '/instance/fetchInstances'
        && url.searchParams.has('instanceName')
        && (init?.method ?? 'GET') === 'GET';
      const sequence = responseStatuses.length;
      const status = !isExpectedObservation ? 400 : sequence === 0 ? 503 : 200;
      responseStatuses.push(status);
      if (status === 200 && instanceName === oldInstanceName) evolutionFake.inventory.delete(oldInstanceName);
      if (sequence === 2 && instanceName === oldInstanceName) {
        lateObservationEntered?.();
        await release;
      }
      const observed = evolutionFake.inventory.get(instanceName);
      const body = status === 503
        ? JSON.stringify({ message: 'synthetic provider unavailable' })
        : JSON.stringify(observed ? [{ name: observed.name, ownerJid: observed.ownerJid, connectionStatus: observed.connectionStatus }] : []);
      return new Response(body, { status, headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' } });
    });

    vi.stubEnv('EVOLUTION_API_URL', baseUrl);
    vi.stubEnv('EVOLUTION_API_KEY', 'synthetic-evolution-key');
    globalThis.fetch = fetchStub;
    try {
      const actualEvolution = new EvolutionHttpClient();
      evolutionFake.consultarInstancia = (name) => actualEvolution.consultarInstancia(name);

      const initialCreates = evolutionFake.createCalls;
      const renewed = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/nao-oficial/pair`)
        .send({ intent: 'RENEW', expectedGeneration: channel.generation }).expect(200);
      expect(renewed.body.state).toBe('CLEANUP_AMBIGUOUS');
      const ambiguous = await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: channel.id } });
      const tuple = {
        generation: ambiguous.generation,
        pairingEpoch: ambiguous.pairingEpoch,
        currentAttemptId: ambiguous.currentAttemptId,
        evolutionInstanceName: ambiguous.evolutionInstanceName,
      };
      const attemptFence = {
        id: tuple.currentAttemptId!, channelId: channel.id, buId: tenant.businessUnit.id,
        type: 'NAO_OFICIAL', generation: tuple.generation, pairingEpoch: tuple.pairingEpoch,
      };
      const priorIdentityId = ambiguous.pendingCleanupIdentityId!;
      expect(ambiguous).toMatchObject({ ...tuple, pendingCleanupInstanceName: oldInstanceName, pendingCleanupIdentityId: priorIdentityId });
      expect(await prisma.channelRegistrationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: tuple.currentAttemptId! } }))
        .toMatchObject({ ...attemptFence, state: 'ERROR', stage: 'CLEANUP_AMBIGUOUS', safeErrorCode: 'CLEANUP_AMBIGUOUS' });
      expect(await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: priorIdentityId } }))
        .toMatchObject({ buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, provider: 'NAO_OFICIAL', externalResourceId: oldInstanceName, currentChannelId: null, cleanupState: 'CLEANUP_PENDING' });

      const reconciled = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/nao-oficial/pair`)
        .send({ intent: 'INITIAL', expectedGeneration: tuple.generation });
      expect(reconciled.status).toBe(409);
      expect(reconciled.body.code).toBe('CLEANUP_COMPLETED_RETRY_PAIR');
      const cleanedPrior = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: priorIdentityId } });
      expect(cleanedPrior).toMatchObject({ cleanupState: 'CLEANED', cleanupLeaseToken: null, cleanupLeaseUntil: null });
      const afterInitialCleanup = await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: channel.id } });
      expect(afterInitialCleanup).toMatchObject({ ...tuple, pendingCleanupInstanceName: oldInstanceName, pendingCleanupIdentityId: priorIdentityId });
      expect(evolutionFake.createCalls).toBe(initialCreates);

      lateRequestPromise = bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/nao-oficial/pair`)
        .send({ intent: 'INITIAL', expectedGeneration: tuple.generation }).then((response) => response);
      await lateObservation;
      const ownerA = await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: channel.id } });
      expect(ownerA).toMatchObject({ ...tuple, operationLeaseToken: expect.any(String) });
      expect(ownerA.operationLeaseUntil!.getTime()).toBeGreaterThan(Date.now());
      const expired = await prisma.buChannel.updateMany({
        where: {
          id: channel.id, generation: tuple.generation, pairingEpoch: tuple.pairingEpoch,
          currentAttemptId: tuple.currentAttemptId, operationLeaseToken: ownerA.operationLeaseToken,
          operationLeaseUntil: { gt: new Date() },
        },
        data: { operationLeaseUntil: new Date(Date.now() - 1_000) },
      });
      expect(expired.count).toBe(1);

      const ownerBResponse = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/nao-oficial/pair`)
        .send({ intent: 'INITIAL', expectedGeneration: tuple.generation });
      expect(ownerBResponse.status, JSON.stringify(ownerBResponse.body)).toBe(200);
      expect(ownerBResponse.body.state).toBe('CLEANUP_COMPLETED_RETRY_PAIR');
      const ownerBChannel = await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: channel.id } });
      const ownerBAttempt = await prisma.channelRegistrationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: tuple.currentAttemptId! } });
      const ownerBPrior = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: priorIdentityId } });
      expect(ownerBChannel).toMatchObject({ ...tuple, pendingCleanupInstanceName: null, pendingCleanupIdentityId: null, operationLeaseToken: null, operationLeaseUntil: null });
      expect(ownerBAttempt).toMatchObject({ ...attemptFence, state: 'PENDING_NUMBER', stage: 'RESERVED_NEVER_CREATED', safeErrorCode: null });
      expect(ownerBPrior).toMatchObject({ cleanupState: 'CLEANED', cleanupLeaseToken: null, cleanupLeaseUntil: null });
      expect(evolutionFake.createCalls).toBe(initialCreates);

      releaseLateObservation?.();
      const lateResponse = await lateRequestPromise;
      const afterLateFailure = await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: channel.id } });
      const attemptAfterLateFailure = await prisma.channelRegistrationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: tuple.currentAttemptId! } });
      const priorAfterLateFailure = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: priorIdentityId } });
      expect(afterLateFailure).toMatchObject({ ...tuple, pendingCleanupInstanceName: null, pendingCleanupIdentityId: null, operationLeaseToken: null, operationLeaseUntil: null });
      expect(attemptAfterLateFailure).toEqual(ownerBAttempt);
      expect(priorAfterLateFailure).toEqual(ownerBPrior);
      expect(lateResponse?.status).toBe(409);
      expect(lateResponse?.body.code).toBe('CHANNEL_OPERATION_STALE');

      const created = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/nao-oficial/pair`)
        .send({ intent: 'INITIAL', expectedGeneration: tuple.generation });
      expect(created.status, JSON.stringify(created.body)).toBe(200);
      expect(created.body.state).toBe('PENDING_IDENTITY');
      expect(evolutionFake.createCalls).toBe(initialCreates + 1);
      const afterCreate = await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: channel.id } });
      expect(afterCreate).toMatchObject({ ...tuple, pendingCleanupInstanceName: null, pendingCleanupIdentityId: null, ownNumberVerified: false, verifiedEpoch: null });
      const replacement = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({
        where: { provider_externalResourceId: { provider: 'NAO_OFICIAL', externalResourceId: tuple.evolutionInstanceName! } },
      });
      expect(replacement).toMatchObject({ currentChannelId: null, unverifiedStaging: true, businessIdentityId: null, cleanupState: 'ACTIVE' });
      expect(requestedInstances).toEqual([oldInstanceName, oldInstanceName, oldInstanceName, oldInstanceName, tuple.evolutionInstanceName]);
      expect(responseStatuses).toEqual([503, 200, 200, 200, 200]);
      const publish = vi.spyOn(app.get(RealtimeService), 'publicarNaBu');
      try {
        const messageCountBefore = await prisma.message.count({ where: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id } });
        const conversationsBefore = await prisma.conversation.count({ where: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id } });
        const botStartsBefore = typebotFake.starts;
        const ingress = await webhook(tuple.evolutionInstanceName!, 'late-cleanup-unproved-ingress');
        expect(ingress.status).toBe(503);
        expect(ingress.body.code).toBe('PENDING_IDENTITY');
        expect(await prisma.message.count({ where: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id } })).toBe(messageCountBefore);
        expect(await prisma.conversation.count({ where: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id } })).toBe(conversationsBefore);
        expect(typebotFake.starts).toBe(botStartsBefore);
        expect(publish).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      } finally {
        publish.mockRestore();
      }
    } finally {
      releaseLateObservation?.();
      if (lateRequestPromise) await lateRequestPromise.catch(() => undefined);
      await prisma.buChannel.updateMany({ where: { id: channel.id }, data: { pendingCleanupInstanceName: null, pendingCleanupIdentityId: null } });
      evolutionFake.consultarInstancia = previousObservation;
      globalThis.fetch = previousFetch;
      vi.unstubAllEnvs();
    }
  });

  it('revalidates the real BFF session before persisting a failed cleanup observation', async () => {
    await contratarCanalNaoOficial(prisma, tenant.mantenedora.id);
    const { channel } = await seedRenewalOwner();
    const user = await prisma.appUser.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { mantenedoraId_email: { mantenedoraId: tenant.mantenedora.id, email: 'instagram-lifecycle@example.test' } }, select: { id: true, passwordChangedAt: true } });
    const priorPasswordChangedAt = user.passwordChangedAt;
    const baseUrl = 'https://evolution.synthetic.invalid';
    const previousFetch = globalThis.fetch;
    const previousObservation = evolutionFake.consultarInstancia.bind(evolutionFake);
    let releaseFailure: (() => void) | undefined;
    let providerEntered: (() => void) | undefined;
    const entered = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { providerEntered = resolve; });
    const failureGate = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { releaseFailure = resolve; });
    let requestPromise: Promise<request.Response> | undefined;
    const requestNames: string[] = [];
    const fetchStub = vi.fn(async (input: Parameters<typeof fetch>[0], init?: Parameters<typeof fetch>[1]): Promise<Response> => {
      const url = new URL(input instanceof Request ? input.url : String(input));
      requestNames.push(url.searchParams.get('instanceName') ?? '');
      const expected = url.origin === baseUrl && url.pathname === '/instance/fetchInstances'
        && url.searchParams.has('instanceName') && (init?.method ?? 'GET') === 'GET';
      if (!expected) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ message: 'unexpected synthetic request' }), { status: 400 });
      providerEntered?.();
      await failureGate;
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ message: 'synthetic provider unavailable' }), { status: 503, headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' } });
    });

    vi.stubEnv('EVOLUTION_API_URL', baseUrl);
    vi.stubEnv('EVOLUTION_API_KEY', 'synthetic-evolution-key');
    globalThis.fetch = fetchStub;
    try {
      const actualEvolution = new EvolutionHttpClient();
      evolutionFake.consultarInstancia = (name) => actualEvolution.consultarInstancia(name);
      requestPromise = bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/nao-oficial/pair`)
        .send({ intent: 'RENEW', expectedGeneration: channel.generation }).then((response) => response);
      await entered;
      const staged = await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: channel.id } });
      expect(staged).toMatchObject({ operationLeaseToken: expect.any(String), pendingCleanupInstanceName: channel.evolutionInstanceName, pendingCleanupIdentityId: expect.any(String) });
      await prisma.appUser.update({ where: { id: user.id }, data: { passwordChangedAt: new Date(Date.now() + 2_000) } });
      releaseFailure?.();
      const response = await requestPromise;
      const after = await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: channel.id } });
      expect(after).toMatchObject({
        generation: staged.generation, pairingEpoch: staged.pairingEpoch,
        currentAttemptId: staged.currentAttemptId, evolutionInstanceName: staged.evolutionInstanceName,
        pendingCleanupInstanceName: channel.evolutionInstanceName,
        pendingCleanupIdentityId: staged.pendingCleanupIdentityId,
        operationLeaseToken: null, operationLeaseUntil: null,
      });
      expect(await prisma.channelRegistrationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: staged.currentAttemptId! } }))
        .toMatchObject({ state: 'PENDING_NUMBER', stage: 'PAIRING', safeErrorCode: null });
      expect(await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: staged.pendingCleanupIdentityId! } }))
        .toMatchObject({ cleanupState: 'CLEANUP_PENDING', cleanupLeaseToken: null, cleanupLeaseUntil: null });
      expect(response.status).toBe(401);
      expect(response.body.code).toBe('NAO_AUTENTICADO');
      expect(requestNames).toEqual([channel.evolutionInstanceName]);
      expect(fetchStub).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    } finally {
      releaseFailure?.();
      if (requestPromise) await requestPromise.catch(() => undefined);
      await prisma.appUser.update({ where: { id: user.id }, data: { passwordChangedAt: priorPasswordChangedAt } });
      await prisma.buChannel.updateMany({ where: { id: channel.id }, data: { pendingCleanupInstanceName: null, pendingCleanupIdentityId: null } });
      evolutionFake.consultarInstancia = previousObservation;
      globalThis.fetch = previousFetch;
      vi.unstubAllEnvs();
    }
  });

  it('rejects a replacement RENEW instance that proves a different number and retains the old owner', async () => {

      expect(await prisma.message.count({ where: { externalId: 'trusted-owner-contradiction' } })).toBe(0);
      expect(await prisma.message.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: priorMessage.id } })).toMatchObject({ externalId: 'receipt-before-contradiction', content: 'accepted before quarantine', createdAt: priorMessage.createdAt });
      expect(await prisma.conversation.count({ where: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id } })).toBe(1);
      expect(typebotFake.starts).toBe(typebotStartsBeforeQuarantine);
      expect(publish).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      evolutionFake.inventory.set(channel.evolutionInstanceName!, { name: channel.evolutionInstanceName!, ownerJid: '5584999990000@s.whatsapp.net', connectionStatus: 'open' });
      await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/NAO_OFICIAL/verify`).send({}).expect(200);
      expect(await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: channel.id } })).toMatchObject({ status: 'CONECTADO', ownNumberVerified: true, receiptVerifiedAt: connected.receiptVerifiedAt, generation: connected.generation, pairingEpoch: connected.pairingEpoch, currentAttemptId: connected.currentAttemptId });
      expect(await prisma.channelRegistrationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: attempt.id } })).toMatchObject({ state: 'CONNECTED', stage: 'RECEIPT_VERIFIED' });
      await webhook(channel.evolutionInstanceName!, 'trusted-owner-contradiction').expect(200);
      await webhook(channel.evolutionInstanceName!, 'trusted-owner-contradiction').expect(200);
      expect(await prisma.message.count({ where: { externalId: 'trusted-owner-contradiction' } })).toBe(1);
      expect(await prisma.message.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: priorMessage.id } })).toMatchObject({ content: 'accepted before quarantine', createdAt: priorMessage.createdAt });
      expect(publish).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    } finally {
      publish.mockRestore();
    }
  });

  it('rejects an unlink whose real channel snapshot was replaced by a completed RENEW and staged replacement', async () => {
    await contratarCanalNaoOficial(prisma, tenant.mantenedora.id);
    const { channel } = await seedRenewalOwner();
    evolutionFake.createdState = 'connecting';
    evolutionFake.ownerPhone = null;

    let signalSnapshot!: () => void;
    let releaseSnapshot!: () => void;
    const snapshotRead = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { signalSnapshot = resolve; });
    const snapshotGate = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { releaseSnapshot = resolve; });
    const originalFindCompatible = businessResourceOwnership.findCompatibleWhatsAppChannel;
    let pauseNextUnlinkSnapshot = true;
    const snapshotSpy = vi.spyOn(businessResourceOwnership, 'findCompatibleWhatsAppChannel').mockImplementation(async (db, buId, adapter) => {
      const channelSnapshot = await originalFindCompatible(db, buId, adapter);
      if (pauseNextUnlinkSnapshot && db === prisma && buId === tenant.businessUnit.id && adapter === 'NAO_OFICIAL') {
        pauseNextUnlinkSnapshot = false;
        signalSnapshot();
        await snapshotGate;
      }
      return channelSnapshot;
    });
    let unlink: Promise<void> | undefined;
    let unlinkResponse: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof request>> | undefined;
    try {
      unlink = request(app.getHttpServer()).delete(`/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/NAO_OFICIAL`)
        .set('Cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste')
        .then((response) => { unlinkResponse = response; });
      await snapshotRead;

        .send({ intent: 'RENEW', expectedGeneration: channel.generation }).expect(200);
      evolutionFake.createHook = async () => { throw new Error('synthetic replacement create acknowledgement lost'); };
      const pending = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/nao-oficial/pair`)
        .send({ intent: 'INITIAL', expectedGeneration: renewed.body.generation }).expect(200);
      evolutionFake.createHook = null;
      expect(pending.body.state).toBe('PENDING_IDENTITY');
      const replacement = await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: channel.id } });
      const staged = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({
        where: { provider_externalResourceId: { provider: 'NAO_OFICIAL', externalResourceId: replacement.evolutionInstanceName! } },
      });
      const attempt = await prisma.channelRegistrationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: replacement.currentAttemptId! } });
      expect(replacement.generation).toBeGreaterThan(channel.generation);
      expect(replacement.evolutionInstanceName).not.toBe(channel.evolutionInstanceName);
      expect(staged).toMatchObject({ currentChannelId: null, unverifiedStaging: true, cleanupState: 'ACTIVE' });

      releaseSnapshot();
      await unlink;
      const after = await prisma.buChannel.findUnique({ where: { id: replacement.id } });
      const stagedAfter = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: staged.id } });
      const attemptAfter = await prisma.channelRegistrationAttempt.findUnique({ where: { id: attempt.id } });
      expect(unlinkResponse?.status).toBe(409);
      expect(after).toMatchObject({ id: replacement.id, generation: replacement.generation, pairingEpoch: replacement.pairingEpoch, currentAttemptId: replacement.currentAttemptId, evolutionInstanceName: replacement.evolutionInstanceName });
      expect(stagedAfter).toMatchObject({ currentChannelId: null, unverifiedStaging: true, cleanupState: 'ACTIVE' });
      expect(attemptAfter).toMatchObject({ id: attempt.id, state: attempt.state, stage: attempt.stage, generation: attempt.generation, pairingEpoch: attempt.pairingEpoch });

      evolutionFake.deleteFails = true;
      await request(app.getHttpServer()).delete(`/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/NAO_OFICIAL`)
        .set('Cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').expect(204);
      expect(await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: staged.id } }))
        .toMatchObject({ currentChannelId: null, unverifiedStaging: true, cleanupState: 'CLEANUP_PENDING' });
    } finally {
      releaseSnapshot();
      snapshotSpy.mockRestore();
      evolutionFake.createHook = null;
      if (unlink) await unlink.catch(() => undefined);
      evolutionFake.deleteFails = false;
    }
  }, 20_000);

    const after = await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: channel.id } });
    const attemptAfter = await prisma.channelRegistrationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: after.currentAttemptId! } });
    expect(observed.body.state).toBe('PENDING_IDENTITY');
    expect(evolutionFake.createCalls).toBe(creates);
    expect(after).toMatchObject({ id: before.id, generation: before.generation, pairingEpoch: before.pairingEpoch, currentAttemptId: before.currentAttemptId, evolutionInstanceName: before.evolutionInstanceName });
    expect(attemptAfter).toMatchObject({ id: attemptBefore.id, generation: attemptBefore.generation, pairingEpoch: attemptBefore.pairingEpoch, stage: 'PAIRING' });
  });

    const busy = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/nao-oficial/pair`).send({ intent: 'INITIAL' }).expect(409);
    expect(busy.body.code).toBe('CLEANUP_RECOVERY_BUSY');
    expect(evolutionFake.createCalls).toBe(createCount);
    await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.update({ where: { id: staged.id }, data: { cleanupLeaseUntil: new Date(Date.now() - 1_000) } });
    const ambiguous = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/nao-oficial/pair`).send({ intent: 'INITIAL' }).expect(409);
    expect(ambiguous.body.code).toBe('CLEANUP_AMBIGUOUS');
    expect(evolutionFake.createCalls).toBe(createCount);
    evolutionFake.deleteFails = false;
    const reconciled = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/nao-oficial/pair`).send({ intent: 'INITIAL' }).expect(409);
    expect(reconciled.body.code).toBe('CLEANUP_COMPLETED_RETRY_PAIR');
    expect(evolutionFake.createCalls).toBe(createCount);

    expect(await prisma.webhookEvent.count({ where: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id } })).toBe(1);
    expect(await prisma.message.count({ where: { providerResourceIdentityId: provider.id } })).toBe(0);
    expect(await prisma.conversation.count({ where: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id } })).toBe(0);
    const metadata = await prisma.webhookEvent.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, eventType: 'messages.upsert' } });
    expect(JSON.stringify(metadata.payload)).not.toContain('must remain retryable');

    expect(await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: provider.id } })).toMatchObject({ currentChannelId: channel.id, unverifiedStaging: false });
    await webhook(name, 'same-key-after-proof', 'accepted once').expect(200);
    await webhook(name, 'same-key-after-proof', 'accepted once').expect(200);
    expect(await prisma.message.count({ where: { providerResourceIdentityId: provider.id, externalId: 'same-key-after-proof' } })).toBe(1);
```
