```text
import request from 'supertest';
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { INestApplication } from '@nestjs/common';
import { PrismaService } from '../src/infra/prisma/prisma.service';
import { criarMantenedora, instagramFake, limparBanco, login, subirApp } from './helpers';

const DATABASE_GUARD = 'leadshug_timeline_channels_ci';
const TABLES = {
  appUsers: 'app_users',
  appUsersHidden: 'app_users_oauth_fault_hidden',
  settings: 'mantenedora_settings',
  settingsHidden: 'mantenedora_settings_oauth_fault_hidden',
} as const;

describe('Instagram OAuth session infrastructure faults (Nest/PostgreSQL)', () => {
  let app: INestApplication;
  let prisma: PrismaService;

  beforeAll(async () => {
    ({ app, prisma } = await subirApp(undefined, true));
    await app.listen(0, '127.0.0.1');
    await limparBanco(prisma);
  });

  afterAll(async () => {
    try {
      if (prisma) await limparBanco(prisma);
    } finally {
      await app?.close();
    }
  });

  async function assertCanonicalDatabaseAndTables() {
    const database = await prisma.$queryRaw<Array<{ database: string }>>`SELECT current_database() AS database`;
    expect(database[0]?.database).toBe(DATABASE_GUARD);
    const rows = await prisma.$queryRaw<Array<{ table_name: string }>>`
      SELECT table_name FROM information_schema.tables
      WHERE table_schema = current_schema()
        AND table_name IN (${TABLES.appUsers}, ${TABLES.appUsersHidden}, ${TABLES.settings}, ${TABLES.settingsHidden})`;
    const names = new Set(rows.map((row) => row.table_name));
    expect(names.has(TABLES.appUsers)).toBe(true);
    expect(names.has(TABLES.settings)).toBe(true);
    expect(names.has(TABLES.appUsersHidden)).toBe(false);
    expect(names.has(TABLES.settingsHidden)).toBe(false);
  }

  async function renameTable(original: string, hidden: string) {
    await assertCanonicalDatabaseAndTables();
    await prisma.$transaction(async (tx) => {
      await tx.$executeRaw`SET LOCAL lock_timeout = '2s'`;
      await tx.$executeRawUnsafe(`ALTER TABLE ${original} RENAME TO ${hidden}`);
    }, { timeout: 5_000, maxWait: 5_000 });
  }

  async function restoreTable(original: string, hidden: string) {
    const database = await prisma.$queryRaw<Array<{ database: string }>>`SELECT current_database() AS database`;
    expect(database[0]?.database).toBe(DATABASE_GUARD);
    const rows = await prisma.$queryRaw<Array<{ table_name: string }>>`
      SELECT table_name FROM information_schema.tables
      WHERE table_schema = current_schema() AND table_name IN (${original}, ${hidden})`;
    const names = new Set(rows.map((row) => row.table_name));
    if (names.has(hidden) && !names.has(original)) {
      await prisma.$transaction(async (tx) => {
        await tx.$executeRaw`SET LOCAL lock_timeout = '2s'`;
        await tx.$executeRawUnsafe(`ALTER TABLE ${hidden} RENAME TO ${original}`);
      }, { timeout: 5_000, maxWait: 5_000 });
    }
    await assertCanonicalDatabaseAndTables();
  }

  async function expectInfrastructureCallbackFailure(cookie: string, attempts: number, credentials: number, providerCalls: number) {
    const callback = await request(app.getHttpServer()).get('/api/bff/instagram-authorizations/callback')
      .set('cookie', cookie).query({ code: 'fault-private-code', state: 'fault-private-state' });
    expect(callback.status).toBe(303);
    expect(callback.headers.location).toBe('/numeros?instagramAuthorizationError=INSTAGRAM_AUTHORIZATION_UNAVAILABLE');
    expect(callback.headers.location).not.toContain('fault-private-code');
    expect(callback.headers.location).not.toContain('fault-private-state');
    expect(callback.text).not.toContain('fault-private-code');
    expect(callback.text).not.toContain('fault-private-state');
    expect(callback.headers['cache-control']).toMatch(/no-store/i);
    expect(callback.headers['referrer-policy']).toBe('no-referrer');
    expect(await prisma.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.count()).toBe(attempts);
    expect(await prisma.channelCredential.count()).toBe(credentials);
    expect(instagramFake.calls).toHaveLength(providerCalls);
    return callback;
  }

  it('maps an unexpected app_users lookup failure to the generic callback redirect and restores normal session access', async () => {
    await assertCanonicalDatabaseAndTables();
    const tenant = await criarMantenedora(app, 'OAuth app-user fault control', 'oauth-app-user-fault@example.test');
    const cookie = await login(app, 'oauth-app-user-fault@example.test');
    const attempts = await prisma.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.count();
    const credentials = await prisma.channelCredential.count();
    const providerCalls = instagramFake.calls.length;
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).get('/api/bff/me').set('cookie', cookie).expect(200);
    try {
      await renameTable(TABLES.appUsers, TABLES.appUsersHidden);
      await expectInfrastructureCallbackFailure(cookie, attempts, credentials, providerCalls);
      const ordinary = await request(app.getHttpServer()).get('/api/bff/me').set('cookie', cookie)
        .query({ code: 'ordinary-private-code', state: 'ordinary-private-state' });
      expect(ordinary.status).toBe(503);
      expect(ordinary.body.code).toBe('BANCO_DESATUALIZADO');
      expect(ordinary.headers.location).toBeUndefined();
    } finally {
      await restoreTable(TABLES.appUsers, TABLES.appUsersHidden);
    }
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).get('/api/bff/me').set('cookie', cookie).expect(200);
    expect(tenant.owner.id).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it('maps an unexpected mantenedora_settings lookup failure to the generic callback redirect and preserves the ordinary error path', async () => {
    await assertCanonicalDatabaseAndTables();
    await criarMantenedora(app, 'OAuth settings fault control', 'oauth-settings-fault@example.test');
    const cookie = await login(app, 'oauth-settings-fault@example.test');
    const attempts = await prisma.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.count();
    const credentials = await prisma.channelCredential.count();
    const providerCalls = instagramFake.calls.length;
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).get('/api/bff/setores').set('cookie', cookie).expect(200);
    try {
      await renameTable(TABLES.settings, TABLES.settingsHidden);
      await expectInfrastructureCallbackFailure(cookie, attempts, credentials, providerCalls);
      const ordinary = await request(app.getHttpServer()).get('/api/bff/setores').set('cookie', cookie)
        .query({ code: 'ordinary-private-code', state: 'ordinary-private-state' });
      expect(ordinary.status).toBe(503);
      expect(ordinary.body.code).toBe('BANCO_DESATUALIZADO');
      expect(ordinary.headers.location).toBeUndefined();
    } finally {
      await restoreTable(TABLES.settings, TABLES.settingsHidden);
    }
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).get('/api/bff/me').set('cookie', cookie).expect(200);
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).get('/api/bff/setores').set('cookie', cookie).expect(200);
  });
});
```

### api-app/test/instagram-oauth-session.e2e-spec.ts — 8096 bytes SHA256 99f980ee88a4665c0c8f1ef9ac522dac3ce8ecc6054a1f8df5dc482c7a1bd761

```text

import request from 'supertest';
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { INestApplication } from '@nestjs/common';
import { PrismaService } from '../src/infra/prisma/prisma.service';
import { criarMantenedora, criarMantenedoraPendente, instagramFake, limparBanco, login, subirApp } from './helpers';

describe('Instagram OAuth callback session confidentiality (Nest/PostgreSQL)', () => {
  let app: INestApplication;
  let prisma: PrismaService;

  beforeAll(async () => {
    ({ app, prisma } = await subirApp());
    await app.listen(0, '127.0.0.1');
    await limparBanco(prisma);
  });

  afterAll(async () => {
    if (prisma) await limparBanco(prisma);
    await app?.close();
  });

  const callbackPaths = [
    '/api/bff/instagram-authorizations/callback',
    '/API/BFF/INSTAGRAM-AUTHORIZATIONS/CALLBACK',
    '/api/bff/instagram-authorizations/callback/',
  ];

  async function expectSafeCallback(path: string, expectedCode: 'NAO_AUTENTICADO' | 'TROCA_DE_SENHA_OBRIGATORIA' | 'ONBOARDING_PENDENTE', cookie?: string) {
    const beforeAttempts = await prisma.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.count();
    const beforeProvider = instagramFake.calls.length;
    const response = await request(app.getHttpServer()).get(path).query({ code: 'private-code-value', state: 'private-state-value' })
      .set(cookie ? 'cookie' : 'x-test-no-cookie', cookie ?? '');
    expect(response.status).toBe(303);
    expect(response.headers['cache-control']).toMatch(/no-store/i);
    expect(response.headers['referrer-policy']).toBe('no-referrer');
    expect(response.headers.location).toBe(`/numeros?instagramAuthorizationError=${expectedCode}`);
    expect(response.headers.location).not.toContain('private-code-value');
    expect(response.headers.location).not.toContain('private-state-value');
    expect(response.text).not.toContain('private-code-value');
    expect(response.text).not.toContain('private-state-value');
    expect(await prisma.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.count()).toBe(beforeAttempts);
    expect(instagramFake.calls).toHaveLength(beforeProvider);
    return response;
  }

  it('redirects absent, invalid, inactive, password-changed, must-change-password and pending-onboarding sessions before controller effects', async () => {
    const active = await criarMantenedora(app, 'OAuth callback active baseline', 'oauth-session-active@example.test');
    const activeCookie = await login(app, 'oauth-session-active@example.test');
    await expectSafeCallback(callbackPaths[0], 'NAO_AUTENTICADO');
    await expectSafeCallback(callbackPaths[0], 'NAO_AUTENTICADO', 'lh_session=not-a-valid-signed-session');

    const inactiveCookie = await login(app, 'oauth-session-active@example.test');
    await prisma.appUser.update({ where: { id: active.owner.id }, data: { status: 'INATIVO' } });
    await expectSafeCallback(callbackPaths[0], 'NAO_AUTENTICADO', inactiveCookie);
    await prisma.appUser.update({ where: { id: active.owner.id }, data: { status: 'ATIVO' } });

    const passwordChangedCookie = await login(app, 'oauth-session-active@example.test');
    await prisma.appUser.update({ where: { id: active.owner.id }, data: { passwordChangedAt: new Date(Date.now() + 2_000) } });
    await expectSafeCallback(callbackPaths[0], 'NAO_AUTENTICADO', passwordChangedCookie);

    const mustChange = await criarMantenedoraPendente(app, 'OAuth callback must change password', 'oauth-session-must-change@example.test');
    await prisma.appUser.update({ where: { id: mustChange.owner.id }, data: { mustChangePassword: true } });
    const mustChangeCookie = await login(app, 'oauth-session-must-change@example.test');
    await expectSafeCallback(callbackPaths[0], 'TROCA_DE_SENHA_OBRIGATORIA', mustChangeCookie);
    const mustChangeControl = await request(app.getHttpServer()).get('/api/bff/onboarding').set('cookie', mustChangeCookie)
      .query({ code: 'ordinary-query-code', state: 'ordinary-query-state' });
    expect(mustChangeControl.status).toBe(403);
    expect(mustChangeControl.body.code).toBe('TROCA_DE_SENHA_OBRIGATORIA');
    expect(mustChangeControl.headers.location).toBeUndefined();

    await criarMantenedoraPendente(app, 'OAuth callback pending onboarding', 'oauth-session-pending@example.test');
    const pendingCookie = await login(app, 'oauth-session-pending@example.test');
    await expectSafeCallback(callbackPaths[0], 'ONBOARDING_PENDENTE', pendingCookie);
    const onboardingControl = await request(app.getHttpServer()).get('/api/bff/setores').set('cookie', pendingCookie)
      .query({ code: 'ordinary-query-code', state: 'ordinary-query-state' });
    expect(onboardingControl.status).toBe(409);
    expect(onboardingControl.body.code).toBe('ONBOARDING_PENDENTE');
    expect(onboardingControl.headers.location).toBeUndefined();
    expect(activeCookie).toMatch(/^lh_session=/);
  });

  it('applies the same precontroller protection to callback case and trailing-slash aliases', async () => {
    await expectSafeCallback(callbackPaths[1], 'NAO_AUTENTICADO');
    await expectSafeCallback(callbackPaths[2], 'NAO_AUTENTICADO');
  });

  it('keeps noncallback authentication errors and query handling in the original JSON path', async () => {
    const beforeProvider = instagramFake.calls.length;
    const denied = await request(app.getHttpServer()).get('/api/bff/me').query({ code: 'query-code', state: 'query-state' });
    expect(denied.status).toBe(401);
    expect(denied.body).toMatchObject({ code: 'NAO_AUTENTICADO' });
    expect(denied.headers.location).toBeUndefined();
    expect(String(denied.headers['cache-control'] ?? '')).not.toMatch(/no-store/i);

    const tenant = await criarMantenedora(app, 'OAuth callback valid control', 'oauth-session-control@example.test');
    const cookie = await login(app, 'oauth-session-control@example.test');
    const allowed = await request(app.getHttpServer()).get('/api/bff/me').set('cookie', cookie)
      .query({ code: 'query-code', state: 'query-state' }).expect(200);
    expect(allowed.headers.location).toBeUndefined();
    expect(allowed.body).toBeDefined();

    const controllerReached = await request(app.getHttpServer()).get(callbackPaths[0]).set('cookie', cookie)
      .query({ code: 'private-invalid-code', state: 'private-invalid-state' });
    expect(controllerReached.status).toBe(303);
    expect(controllerReached.headers.location).toContain('instagramAuthorizationError=OAUTH_STATE_INVALID');
    expect(controllerReached.headers['cache-control']).toMatch(/no-store/i);
    expect(controllerReached.headers['referrer-policy']).toBe('no-referrer');
    expect(controllerReached.headers.location).not.toContain('private-invalid-code');
    expect(controllerReached.headers.location).not.toContain('private-invalid-state');
    expect(await prisma.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.count({ where: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id } })).toBe(0);
    expect(instagramFake.calls).toHaveLength(beforeProvider);

    const reserved = await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/instagram-authorizations`)
      .set('cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').set('idempotency-key', '6a342f53-d4e2-4f1b-845a-7882d532ad1c')
      .send({ operation: 'CONNECT' }).expect(201);
    const state = new URL(reserved.body.authorizationUrl).searchParams.get('state');
    expect(state).toBeTruthy();
    const validCallback = await request(app.getHttpServer()).get(callbackPaths[0]).set('cookie', cookie)
      .query({ state, code: 'synthetic-valid-session-code' });
    expect(validCallback.status).toBe(303);
    expect(validCallback.headers.location).toContain(`instagramAuthorization=${reserved.body.id}`);
    expect(validCallback.headers.location).not.toContain(state);
    expect(validCallback.headers.location).not.toContain('synthetic-valid-session-code');
    expect((await prisma.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: reserved.body.id } })).status)
      .toBe('CANDIDATE_READY');
    expect(instagramFake.calls.length).toBeGreaterThan(beforeProvider);
  });
});
```

### api-app/test/number-onboarding-flow.e2e-spec.ts — 83275 bytes SHA256 ec3d8cfd9733ef968bd00303aea68e22eba82a827118a94e50e570aae19c122f

```text
import { INestApplication } from '@nestjs/common';
import { createHmac } from 'node:crypto';
import request from 'supertest';
import type { Prisma } from '@prisma/client';
import { afterAll, afterEach, beforeAll, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { MetaHttpClient } from '../src/integrations/meta/meta.client';
import { RealtimeService } from '../src/realtime/realtime.service';
import { PrismaService } from '../src/infra/prisma/prisma.service';
import { EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_SECRET, META_APP_SECRET, contratarCanalNaoOficial, criarCanalVerificado, criarMantenedora, criarUsuario, limparBanco, login, metaFake, evolutionFake, subirApp, tetoComCanalNaoOficial, typebotFake } from './helpers';

describe('BFF number registration workflow (PostgreSQL)', () => {
  let app: INestApplication;
  let prisma: PrismaService;
  let tenant: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof criarMantenedora>>;
  let cookie: string;

  beforeAll(async () => { tetoComCanalNaoOficial(); process.env.EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_URL = 'https://example.test/webhooks/evolution'; ({ app, prisma } = await subirApp()); });
  afterAll(async () => { if (prisma) await limparBanco(prisma); await app?.close(); });
  afterEach(() => { vi.restoreAllMocks(); vi.unstubAllGlobals(); vi.unstubAllEnvs(); });
  beforeEach(async () => {
    await limparBanco(prisma);
    tenant = await criarMantenedora(app, 'Number registration', 'number-registration@example.test');
    cookie = await login(app, 'number-registration@example.test');
    metaFake.wabaRecusada = null;
    metaFake.wabaRecursos = [
      { wabaId: '123456789', phoneNumberId: '111111111', telefone: '5584998880000' },
      { wabaId: '123456789', phoneNumberId: '987654321', telefone: '5584999990000' },
      { wabaId: '555555555', phoneNumberId: '222222222', telefone: '5584997770000' },
    ];
    metaFake.registrosNumero = 0;
    metaFake.subscriptionHook = null;
  });

  const bff = (verb: 'get' | 'post', path: string) => {
    const req = request(app.getHttpServer())[verb](path).set('Cookie', cookie);
    return verb === 'post' ? req.set('x-requested-with', 'teste') : req;
  };

  it('recovers the same one-use nonce for idempotent create and connects only after provider subscribe plus receipt', async () => {
    const key = 'aaaaaaaa-aaaa-4aaa-8aaa-aaaaaaaaaaaa';
    const createBody = { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, tipo: 'OFICIAL', operation: 'NOVO' };
    const first = await bff('post', '/api/bff/number-registrations').set('Idempotency-Key', key).send(createBody).expect(201);
    const replay = await bff('post', '/api/bff/number-registrations').set('Idempotency-Key', key).send(createBody).expect(201);
    expect(replay.body.attempt.id).toBe(first.body.attempt.id);
    expect(replay.body.nonce).toBe(first.body.nonce);
    expect(JSON.stringify(first.body)).not.toMatch(/nonceHash|sessionHash|candidateCiphertext|fixture-scoped-token/);

    const id = first.body.attempt.id as string;
    await bff('post', `/api/bff/number-registrations/${id}/authorize`).send({ nonce: first.body.nonce, code: 'fixture-oauth-code' }).expect(200);
    await bff('post', `/api/bff/number-registrations/${id}/authorize`).send({ nonce: first.body.nonce, code: 'fixture-oauth-code' }).expect(409);

    const options = await bff('get', `/api/bff/number-registrations/${id}/options?wabaId=123456789&after=fixture-page-1`).expect(200);
    expect(options.body.numbers).toEqual([{ id: '987654321', telefone: '5584999990000', nomeVerificado: 'Fixture' }]);
    expect(options.body.next).toBeNull();
    await bff('get', `/api/bff/number-registrations/${id}/options?wabaId=123456789&after=${'x'.repeat(513)}`).expect(400);

    const confirmed = await bff('post', `/api/bff/number-registrations/${id}/confirm`)
      .send({ wabaId: '123456789', phoneNumberId: '987654321', modoRegistro: 'JA_REGISTRADO', after: 'fixture-page-1' }).expect(200);
    expect(confirmed.body.registration).toMatchObject({ state: 'PENDING_RECEIPT', stage: 'MAPPED_PENDING_RECEIPT' });
    expect(JSON.stringify(confirmed.body)).not.toMatch(/fixture-scoped-token|candidateCiphertext|sessionHash/);

    const webhook = {
      entry: [{ changes: [{ field: 'messages', value: {
        metadata: { phone_number_id: '987654321' },
        messages: [{ id: 'wamid-registration-receipt', from: '5584997770000', type: 'text', text: { body: 'receipt' } }],
      } }] }],
    };
    const deliverReceipt = async () => {
      const signature = createHmac('sha256', META_APP_SECRET).update(JSON.stringify(webhook)).digest('hex');
      await request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/webhooks/meta').set('content-type', 'application/json')
        .set('x-hub-signature-256', `sha256=${signature}`).send(webhook).expect(200);
    };
    metaFake.subscriptionHook = deliverReceipt;
    const advanced = await bff('post', `/api/bff/number-registrations/${id}/advance`).send({}).expect(200);
    metaFake.subscriptionHook = null;
    expect(advanced.body).toMatchObject({ state: 'CONNECTED', stage: 'RECEIPT_VERIFIED' });
    const connected = await bff('get', `/api/bff/number-registrations/${id}`).expect(200);
    expect(connected.body).toMatchObject({ state: 'CONNECTED', stage: 'RECEIPT_VERIFIED' });
    const channel = await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: connected.body.channelId } });
    const attemptBeforeVerification = await prisma.channelRegistrationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id } });
    const credentialBeforeVerification = await prisma.channelCredential.findUniqueOrThrow({
      where: { channelId: channel.id }, select: { id: true, provider: true, buId: true, generation: true, version: true },
    });
    const identityBeforeVerification = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { currentChannelId: channel.id } });
    expect(channel.status).toBe('CONECTADO');
    expect(channel.receiptVerifiedAt).toBeInstanceOf(Date);
    expect(channel.pairingEpoch).toBe(0);
    expect(channel.currentAttemptId).toBe(id);
    expect(attemptBeforeVerification).toMatchObject({ id, state: 'CONNECTED', stage: 'RECEIPT_VERIFIED', pairingEpoch: null, generation: channel.generation });

    const firstVerification = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/OFICIAL/verify`).send({});
    const secondVerification = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/OFICIAL/verify`).send({});
    const verificationResults = [firstVerification, secondVerification].map(({ status, body }) => ({
      status,
      catalogCode: body?.code ?? body?.error?.code ?? null,
    }));
    expect(verificationResults).toEqual([{ status: 200, catalogCode: null }, { status: 200, catalogCode: null }]);

    const channelAfterVerification = await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: channel.id } });
    const attemptAfterVerification = await prisma.channelRegistrationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id } });
    const credentialAfterVerification = await prisma.channelCredential.findUniqueOrThrow({
      where: { channelId: channel.id }, select: { id: true, provider: true, buId: true, generation: true, version: true },
    });
    const identityAfterVerification = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: identityBeforeVerification.id } });
    expect(channelAfterVerification).toMatchObject({ id: channel.id, generation: channel.generation, pairingEpoch: 0, currentAttemptId: id, status: 'CONECTADO', receiptVerifiedAt: channel.receiptVerifiedAt });
    expect(attemptAfterVerification).toMatchObject({ id, state: 'CONNECTED', stage: 'RECEIPT_VERIFIED', pairingEpoch: null, generation: channel.generation, version: attemptBeforeVerification.version });
    expect(credentialAfterVerification).toEqual(credentialBeforeVerification);
    expect(identityAfterVerification).toMatchObject({ id: identityBeforeVerification.id, currentChannelId: channel.id, buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, externalResourceId: channel.phoneNumberId });

    const auditCountBeforeFailure = await prisma.activityLog.count({ where: { action: 'canal.identidade.verificada', entidadeId: id } });
    const buBeforeFailure = await prisma.businessUnit.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: tenant.businessUnit.id }, select: { id: true, phone: true } });
    const numberPages = vi.spyOn(metaFake, 'numerosDaWaba')
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ data: [], next: 'cursor-page-two' })
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ data: [], next: 'cursor-page-three' });
    const inconclusive = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/OFICIAL/verify`).send({});
    const pageCalls = numberPages.mock.calls.map((call) => [...call]);
    numberPages.mockRestore();
    expect(pageCalls).toEqual([
      [channel.wabaId, 'fixture-scoped-token', undefined],
      [channel.wabaId, 'fixture-scoped-token', 'cursor-page-two'],
    ]);
    const inconclusiveResult = { status: inconclusive.status, catalogCode: inconclusive.body?.code ?? inconclusive.body?.error?.code ?? null };
    expect(inconclusiveResult).toEqual({ status: 503, catalogCode: 'META_INDISPONIVEL' });

    const channelAfterFailure = await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: channel.id } });
    const attemptAfterFailure = await prisma.channelRegistrationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id } });
    const credentialAfterFailure = await prisma.channelCredential.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { channelId: channel.id }, select: { id: true, provider: true, buId: true, generation: true, version: true } });
    const identityAfterFailure = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: identityBeforeVerification.id } });
    const buAfterFailure = await prisma.businessUnit.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: tenant.businessUnit.id }, select: { id: true, phone: true } });
    expect(channelAfterFailure).toMatchObject({
      id: channel.id, buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, type: 'OFICIAL', status: channel.status,
      providerConnectionState: channel.providerConnectionState, ownNumberVerified: channel.ownNumberVerified,
      verifiedEpoch: channel.verifiedEpoch, generation: channel.generation, pairingEpoch: channel.pairingEpoch,
      currentAttemptId: id, wabaId: channel.wabaId, phoneNumberId: channel.phoneNumberId,
      receiptVerifiedAt: channel.receiptVerifiedAt, operationLeaseToken: null, operationLeaseUntil: null,
    });
    expect(attemptAfterFailure).toMatchObject({ id, state: attemptBeforeVerification.state, stage: attemptBeforeVerification.stage, generation: attemptBeforeVerification.generation, pairingEpoch: attemptBeforeVerification.pairingEpoch, version: attemptBeforeVerification.version });
    expect(credentialAfterFailure).toEqual(credentialBeforeVerification);
    expect(identityAfterFailure).toEqual(identityBeforeVerification);
    expect(buAfterFailure).toEqual(buBeforeFailure);
    expect(await prisma.activityLog.count({ where: { action: 'canal.identidade.verificada', entidadeId: id } })).toBe(auditCountBeforeFailure);

    const [channelBeforeInvalidPayload, attemptBeforeInvalidPayload, credentialBeforeInvalidPayload, identityBeforeInvalidPayload, buBeforeInvalidPayload] = await Promise.all([
      prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: channel.id } }),
      prisma.channelRegistrationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id } }),
      prisma.channelCredential.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { channelId: channel.id }, select: { id: true, provider: true, buId: true, generation: true, version: true } }),
      prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: identityBeforeVerification.id } }),
      prisma.businessUnit.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: tenant.businessUnit.id }, select: { id: true, phone: true } }),
    ]);
    const auditsBeforeInvalidPayload = await prisma.activityLog.count({ where: { action: 'canal.identidade.verificada', entidadeId: id } });
    vi.stubEnv('META_APP_ID', 'fixture-app-id');
    vi.stubEnv('META_APP_SECRET', 'fixture-app-secret');
    vi.stubEnv('META_SIGNUP_CONFIG_ID', 'fixture-config-id');

    const providerDetail = 'private-provider-json-detail';

    const adapterPort = vi.spyOn(metaFake, 'verificarNumeroVinculado').mockImplementation((wabaId, phoneNumberId, accessToken) =>
      realAdapter.verificarNumeroVinculado(wabaId, phoneNumberId, accessToken));
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', vi.fn()
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ ok: true, json: async () => ({ data: {

        granular_scopes: [
          { scope: 'whatsapp_business_management', target_ids: ['123456789'] },
          { scope: 'whatsapp_business_messaging', target_ids: ['123456789'] },
        ],
      } }) })
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({ ok: true, json: async () => { throw new SyntaxError(providerDetail); } }));
    const malformedPayload = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/OFICIAL/verify`).send({});
    expect({ status: malformedPayload.status, body: malformedPayload.body }).toEqual({
      status: 503,
      body: { code: 'META_INDISPONIVEL', error: 'A verificação do número está temporariamente indisponível.' },
    });
    expect(JSON.stringify(malformedPayload.body)).not.toContain(providerDetail);

    const malformedGrantDetail = 'private-debug-grant-detail';
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', vi.fn().mockResolvedValueOnce({ ok: true, json: async () => ({ data: {
      app_id: 'fixture-app-id', is_valid: true, scopes: { malformed: malformedGrantDetail },
      granular_scopes: [
        { scope: 'whatsapp_business_management', target_ids: ['123456789'] },
        { scope: 'whatsapp_business_messaging', target_ids: ['123456789'] },
      ],
    } }) }));
    const malformedGrantPayload = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/OFICIAL/verify`).send({});
    expect({ status: malformedGrantPayload.status, body: malformedGrantPayload.body }).toEqual({
      status: 503,
      body: { code: 'META_INDISPONIVEL', error: 'A verificação do número está temporariamente indisponível.' },
    });
    expect(JSON.stringify(malformedGrantPayload.body)).not.toContain(malformedGrantDetail);

    const genericDetail = 'private-generic-port-detail';
    adapterPort.mockRejectedValue(new Error(genericDetail));
    const genericFailure = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/OFICIAL/verify`).send({});
    expect(genericFailure.status).toBe(500);
    expect(JSON.stringify(genericFailure.body)).not.toContain('META_INDISPONIVEL');
    expect(JSON.stringify(genericFailure.body)).not.toContain(genericDetail);
    adapterPort.mockRestore();
    vi.unstubAllGlobals();
    vi.unstubAllEnvs();

    const [channelAfterInvalidPayload, attemptAfterInvalidPayload, credentialAfterInvalidPayload, identityAfterInvalidPayload, buAfterInvalidPayload] = await Promise.all([
      prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: channel.id } }),
      prisma.channelRegistrationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id } }),
      prisma.channelCredential.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { channelId: channel.id }, select: { id: true, provider: true, buId: true, generation: true, version: true } }),
      prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: identityBeforeVerification.id } }),
      prisma.businessUnit.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: tenant.businessUnit.id }, select: { id: true, phone: true } }),
    ]);
    expect(channelAfterInvalidPayload).toMatchObject({
      id: channelBeforeInvalidPayload.id, buId: channelBeforeInvalidPayload.buId, type: channelBeforeInvalidPayload.type,
      status: channelBeforeInvalidPayload.status, generation: channelBeforeInvalidPayload.generation,
      pairingEpoch: channelBeforeInvalidPayload.pairingEpoch, currentAttemptId: id,
      phoneNumberId: channelBeforeInvalidPayload.phoneNumberId, wabaId: channelBeforeInvalidPayload.wabaId,
      receiptVerifiedAt: channelBeforeInvalidPayload.receiptVerifiedAt,
      operationLeaseToken: null, operationLeaseUntil: null,
    });
    expect(attemptAfterInvalidPayload).toMatchObject({
      id, state: attemptBeforeInvalidPayload.state, stage: attemptBeforeInvalidPayload.stage,
      generation: attemptBeforeInvalidPayload.generation, pairingEpoch: attemptBeforeInvalidPayload.pairingEpoch,
      version: attemptBeforeInvalidPayload.version,
    });
    expect(credentialAfterInvalidPayload).toEqual(credentialBeforeInvalidPayload);
    expect(identityAfterInvalidPayload).toEqual(identityBeforeInvalidPayload);
    expect(buAfterInvalidPayload).toEqual(buBeforeInvalidPayload);
    expect(await prisma.activityLog.count({ where: { action: 'canal.identidade.verificada', entidadeId: id } })).toBe(auditsBeforeInvalidPayload);
  });

  it('consumes OAuth codes once and requires a fresh attempt and nonce after an ambiguous exchange', async () => {
    const created = await bff('post', '/api/bff/number-registrations').set('Idempotency-Key', 'a1010101-a101-4101-8101-a10101010101')
      .send({ buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, tipo: 'OFICIAL', operation: 'NOVO' }).expect(201);
    const firstId = created.body.attempt.id as string;
    const exchangeStart = metaFake.exchangeCalls;
    await bff('post', `/api/bff/number-registrations/${firstId}/authorize`)
      .send({ nonce: created.body.nonce, code: 'fixture-consumed-code' }).expect(200);
    expect(metaFake.exchangeCalls).toBe(exchangeStart + 1);
    await bff('post', `/api/bff/number-registrations/${firstId}/authorize`)
      .send({ nonce: created.body.nonce, code: 'fixture-consumed-code' }).expect(409);
    expect(metaFake.exchangeCalls).toBe(exchangeStart + 1);

    const firstChannel = await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: created.body.attempt.channelId } });
    const ambiguous = await bff('post', '/api/bff/number-registrations').set('Idempotency-Key', 'a2020202-a202-4202-8202-a20202020202')
      .send({ buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, tipo: 'OFICIAL', operation: 'REAUTORIZAR', channelId: firstChannel.id, expectedGeneration: firstChannel.generation }).expect(201);
    metaFake.exchangeFails = 1;
    const beforeAmbiguous = metaFake.exchangeCalls;
    await bff('post', `/api/bff/number-registrations/${ambiguous.body.attempt.id}/authorize`)
      .send({ nonce: ambiguous.body.nonce, code: 'fixture-lost-response-code' }).expect(409)
      .expect(({ body }) => expect(body.code).toBe('AUTHORIZATION_AMBIGUOUS'));
    expect(metaFake.exchangeCalls).toBe(beforeAmbiguous + 1);
    await bff('post', `/api/bff/number-registrations/${ambiguous.body.attempt.id}/authorize`)
      .send({ nonce: ambiguous.body.nonce, code: 'fixture-lost-response-code' }).expect(409);
    expect(metaFake.exchangeCalls).toBe(beforeAmbiguous + 1);

    const fresh = await bff('post', '/api/bff/number-registrations').set('Idempotency-Key', 'a3030303-a303-4303-8303-a30303030303')
      .send({ buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, tipo: 'OFICIAL', operation: 'REAUTORIZAR', channelId: firstChannel.id, expectedGeneration: firstChannel.generation }).expect(201);
    expect(fresh.body.attempt.id).not.toBe(ambiguous.body.attempt.id);
    expect(fresh.body.nonce).not.toBe(ambiguous.body.nonce);
    await bff('post', `/api/bff/number-registrations/${fresh.body.attempt.id}/authorize`)
      .send({ nonce: fresh.body.nonce, code: 'fixture-fresh-code' }).expect(200);
    expect(metaFake.exchangeCalls).toBe(beforeAmbiguous + 2);
  });

    expect(await prisma.channelCredential.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { channelId: channel.id } }))

    const tenantBu = await prisma.businessUnit.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: tenant.businessUnit.id }, select: { setorId: true, phone: true } });
    const sameTenantOtherBu = await prisma.businessUnit.create({ data: {
      mantenedoraId: tenant.mantenedora.id, setorId: tenantBu.setorId, name: 'Other Official slot', phone: tenantBu.phone,
    } });
    const otherSlot = await prisma.buChannel.create({ data: { buId: sameTenantOtherBu.id, type: 'OFICIAL', phoneNumberId: '111111111' } });
    const foreign = await criarMantenedora(app, 'Foreign resource owner', 'foreign-resource-owner@example.test');
    const foreignSlot = await prisma.buChannel.create({ data: { buId: foreign.businessUnit.id, type: 'OFICIAL', phoneNumberId: '222222222' } });
    await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.createMany({ data: [
      { provider: 'OFICIAL', externalResourceId: '111111111', buId: sameTenantOtherBu.id, currentChannelId: otherSlot.id, managedLifecycle: false, cleanupState: 'UNMANAGED' },
      { provider: 'OFICIAL', externalResourceId: '222222222', buId: foreign.businessUnit.id, currentChannelId: foreignSlot.id, managedLifecycle: false, cleanupState: 'UNMANAGED' },
    ] });
    metaFake.wabaRecursos = [
      { wabaId: '123456789', phoneNumberId: '111111111', telefone: tenantBu.phone! },
      { wabaId: '123456789', phoneNumberId: '222222222', telefone: tenantBu.phone! },
    ];
    const attempt = await bff('post', '/api/bff/number-registrations').set('Idempotency-Key', 'a5050505-a505-4505-8505-a50505050505')
      .send({ buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, tipo: 'OFICIAL', operation: 'REAUTORIZAR', channelId: ownChannel.id, expectedGeneration: ownChannel.generation }).expect(201);
    await bff('post', `/api/bff/number-registrations/${attempt.body.attempt.id}/authorize`)
      .send({ nonce: attempt.body.nonce, code: 'fixture-owner-check' }).expect(200);
    for (const phoneNumberId of ['111111111', '222222222']) {
      await bff('post', `/api/bff/number-registrations/${attempt.body.attempt.id}/confirm`)
        .send({ wabaId: '123456789', phoneNumberId, modoRegistro: 'NOVO', pin: '123456', ...(phoneNumberId === '222222222' ? { after: 'fixture-page-1' } : {}) }).expect(409)
        .expect(({ body }) => expect(body.code).toBe('RESOURCE_OWNED'));
    }
    expect(metaFake.registrosNumero).toBe(0);
    expect(await prisma.channelCredential.count({ where: { channelId: ownChannel.id } })).toBe(0);
    expect(await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { provider_externalResourceId: { provider: 'OFICIAL', externalResourceId: '987654321' } } }))
      .toMatchObject({ managedLifecycle: false, cleanupState: 'UNMANAGED' });
  });

  it('keeps an active credential unchanged after wrong-phone confirmation and post-auth cancellation', async () => {
    const active = await criarCanalVerificado(prisma, tenant.businessUnit.id, 'OFICIAL', 'active-preserved-resource');
    const credentialBefore = await prisma.channelCredential.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { channelId: active.channel.id } });
    const attempt = await bff('post', '/api/bff/number-registrations').set('Idempotency-Key', 'a6060606-a606-4606-8606-a60606060606')
      .send({ buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, tipo: 'OFICIAL', operation: 'REAUTORIZAR', channelId: active.channel.id, expectedGeneration: active.channel.generation }).expect(201);
    await bff('post', `/api/bff/number-registrations/${attempt.body.attempt.id}/authorize`)
      .send({ nonce: attempt.body.nonce, code: 'fixture-wrong-phone-proof' }).expect(200);
    const candidate = await prisma.channelRegistrationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: attempt.body.attempt.id } });
    expect(candidate.candidateCiphertext).not.toBeNull();
    await bff('post', `/api/bff/number-registrations/${attempt.body.attempt.id}/confirm`)
      .send({ wabaId: '123456789', phoneNumberId: '111111111', modoRegistro: 'NOVO', pin: '123456' }).expect(409)
      .expect(({ body }) => expect(body.code).toBe('PHONE_MISMATCH'));
    expect(metaFake.registrosNumero).toBe(0);

    await bff('post', `/api/bff/number-registrations/${attempt.body.attempt.id}/cancel`).send({}).expect(200);
    const [credentialAfter, channelAfter, cancelled] = await Promise.all([
      prisma.channelCredential.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { channelId: active.channel.id } }),
      prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: active.channel.id } }),
      prisma.channelRegistrationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: attempt.body.attempt.id } }),
    ]);
    expect(credentialAfter).toMatchObject({
      version: credentialBefore.version, generation: credentialBefore.generation,
      keyId: credentialBefore.keyId, nonce: credentialBefore.nonce,
      ciphertext: credentialBefore.ciphertext, tag: credentialBefore.tag,
    });
    expect(channelAfter).toMatchObject({ generation: active.channel.generation, phoneNumberId: active.channel.phoneNumberId, ownNumberVerified: true });
    expect(cancelled).toMatchObject({ state: 'CANCELLED', candidateKeyId: null, candidateNonce: null, candidateCiphertext: null, candidateTag: null });
  });

  it('replays the winner when a same-key request resumes after the reservation transaction commits', async () => {
    const key = 'efefefef-efef-4fef-8fef-efefefefefef';
    const payload = { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, tipo: 'OFICIAL', operation: 'NOVO' };
    const delegate = prisma.channelRegistrationAttempt as unknown as {
      findUnique: (args: Prisma.ChannelRegistrationAttemptFindUniqueArgs) => Promise<unknown>;
    };
    const originalFindUnique = delegate.findUnique.bind(prisma.channelRegistrationAttempt);
    let entered!: () => void;
    let release!: () => void;
    const firstRead = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { entered = resolve; });
    const gate = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { release = resolve; });
    let keyedReads = 0;
    delegate.findUnique = async (args: Prisma.ChannelRegistrationAttemptFindUniqueArgs) => {
      const result = await originalFindUnique(args);
      if (args.where.mantenedoraId_idempotencyKey?.idempotencyKey === key && ++keyedReads === 1) {
        entered();
        await gate;
      }
      return result;
    };
    try {
      const pausedRequest = bff('post', '/api/bff/number-registrations').set('Idempotency-Key', key).send(payload);
      const pausedResult = pausedRequest.then((response) => response);
      await firstRead;
      const winner = await bff('post', '/api/bff/number-registrations').set('Idempotency-Key', key).send(payload).expect(201);
      release();
      const replay = await pausedResult;
      expect(replay.status).toBe(201);
      expect(replay.body.attempt.id).toBe(winner.body.attempt.id);
      expect(replay.body.nonce).toBe(winner.body.nonce);
      expect(await prisma.channelRegistrationAttempt.count({ where: { mantenedoraId: tenant.mantenedora.id, idempotencyKey: key } })).toBe(1);
    } finally {
      release();
      delegate.findUnique = originalFindUnique;
    }
  });

  it('creates a new BU and inherited founder grant once, then replays the same attempt and nonce', async () => {
    const key = 'abababab-abab-4bab-8bab-abababababab';
    const payload = { novaBu: { nome: 'Atomic onboarding BU' }, tipo: 'OFICIAL', operation: 'NOVO' };
    const beforeBusinessUnits = await prisma.businessUnit.count({ where: { mantenedoraId: tenant.mantenedora.id } });

    const first = await bff('post', '/api/bff/number-registrations').set('Idempotency-Key', key).send(payload).expect(201);
    const createdBuId = first.body.attempt.buId as string;
    const replay = await bff('post', '/api/bff/number-registrations').set('Idempotency-Key', key).send(payload).expect(201);

    expect(replay.body.attempt.id).toBe(first.body.attempt.id);
    expect(replay.body.attempt.buId).toBe(createdBuId);
    expect(replay.body.nonce).toBe(first.body.nonce);
    expect(await prisma.businessUnit.count({ where: { mantenedoraId: tenant.mantenedora.id } })).toBe(beforeBusinessUnits + 1);
    expect(await prisma.channelRegistrationAttempt.count({ where: { mantenedoraId: tenant.mantenedora.id, idempotencyKey: key } })).toBe(1);
    expect(await prisma.userBuRole.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { userId_buId: { userId: tenant.owner.id, buId: createdBuId } } }))
      .toMatchObject({ role: 'OWNER', grantedViaTipo: 'MANTENEDORA', grantedViaId: tenant.mantenedora.id });

    await bff('post', '/api/bff/number-registrations').set('Idempotency-Key', key)
      .send({ ...payload, novaBu: { nome: 'Different payload must conflict' } }).expect(409)
      .expect(({ body }) => expect(body.code).toBe('IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT'));
    expect(await prisma.businessUnit.count({ where: { mantenedoraId: tenant.mantenedora.id } })).toBe(beforeBusinessUnits + 1);
  });

    await request(app.getHttpServer()).delete(`/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/OFICIAL`)
      .set('Cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').expect(204);
    expect(await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: old.identity.id } })).toMatchObject({ buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, currentChannelId: null });

    const other = await criarMantenedora(app, 'Attempted resource transfer', 'resource-transfer@example.test');
    const otherCookie = await login(app, 'resource-transfer@example.test');
    const created = await request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/api/bff/number-registrations').set('Cookie', otherCookie)
      .set('x-requested-with', 'teste').set('Idempotency-Key', 'cdefcdef-cdef-4def-8def-cdefcdefcdef')
      .send({ buId: other.businessUnit.id, tipo: 'OFICIAL', operation: 'NOVO' }).expect(201);
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/number-registrations/${created.body.attempt.id}/authorize`).set('Cookie', otherCookie)
      .set('x-requested-with', 'teste').send({ nonce: created.body.nonce, code: 'fixture-oauth-code' }).expect(200);
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/number-registrations/${created.body.attempt.id}/confirm`).set('Cookie', otherCookie)
      .set('x-requested-with', 'teste').send({ wabaId: '123456789', phoneNumberId: resource, modoRegistro: 'NOVO', pin: '123456', after: 'fixture-page-1' })
      .expect(409).expect(({ body }) => expect(body.code).toBe('RESOURCE_OWNED'));
    expect(metaFake.registrosNumero).toBe(0);
    expect(await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: old.identity.id } })).toMatchObject({ buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, currentChannelId: null });

    const late = { entry: [{ changes: [{ field: 'messages', value: {
      metadata: { phone_number_id: resource }, messages: [{ id: 'wamid-tombstone-late', from: '5584997778888', type: 'text', text: { body: 'late old-owner content' } }],
    } }] }] };
    const signature = `sha256=${createHmac('sha256', META_APP_SECRET).update(JSON.stringify(late)).digest('hex')}`;
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/webhooks/meta').set('x-hub-signature-256', signature).send(late).expect(200);
    expect(await prisma.message.count({ where: { externalId: 'wamid-tombstone-late' } })).toBe(0);
    expect(await prisma.conversation.count({ where: { buId: other.businessUnit.id } })).toBe(0);
  });

  it('allows only one BU to reserve a concurrently selected provider resource', async () => {
    const competitor = await criarMantenedora(app, 'Concurrent owner', 'concurrent-owner@example.test');
    const competitorCookie = await login(app, 'concurrent-owner@example.test');
    const createAndAuthorize = async (actorCookie: string, buId: string, idempotencyKey: string) => {
      const created = await request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/api/bff/number-registrations').set('Cookie', actorCookie)
        .set('x-requested-with', 'teste').set('Idempotency-Key', idempotencyKey)
        .send({ buId, tipo: 'OFICIAL', operation: 'NOVO' }).expect(201);
      await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/number-registrations/${created.body.attempt.id}/authorize`)
        .set('Cookie', actorCookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste')
        .send({ nonce: created.body.nonce, code: 'fixture-oauth-code' }).expect(200);
      return { id: created.body.attempt.id as string, actorCookie };
    };
    const [first, second] = await Promise.all([
      createAndAuthorize(cookie, tenant.businessUnit.id, 'dddddddd-dddd-4ddd-8ddd-dddddddddddd'),
      createAndAuthorize(competitorCookie, competitor.businessUnit.id, 'eeeeeeee-eeee-4eee-8eee-eeeeeeeeeeee'),
    ]);
    const confirm = ({ id, actorCookie }: { id: string; actorCookie: string }) => request(app.getHttpServer())
      .post(`/api/bff/number-registrations/${id}/confirm`).set('Cookie', actorCookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste')
      .send({ wabaId: '123456789', phoneNumberId: '987654321', modoRegistro: 'NOVO', pin: '123456', after: 'fixture-page-1' });
    const outcomes = await Promise.all([confirm(first), confirm(second)]);
    expect(outcomes.filter((result) => result.status === 200)).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(outcomes.filter((result) => result.status === 409)).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(metaFake.registrosNumero).toBe(1);
    const identity = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({
      where: { provider_externalResourceId: { provider: 'OFICIAL', externalResourceId: '987654321' } },
    });
    expect([tenant.businessUnit.id, competitor.businessUnit.id]).toContain(identity.buId);
  });

  it('atomically prevents official enrollment from taking a different phone after Evolution proves the BU phone', async () => {
    await contratarCanalNaoOficial(prisma, tenant.mantenedora.id);
    await prisma.businessUnit.update({ where: { id: tenant.businessUnit.id }, data: { phone: null } });
    metaFake.wabaRecursos = [{ wabaId: '7654321', phoneNumberId: '7654322', telefone: '5584999000001' }];
    evolutionFake.ownerPhone = '5584999000002';
    evolutionFake.createdState = 'open';
    const created = await bff('post', '/api/bff/number-registrations').set('Idempotency-Key', 'abab0000-abab-4bab-8bab-abababababab')
      .send({ buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, tipo: 'OFICIAL', operation: 'NOVO' }).expect(201);
    await bff('post', `/api/bff/number-registrations/${created.body.attempt.id}/authorize`)
      .send({ nonce: created.body.nonce, code: 'fixture-oauth-code' }).expect(200);
    let entered!: () => void; let release!: () => void;
    const atMeta = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { entered = resolve; });
    const gate = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { release = resolve; });
    metaFake.confirmHook = async () => { entered(); await gate; };
    const pending = bff('post', `/api/bff/number-registrations/${created.body.attempt.id}/confirm`)
      .send({ wabaId: '7654321', phoneNumberId: '7654322', modoRegistro: 'NOVO', pin: '123456' });
    const result = pending.then((response) => response);
    await atMeta;
    const paired = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/nao-oficial/pair`)
      .send({ intent: 'INITIAL' }).expect(200);
    expect(paired.body.state).toBe('PENDING_RECEIPT');
    expect(await prisma.businessUnit.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: tenant.businessUnit.id }, select: { phone: true } })).toEqual({ phone: '5584999000002' });
    release();
    metaFake.confirmHook = null;
    const rejected = await result;
    expect(rejected.status).toBe(409);
    expect(rejected.body.code).toBe('PHONE_MISMATCH');
    expect(metaFake.registrosNumero).toBe(0);
    expect(await prisma.channelCredential.count({ where: { channelId: created.body.attempt.channelId } })).toBe(0);
  });

  it('rejects a stale confirmation after its lease expires and a replacement attempt wins', async () => {
    const created = await bff('post', '/api/bff/number-registrations').set('Idempotency-Key', 'abababab-abab-4bab-8bab-abababababab')
      .send({ buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, tipo: 'OFICIAL', operation: 'NOVO' }).expect(201);
    const oldId = created.body.attempt.id as string;
    await bff('post', `/api/bff/number-registrations/${oldId}/authorize`).send({ nonce: created.body.nonce, code: 'fixture-oauth-code' }).expect(200);
    let entered!: () => void;
    let release!: () => void;
    const atMeta = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { entered = resolve; });
    const providerGate = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { release = resolve; });
    metaFake.confirmHook = async () => { entered(); await providerGate; };
    const stale = bff('post', `/api/bff/number-registrations/${oldId}/confirm`)
      .send({ wabaId: '123456789', phoneNumberId: '987654321', modoRegistro: 'JA_REGISTRADO', after: 'fixture-page-1' });
    const staleResult = stale.then((response) => response);
    await atMeta;
    const priorChannel = await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: created.body.attempt.channelId } });
    await prisma.buChannel.update({ where: { id: priorChannel.id }, data: { operationLeaseUntil: new Date(Date.now() - 1_000) } });
    const replacement = await bff('post', '/api/bff/number-registrations').set('Idempotency-Key', 'cdcdcdcd-cdcd-4dcd-8dcd-cdcdcdcdcdcd')
      .send({ buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, tipo: 'OFICIAL', operation: 'REAUTORIZAR', channelId: priorChannel.id, expectedGeneration: priorChannel.generation }).expect(201);
    release();
    metaFake.confirmHook = null;
    expect((await staleResult).status).toBe(409);
    expect((await staleResult).body.code).toBe('ATTEMPT_STALE');
    expect(await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: priorChannel.id } })).toMatchObject({ currentAttemptId: replacement.body.attempt.id, generation: priorChannel.generation, ownNumberVerified: false });
    expect(await prisma.channelCredential.count({ where: { channelId: priorChannel.id } })).toBe(0);
  });

  it('renews a managed Evolution identity through cleanup then creates the reserved replacement once', async () => {
    await contratarCanalNaoOficial(prisma, tenant.mantenedora.id);
    const initial = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/nao-oficial/pair`).send({ intent: 'INITIAL' }).expect(200);
    const first = await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: initial.body.channelId } });
    const oldName = first.evolutionInstanceName!;
    const oldIdentity = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { provider_externalResourceId: { provider: 'NAO_OFICIAL', externalResourceId: oldName } } });
    const renewed = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/nao-oficial/pair`)
      .send({ intent: 'RENEW', expectedGeneration: first.generation }).expect(200);
    expect(renewed.body.state).toBe('CLEANUP_COMPLETED_RETRY_PAIR');
    expect(evolutionFake.inventory.has(oldName)).toBe(false);
    expect(await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: oldIdentity.id } })).toMatchObject({ cleanupState: 'CLEANED', managedLifecycle: true });
    const reserved = await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: first.id } });
    const stableAttemptId = reserved.currentAttemptId!;
    expect(reserved.pairingEpoch).toBe(first.pairingEpoch + 1);
    expect((await prisma.channelRegistrationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: stableAttemptId } })).stage).toBe('RESERVED_NEVER_CREATED');
    const resumed = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/nao-oficial/pair`)
      .send({ intent: 'INITIAL', expectedGeneration: reserved.generation }).expect(200);
    expect(resumed.body).toMatchObject({ attemptId: stableAttemptId, pairingEpoch: reserved.pairingEpoch, state: 'PENDING_RECEIPT' });
    expect(evolutionFake.createCalls).toBe(2);
    expect(await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: first.id } })).toMatchObject({ evolutionInstanceName: reserved.evolutionInstanceName, currentAttemptId: stableAttemptId });
  });

  it('serializes unlink against an in-flight managed create lease', async () => {
    await contratarCanalNaoOficial(prisma, tenant.mantenedora.id);
    let entered!: () => void;
    let release!: () => void;
    const atProvider = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { entered = resolve; });
    const providerGate = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { release = resolve; });
    evolutionFake.createHook = async () => { entered(); await providerGate; };
    const pairing = bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/nao-oficial/pair`).send({ intent: 'INITIAL' });
    const pairingResponse = pairing.then((response) => response);
    await Promise.race([atProvider, pairingResponse.then((response) => { throw new Error(`pairing returned before create hook: ${response.status} ${JSON.stringify(response.body)}`); })]);
    const channel = await prisma.buChannel.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, type: 'NAO_OFICIAL' } });
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).delete(`/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/NAO_OFICIAL`)
      .set('Cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').expect(409).expect(({ body }) => expect(body.code).toBe('CHANNEL_OPERATION_BUSY'));
    expect(await prisma.buChannel.findUnique({ where: { id: channel.id } })).not.toBeNull();
    release();
    const paired = await pairing.expect(200);
    evolutionFake.createHook = null;
    expect(paired.body.channelId).toBe(channel.id);
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).delete(`/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/NAO_OFICIAL`)
      .set('Cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').expect(204);
  });

  it('retains ambiguous managed cleanup and reconciles it before creating after unlink', async () => {
    await contratarCanalNaoOficial(prisma, tenant.mantenedora.id);
    const initial = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/nao-oficial/pair`).send({ intent: 'INITIAL' }).expect(200);
    const channel = await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: initial.body.channelId } });
    const name = channel.evolutionInstanceName!;
    const identity = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { provider_externalResourceId: { provider: 'NAO_OFICIAL', externalResourceId: name } } });
    evolutionFake.deleteFails = true;
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).delete(`/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/NAO_OFICIAL`)
      .set('Cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').expect(204);
    expect(await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: identity.id } })).toMatchObject({ currentChannelId: null, cleanupState: 'CLEANUP_PENDING' });
    const blocked = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/nao-oficial/pair`).send({ intent: 'INITIAL' }).expect(409);
    expect(blocked.body.code).toBe('CLEANUP_RECOVERY_BUSY');
    expect(evolutionFake.createCalls).toBe(1);
    evolutionFake.deleteFails = false;
    await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.update({ where: { id: identity.id }, data: { cleanupLeaseUntil: new Date(Date.now() - 1_000) } });
    const reconciled = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/nao-oficial/pair`).send({ intent: 'INITIAL' }).expect(409);
    expect(reconciled.body.code).toBe('CLEANUP_COMPLETED_RETRY_PAIR');
    expect(evolutionFake.createCalls).toBe(1);
    const replacement = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/nao-oficial/pair`).send({ intent: 'INITIAL' }).expect(200);
    expect(replacement.body.channelId).not.toBe(channel.id);
    expect(evolutionFake.createCalls).toBe(2);
  });

  it('wipes expired and superseded candidate envelopes while preserving an active credential', async () => {
    const active = await criarCanalVerificado(prisma, tenant.businessUnit.id, 'OFICIAL', 'active-official-resource');
    const create = (key: string) => bff('post', '/api/bff/number-registrations').set('Idempotency-Key', key).send({
      buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, tipo: 'OFICIAL', operation: 'REAUTORIZAR', channelId: active.channel.id, expectedGeneration: active.channel.generation,
    });
    const first = await create('11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111').expect(201);
    await bff('post', `/api/bff/number-registrations/${first.body.attempt.id}/authorize`).send({ nonce: first.body.nonce, code: 'fixture-oauth-code' }).expect(200);
    const secretBefore = await prisma.channelRegistrationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: first.body.attempt.id } });
    expect(secretBefore.candidateCiphertext).not.toBeNull();
    const second = await create('22222222-2222-4222-8222-222222222222').expect(201);
    const superseded = await prisma.channelRegistrationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: first.body.attempt.id } });
    expect(superseded).toMatchObject({ state: 'CANCELLED', stage: 'SUPERSEDED', candidateKeyId: null, candidateNonce: null, candidateCiphertext: null, candidateTag: null, nonceHash: null });
    await bff('post', `/api/bff/number-registrations/${second.body.attempt.id}/authorize`).send({ nonce: second.body.nonce, code: 'fixture-oauth-code' }).expect(200);
    await prisma.channelRegistrationAttempt.update({ where: { id: second.body.attempt.id }, data: { expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() - 1000) } });
    const expired = await bff('get', `/api/bff/number-registrations/${second.body.attempt.id}`).expect(200);
    expect(expired.body).toMatchObject({ state: 'ERROR', stage: 'EXPIRED' });
    const cleared = await prisma.channelRegistrationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: second.body.attempt.id } });
    expect(cleared).toMatchObject({ candidateKeyId: null, candidateNonce: null, candidateCiphertext: null, candidateTag: null, nonceHash: null });
    const activeCredential = await prisma.channelCredential.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { channelId: active.channel.id } });
    expect(activeCredential).toMatchObject({ version: 1, generation: active.channel.generation });
  });
```
