    const receiptBefore = connectedBefore.receiptVerifiedAt;
    const identityBefore = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { currentChannelId: channel.id } });
    const attemptBefore = await prisma.channelRegistrationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: attemptId } });
    expect(connectedBefore.status).toBe('CONECTADO');
    expect(attemptBefore).toMatchObject({ state: 'CONNECTED', stage: 'RECEIPT_VERIFIED' });

    const verification = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/NAO_OFICIAL/verify`).send({});
    const connectionEvent = (state: string) => request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/webhooks/evolution').set('x-leadshug-webhook-secret', EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_SECRET)
      .send({ event: 'connection.update', instance: channel.evolutionInstanceName, data: { state } });
    const connecting = await connectionEvent('connecting');
    const opening = await connectionEvent('open');
    const duplicateOpen = await connectionEvent('open');
    expect({ verification: [verification.status, verification.body.code], connecting: [connecting.status, connecting.body.code], opening: [opening.status, opening.body.code], duplicateOpen: [duplicateOpen.status, duplicateOpen.body.code] }).toEqual({ verification: [200, undefined], connecting: [200, undefined], opening: [200, undefined], duplicateOpen: [200, undefined] });

    const recovered = await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: channel.id } });
    const recoveredAttempt = await prisma.channelRegistrationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: attemptId } });
    expect(recovered).toMatchObject({ status: 'CONECTADO', providerConnectionState: 'CONNECTED', ownNumberVerified: true, verifiedEpoch: channel.pairingEpoch, pairingEpoch: channel.pairingEpoch, receiptVerifiedAt: receiptBefore });
    expect(recoveredAttempt).toMatchObject({ id: attemptId, state: 'CONNECTED', stage: 'RECEIPT_VERIFIED', generation: channel.generation, pairingEpoch: channel.pairingEpoch, version: attemptBefore.version });
    expect(await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: identityBefore.id } })).toMatchObject({ currentChannelId: channel.id, buId: tenant.businessUnit.id });
    expect(await prisma.message.count({ where: { externalId: 'evo-recovery-receipt' } })).toBe(1);
  });

  it('does not revive a cancelled or wrong-generation/epoch attempt during connection recovery', async () => {
    await contratarCanalNaoOficial(prisma, tenant.mantenedora.id);
    const paired = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/nao-oficial/pair`)
      .send({ intent: 'INITIAL' }).expect(200);
    const channel = await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: paired.body.channelId } });
    const attemptId = channel.currentAttemptId!;
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/webhooks/evolution').set('x-leadshug-webhook-secret', EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_SECRET)
      .send({ event: 'messages.upsert', instance: channel.evolutionInstanceName, data: { key: { remoteJid: '5584999777666@s.whatsapp.net', fromMe: false, id: 'evo-stale-recovery-receipt' }, pushName: 'Cliente', message: { conversation: 'prova de recebimento' } } }).expect(200);
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/webhooks/evolution').set('x-leadshug-webhook-secret', EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_SECRET)
      .send({ event: 'connection.update', instance: channel.evolutionInstanceName, data: { state: 'connecting' } }).expect(200);

    const postOpen = () => request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/webhooks/evolution').set('x-leadshug-webhook-secret', EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_SECRET)
      .send({ event: 'connection.update', instance: channel.evolutionInstanceName, data: { state: 'open' } });
    await prisma.channelRegistrationAttempt.update({ where: { id: attemptId }, data: { state: 'CANCELLED', stage: 'CANCELLED' } });
    await postOpen().expect(409).expect(({ body }) => expect(body.code).toBe('ATTEMPT_STALE'));
    expect(await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: channel.id } })).toMatchObject({ status: 'CONECTANDO', providerConnectionState: 'CONNECTING', pairingEpoch: channel.pairingEpoch, generation: channel.generation });

    await prisma.channelRegistrationAttempt.update({ where: { id: attemptId }, data: { state: 'PENDING_RECEIPT', stage: 'MAPPED_PENDING_RECEIPT', generation: channel.generation + 1 } });
    await postOpen().expect(409).expect(({ body }) => expect(body.code).toBe('ATTEMPT_STALE'));
    await prisma.channelRegistrationAttempt.update({ where: { id: attemptId }, data: { generation: channel.generation, pairingEpoch: channel.pairingEpoch + 1 } });
    await postOpen().expect(409).expect(({ body }) => expect(body.code).toBe('ATTEMPT_STALE'));
    await prisma.channelRegistrationAttempt.update({ where: { id: attemptId }, data: { pairingEpoch: null } });
    await postOpen().expect(409).expect(({ body }) => expect(body.code).toBe('ATTEMPT_STALE'));
    expect(await prisma.channelRegistrationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: attemptId } })).toMatchObject({ state: 'PENDING_RECEIPT', generation: channel.generation, pairingEpoch: null });
    expect(await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: channel.id } })).toMatchObject({ status: 'CONECTANDO', providerConnectionState: 'CONNECTING', pairingEpoch: channel.pairingEpoch, generation: channel.generation });
  });

  it('fences a held old verification across verified RENEW and accepts the same inbound key only after current X verification', async () => {
    await contratarCanalNaoOficial(prisma, tenant.mantenedora.id);
    const phoneX = '5584999990000';
    const phoneY = '5584888000000';
    await prisma.businessUnit.update({ where: { id: tenant.businessUnit.id }, data: { phone: phoneX } });
    const oldName = 'renew-old-managed-instance';
    const { channel: oldChannel, identity: oldIdentity } = await criarCanalVerificado(prisma, tenant.businessUnit.id, 'NAO_OFICIAL', oldName, { requiresWindow: false, provedBusinessPhone: phoneX });
    evolutionFake.inventory.set(oldName, { name: oldName, ownerJid: `${phoneX}@s.whatsapp.net`, connectionStatus: 'open' });

    let releaseOldVerify!: () => void;
    let oldVerifyEntered!: () => void;
    const oldVerifyGate = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { releaseOldVerify = resolve; });
    const oldVerifyCall = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { oldVerifyEntered = resolve; });
    let releaseCleanup!: () => void;
    let cleanupEntered!: () => void;
    const cleanupGate = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { releaseCleanup = resolve; });
    const cleanupCall = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { cleanupEntered = resolve; });
    const originalConsult = evolutionFake.consultarInstancia.bind(evolutionFake);
    let restoreRealtimePublish: (() => void) | undefined;
    let oldConsultCount = 0;
    evolutionFake.consultarInstancia = async (name) => {
      if (name === oldName) {
        const call = oldConsultCount++;
        if (call === 0) {
          oldVerifyEntered();
          await oldVerifyGate;
          return { name, ownerJid: `${phoneX}@s.whatsapp.net`, connectionStatus: 'open' };
        }
        if (call === 1) {
          cleanupEntered();
          await cleanupGate;
        }
      }
      return originalConsult(name);
    };
    const oldVerifyRequest = bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/nao-oficial/verify`)
      .then((response) => response);
    await oldVerifyCall;
    try {
      await prisma.buChannel.update({ where: { id: oldChannel.id }, data: { operationLeaseUntil: new Date(Date.now() - 1_000) } });
      evolutionFake.ownerPhone = phoneY;
      const renewRequest = bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/nao-oficial/pair`)
        .send({ intent: 'RENEW', expectedGeneration: oldChannel.generation }).then((response) => response);
      await cleanupCall;

      const reserved = await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: oldChannel.id } });
      expect(reserved).toMatchObject({ pairingEpoch: oldChannel.pairingEpoch + 1, generation: oldChannel.generation + 1, ownNumberVerified: false, verifiedEpoch: null, providerConnectionState: 'PENDING', receiptVerifiedAt: null, pendingCleanupInstanceName: oldName });
      expect(reserved.evolutionInstanceName).not.toBe(oldName);
      expect(evolutionFake.inventory.has(reserved.evolutionInstanceName!)).toBe(false);
      expect(evolutionFake.inventory.has(oldName)).toBe(true);
      expect(await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: oldIdentity.id } }))
        .toMatchObject({ currentChannelId: null, cleanupState: 'CLEANUP_PENDING' });

      releaseCleanup();
      const renewed = await renewRequest;
      expect(renewed.status).toBe(200);
      expect(renewed.body).toMatchObject({ channelId: oldChannel.id, generation: reserved.generation, pairingEpoch: reserved.pairingEpoch, state: 'CLEANUP_COMPLETED_RETRY_PAIR' });
      const currentAfterRenew = await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: oldChannel.id } });
      expect(currentAfterRenew).toMatchObject({ pairingEpoch: reserved.pairingEpoch, generation: reserved.generation, ownNumberVerified: false, verifiedEpoch: null, providerConnectionState: 'PENDING', receiptVerifiedAt: null, pendingCleanupInstanceName: null });
      expect(currentAfterRenew.evolutionInstanceName).toBe(reserved.evolutionInstanceName);
      expect(evolutionFake.inventory.has(currentAfterRenew.evolutionInstanceName!)).toBe(false);
      expect(await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: oldIdentity.id } })).toMatchObject({ currentChannelId: null, cleanupState: 'CLEANED' });
      const newIdentity = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { provider_externalResourceId: { provider: 'NAO_OFICIAL', externalResourceId: currentAfterRenew.evolutionInstanceName! } } });
      expect(newIdentity).toMatchObject({ buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, currentChannelId: null, businessIdentityId: null, unverifiedStaging: true, managedLifecycle: true });

      releaseOldVerify();
      const staleVerify = await oldVerifyRequest;
      expect(staleVerify.status).toBe(409);
      expect(staleVerify.body.code).toBe('CHANNEL_OPERATION_STALE');
      expect(await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: oldChannel.id } })).toMatchObject({ pairingEpoch: currentAfterRenew.pairingEpoch, ownNumberVerified: false, verifiedEpoch: null, providerConnectionState: 'PENDING' });

      const staleContent = { event: 'messages.upsert', instance: oldName, data: {
        key: { remoteJid: `${phoneX}@s.whatsapp.net`, fromMe: false, id: 'renew-same-key-after-current-proof' },
        pushName: 'Cliente', message: { conversation: 'private old epoch content' },
      } };
      const publish = vi.spyOn(app.get(RealtimeService), 'publicarNaBu');
      restoreRealtimePublish = () => publish.mockRestore();
      await request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/webhooks/evolution').set('x-leadshug-webhook-secret', EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_SECRET).send(staleContent).expect(200);
      expect(publish).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      const currentName = currentAfterRenew.evolutionInstanceName!;
      const newEpochOpenWithOldWuid = { event: 'connection.update', instance: currentName, data: { state: 'open', wuid: `${phoneX}@s.whatsapp.net` } };
      await request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/webhooks/evolution').set('x-leadshug-webhook-secret', EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_SECRET).send(newEpochOpenWithOldWuid).expect(200);
      const unverifiedChannel = await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: oldChannel.id } });
      expect(unverifiedChannel).toMatchObject({ pairingEpoch: currentAfterRenew.pairingEpoch, status: 'CONECTANDO', providerConnectionState: 'CONNECTED', ownNumberVerified: false, verifiedEpoch: null, receiptVerifiedAt: null });

      const resumed = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/nao-oficial/pair`)
        .send({ intent: 'RENEW', expectedGeneration: currentAfterRenew.generation });
      expect(resumed.status).toBe(409);
      expect(resumed.body.code).toBe('IDENTITY_QUARANTINED');
      expect(evolutionFake.inventory.get(currentName)).toMatchObject({ ownerJid: `${phoneY}@s.whatsapp.net`, connectionStatus: 'open' });
      const quarantined = await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: oldChannel.id } });
      expect(quarantined).toMatchObject({ pairingEpoch: currentAfterRenew.pairingEpoch, generation: currentAfterRenew.generation, ownNumberVerified: false, verifiedEpoch: null, providerConnectionState: 'IDENTITY_QUARANTINED' });
      expect(await prisma.channelRegistrationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: quarantined.currentAttemptId! } }))
        .toMatchObject({ state: 'ERROR', stage: 'IDENTITY_QUARANTINED', safeErrorCode: 'IDENTITY_QUARANTINED' });

      await request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/webhooks/evolution').set('x-leadshug-webhook-secret', EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_SECRET).send(newEpochOpenWithOldWuid).expect(200);
      expect(await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: oldChannel.id } }))
        .toMatchObject({ pairingEpoch: currentAfterRenew.pairingEpoch, status: 'CONECTANDO', ownNumberVerified: false, verifiedEpoch: null, receiptVerifiedAt: null });

      const rejected = await request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/webhooks/evolution').set('x-leadshug-webhook-secret', EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_SECRET).send({ ...staleContent, instance: currentName });
      expect(rejected.status).toBe(503);
      expect(rejected.body.code).toBe('PENDING_IDENTITY');
      expect(await prisma.message.count({ where: { externalId: 'renew-same-key-after-current-proof' } })).toBe(0);
      expect(await prisma.conversation.count({ where: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id } })).toBe(0);
      expect(typebotFake.starts).toBe(0);
      expect(publish).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      const pendingMetadata = await prisma.webhookEvent.findMany({ where: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, eventType: 'messages.upsert' } });
      expect(pendingMetadata.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
      expect(JSON.stringify(pendingMetadata)).not.toContain('private old epoch content');
      expect(JSON.stringify(pendingMetadata)).not.toContain(`${phoneX}@s.whatsapp.net`);

      evolutionFake.inventory.set(currentName, { name: currentName, ownerJid: `${phoneX}@s.whatsapp.net`, connectionStatus: 'open' });
      await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/nao-oficial/verify`).expect(200);
      expect(await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: oldChannel.id } })).toMatchObject({ pairingEpoch: currentAfterRenew.pairingEpoch, ownNumberVerified: true, verifiedEpoch: currentAfterRenew.pairingEpoch, providerConnectionState: 'CONNECTED' });
      await request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/webhooks/evolution').set('x-leadshug-webhook-secret', EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_SECRET)
        .send({ ...staleContent, instance: currentName }).expect(200);
      const accepted = await prisma.message.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { providerResourceIdentityId: newIdentity.id, externalId: 'renew-same-key-after-current-proof' } });
      expect(accepted).toMatchObject({ direction: 'IN', content: 'private old epoch content' });
      expect(await prisma.message.count({ where: { providerResourceIdentityId: newIdentity.id, externalId: 'renew-same-key-after-current-proof' } })).toBe(1);
      expect(await prisma.conversation.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, contact: phoneX } })).toMatchObject({ unreadCount: 1 });
      expect(publish).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
      expect(publish.mock.calls[0][1]).toMatchObject({ direcao: 'IN', naoLidas: 1 });
      expect(typebotFake.starts).toBe(0);
    } finally {
      restoreRealtimePublish?.();
      releaseOldVerify();
      releaseCleanup();
      evolutionFake.consultarInstancia = originalConsult;
    }
  });

  it('rejects a pre-identity IN without content, then accepts its same key after owner verification', async () => {
    await contratarCanalNaoOficial(prisma, tenant.mantenedora.id);
    let registeredName = '';
    const early = { event: 'messages.upsert', data: {
      key: { remoteJid: '5584999777666@s.whatsapp.net', fromMe: false, id: 'evo-preidentity-retry-key' },
      pushName: 'Cliente', message: { conversation: 'private pre-identity content' },
    } };
    evolutionFake.createHook = async (name) => {
      registeredName = name;
      const rejected = await request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/webhooks/evolution')
        .set('x-leadshug-webhook-secret', EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_SECRET)
        .send({ ...early, instance: name });
      expect(rejected.status).toBe(503);
      expect(await prisma.message.count()).toBe(0);
      expect(await prisma.conversation.count()).toBe(0);
      const diagnostic = await prisma.webhookEvent.findFirstOrThrow();
      expect(JSON.stringify(diagnostic.payload)).not.toContain('private pre-identity content');
      expect(diagnostic.payload).toMatchObject({ state: 'PENDING_IDENTITY' });
      evolutionFake.inventory.set(name, { name, ownerJid: '5584999990000@s.whatsapp.net', connectionStatus: 'open' });
    };
    const paired = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/nao-oficial/pair`)
      .send({ intent: 'INITIAL' }).expect(200);
    evolutionFake.createHook = null;
    expect(paired.body.state).toBe('PENDING_RECEIPT');
    expect(registeredName).toBeTruthy();
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/webhooks/evolution')
      .set('x-leadshug-webhook-secret', EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_SECRET)
      .send({ ...early, instance: registeredName }).expect(200);
    expect(await prisma.message.count({ where: { externalId: 'evo-preidentity-retry-key' } })).toBe(1);
    const conversation = await prisma.conversation.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, contact: '5584999777666' } });
    expect(conversation.unreadCount).toBe(1);
  });

  it('rejects a divergent Evolution owner phone before accepting content', async () => {
    await contratarCanalNaoOficial(prisma, tenant.mantenedora.id);
    const paired = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/nao-oficial/pair`)
      .send({ intent: 'INITIAL' }).expect(200);
    const channel = await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: paired.body.channelId } });
    expect(channel.ownNumberVerified).toBe(true);
    evolutionFake.inventory.set(channel.evolutionInstanceName!, { name: channel.evolutionInstanceName!, ownerJid: '5584990000111@s.whatsapp.net', connectionStatus: 'open' });
    const verification = await bff('post', `/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/NAO_OFICIAL/verify`).send({}).expect(409);
    expect(verification.body.code).toBe('IDENTITY_QUARANTINED');
    const quarantined = await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: channel.id } });
    expect(quarantined).toMatchObject({ ownNumberVerified: false, providerConnectionState: 'IDENTITY_QUARANTINED' });
    expect(await prisma.channelRegistrationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: quarantined.currentAttemptId! } }))
      .toMatchObject({ state: 'ERROR', stage: 'IDENTITY_QUARANTINED', safeErrorCode: 'IDENTITY_QUARANTINED' });
    const webhook = { event: 'messages.upsert', instance: channel.evolutionInstanceName, data: {
      key: { remoteJid: '5584999777666@s.whatsapp.net', fromMe: false, id: 'evo-divergent-owner-content' },
      pushName: 'Cliente', message: { conversation: 'must not be accepted' },
    } };
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/webhooks/evolution').set('x-leadshug-webhook-secret', EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_SECRET).send(webhook).expect(503);
    expect(await prisma.message.count()).toBe(0);
    expect(await prisma.conversation.count()).toBe(0);
  });

  it('rejects a pair POST without CSRF before calling Evolution', async () => {
    await contratarCanalNaoOficial(prisma, tenant.mantenedora.id);
    const response = await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/business-units/${tenant.businessUnit.id}/channels/nao-oficial/pair`)
      .set('Cookie', cookie).send({ intent: 'INITIAL' });
    expect(response.status).toBe(403);
    expect(evolutionFake.providerCalls).toBe(0);
    expect(await prisma.buChannel.count({ where: { buId: tenant.businessUnit.id } })).toBe(0);
  });
});

import { INestApplication } from '@nestjs/common';
import { createHmac } from 'node:crypto';
import request from 'supertest';
import { afterAll, beforeAll, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { PrismaService } from '../src/infra/prisma/prisma.service';
import { RealtimeService } from '../src/realtime/realtime.service';
import {
  META_APP_SECRET,
  EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_SECRET,
  contratarCanalNaoOficial,
  tetoComCanalNaoOficial,
  criarCanalVerificado,
  criarMantenedora,
  criarUsuario,
  evolutionFake,
  limparBanco,
  login,
  metaFake,
  typebotFake,
  subirApp,
  criarConversaFixture,
  criarRotaConversaFixture,
  obterSelecaoRespostaV2,
} from './helpers';
import { lerCursorTimeline } from '../src/domain/inbox/timeline-pagination';

describe('atendimento', () => {
  let app: INestApplication;
  let prisma: PrismaService;
  let A: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof criarMantenedora>>;
  let B: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof criarMantenedora>>;
  let cookieOwner: string;
  let conversaId: string;

  const PHONE_A = 'phone-alfa';
  const CONTATO = '5584911112222';

  const receber = async (texto: string, phoneNumberId = PHONE_A) => {
    const corpo = {
      entry: [
        {
          changes: [
            {
              field: 'messages',
              value: {
                metadata: { phone_number_id: phoneNumberId },
                contacts: [{ profile: { name: 'Cliente' } }],
                messages: [{ id: `wamid-${texto}`, from: CONTATO, timestamp: String(Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000)), type: 'text', text: { body: texto } }],
              },
            },
          ],
        },
      ],
    };
    await request(app.getHttpServer())
      .post('/webhooks/meta')
      .set('content-type', 'application/json')
      .set('x-hub-signature-256', 'sha256=' + createHmac('sha256', META_APP_SECRET).update(JSON.stringify(corpo)).digest('hex'))
      .send(corpo)
      .expect(200);
  };

  const responder = async (cookie: string, id: string, texto = 'oi, tudo bem?', tipo: 'OFICIAL' | 'NAO_OFICIAL' = 'OFICIAL') => {
    const conversation = await prisma.conversation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id }, select: { buId: true } });
    const channel = await prisma.buChannel.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { buId: conversation.buId, type: tipo } });
    const tuple = await obterSelecaoRespostaV2(app, id, cookie, channel.id);
    return { request: request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/conversations/${id}/messages`)
      .set('Cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').send({ texto, ...tuple }) };
  };

  beforeAll(async () => {
    tetoComCanalNaoOficial();
    ({ app, prisma } = await subirApp());
  });

  afterAll(async () => {
    await limparBanco(prisma);
    await app?.close();
  });

  beforeEach(async () => {
    await limparBanco(prisma);
    metaFake.enviados = [];
    evolutionFake.enviados = [];
    A = await criarMantenedora(app, 'Alfa', 'dono@alfa.test');
    B = await criarMantenedora(app, 'Beta', 'dono@beta.test');
    await criarCanalVerificado(prisma, A.businessUnit.id, 'OFICIAL', PHONE_A, { requiresWindow: true, provedWabaId: 'synthetic-atendimento-current-waba' });
    cookieOwner = await login(app, 'dono@alfa.test');
    await receber('oi');
    conversaId = (await prisma.conversation.findFirstOrThrow()).id;
  });

  it('registra resultado de envio ambíguo sem chave externa e nunca inventa uma chave', async () => {
    metaFake.sendIdNull = true;
    const beforeSends = metaFake.enviados.length;
    await (await responder(cookieOwner, conversaId)).request.expect(409).expect(({ body }) => {
      expect(body).toMatchObject({ code: 'EXTERNAL_MESSAGE_ID_MISSING', outcome: 'AMBIGUOUS_SEND_RECORDED' });
      expect(body.error).toMatch(/Verifique a conversa antes de tentar novamente/);
    });
    expect(metaFake.enviados.length).toBe(beforeSends + 1);
    const failed = await prisma.message.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { conversationId: conversaId, direction: 'OUT', status: 'FALHOU', externalId: null } });
    expect(failed.providerResourceIdentityId).toBeTruthy();
    expect(failed.content).toBe('oi, tudo bem?');
    metaFake.sendIdNull = false;
  });

  it('reconcilia o eco Evolution autenticado entregue dentro do send sem unread duplicada', async () => {
    const geral = await prisma.setor.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { mantenedoraId: A.mantenedora.id } });
    const bu = await prisma.businessUnit.create({ data: { mantenedoraId: A.mantenedora.id, setorId: geral.id, name: 'Número echo Evolution' } });
    const evolution = await criarCanalVerificado(prisma, bu.id, 'NAO_OFICIAL', 'inst-echo-evolution', { requiresWindow: false });
    await prisma.userBuRole.create({ data: { userId: A.owner.id, buId: bu.id, role: 'OWNER', grantedViaTipo: 'MANTENEDORA', grantedViaId: A.mantenedora.id } });
    const contact = '5584933334555';
    const conversation = await criarConversaFixture(prisma, { buId: bu.id, contact, contactType: 'PESSOA', status: 'IN_SERVICE', unreadCount: 3 });
    await criarRotaConversaFixture(prisma, { conversationId: conversation.id, channelId: evolution.channel.id });
    await prisma.message.create({ data: { conversationId: conversation.id, buId: bu.id, direction: 'IN', messageType: 'TEXTO', content: 'anterior', channelOrigin: 'NAO_OFICIAL', status: 'RECEBIDA' } });
    let echoRow: { id: string; createdAt: Date } | null = null;
    evolutionFake.sendHook = async (id, para, texto) => {
      const webhook = { event: 'messages.upsert', instance: 'inst-echo-evolution', data: {
        key: { remoteJid: `${para}@s.whatsapp.net`, fromMe: true, id }, message: { conversation: `${texto} echo-provider` },
      } };
      await request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/webhooks/evolution').set('x-leadshug-webhook-secret', EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_SECRET).send(webhook).expect(200);
      echoRow = await prisma.message.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { providerResourceIdentity: { externalResourceId: 'inst-echo-evolution' }, externalId: id }, select: { id: true, createdAt: true } });
    };
    await (await responder(cookieOwner, conversation.id, 'trusted sender body', 'NAO_OFICIAL')).request.expect(201);
    evolutionFake.sendHook = null;
    const outbound = await prisma.message.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { externalId: 'evo-enviado-1' } });
    expect(outbound.id).toBe(echoRow?.id);
    expect(outbound.createdAt).toEqual(echoRow?.createdAt);
    expect(outbound).toMatchObject({ direction: 'OUT', content: 'trusted sender body', authorName: 'Dono' });
    expect(await prisma.message.count({ where: { externalId: 'evo-enviado-1' } })).toBe(1);
    expect((await prisma.conversation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: conversation.id } })).unreadCount).toBe(3);
  });

  it('reconciles concurrent authenticated same-key OUT echoes before sender enrichment', async () => {
    await contratarCanalNaoOficial(prisma, A.mantenedora.id);
    const setor = await prisma.businessUnit.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: A.businessUnit.id }, select: { setorId: true } });
    const bu = await prisma.businessUnit.create({ data: { mantenedoraId: A.mantenedora.id, setorId: setor.setorId, name: 'Número concurrent OUT echo' } });
    const instance = 'inst-concurrent-out-echo';
    const { channel, identity } = await criarCanalVerificado(prisma, bu.id, 'NAO_OFICIAL', instance, { requiresWindow: false });
    await prisma.userBuRole.create({ data: { userId: A.owner.id, buId: bu.id, role: 'OWNER', grantedViaTipo: 'MANTENEDORA', grantedViaId: A.mantenedora.id } });
    const conversation = await criarConversaFixture(prisma, { buId: bu.id, contact: CONTATO, contactType: 'PESSOA', status: 'IN_SERVICE', unreadCount: 3 });
    await criarRotaConversaFixture(prisma, { conversationId: conversation.id, channelId: channel.id });
    await prisma.message.create({ data: { conversationId: conversation.id, buId: bu.id, direction: 'IN', messageType: 'TEXTO', content: 'prior inbound', channelOrigin: 'NAO_OFICIAL', status: 'RECEBIDA' } });
    typebotFake.enabled = true;
    const bot = await prisma.typebotBot.create({ data: { mantenedoraId: A.mantenedora.id, typebotId: 'out-echo-bot', publishedTypebotId: 'out-echo-published', status: 'ATIVO', name: 'OUT echo bot', editorUrl: 'https://typebot.invalid/out-echo' } });
    await prisma.typebotTrigger.create({ data: { botId: bot.id, kind: 'NOVA_CONVERSA', value: '*', buId: bu.id } });
    const publish = vi.spyOn(app.get(RealtimeService), 'publicarNaBu');
    const echoRows: Array<{ id: string; createdAt: Date }> = [];
    evolutionFake.sendHook = async (id, para, texto) => {
      const webhook = { event: 'messages.upsert', instance, data: {
        key: { remoteJid: `${para}@s.whatsapp.net`, fromMe: true, id }, message: { conversation: `${texto} provider echo` },
      } };
      await Promise.all([0, 1].map(async () => {
        await request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/webhooks/evolution')
          .set('x-leadshug-webhook-secret', EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_SECRET).send(webhook).expect(200);
        const accepted = await prisma.message.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { providerResourceIdentityId: identity.id, externalId: id }, select: { id: true, createdAt: true } });
        echoRows.push(accepted);
      }));
    };

    await (await responder(cookieOwner, conversation.id, 'trusted sender body', 'NAO_OFICIAL')).request.expect(201);
    evolutionFake.sendHook = null;

    const sent = evolutionFake.enviados.filter((entry) => entry.para === CONTATO && entry.texto === 'trusted sender body');
    expect(sent).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(echoRows).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(echoRows[0]).toEqual(echoRows[1]);
    const outbound = await prisma.message.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { providerResourceIdentityId: identity.id, externalId: 'evo-enviado-1' } });
    expect(outbound).toMatchObject({ id: echoRows[0].id, createdAt: echoRows[0].createdAt, direction: 'OUT', content: 'trusted sender body', authorName: 'Dono' });
    expect(await prisma.message.count({ where: { providerResourceIdentityId: identity.id, externalId: 'evo-enviado-1' } })).toBe(1);
    expect(await prisma.message.count({ where: { conversationId: conversation.id, direction: 'IN' } })).toBe(1);
    expect(await prisma.conversation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: conversation.id } })).toMatchObject({ unreadCount: 3 });
    expect(typebotFake.starts).toBe(0);
    expect(publish).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(publish.mock.calls[0][1]).toMatchObject({ direcao: 'OUT', canal: 'NAO_OFICIAL' });
  });

  it('does not accept an outbound result after channel retirement wins during the provider response', async () => {
    const geral = await prisma.setor.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { mantenedoraId: A.mantenedora.id } });
    const bu = await prisma.businessUnit.create({ data: { mantenedoraId: A.mantenedora.id, setorId: geral.id, name: 'Número retirement fence' } });
    const { channel, identity } = await criarCanalVerificado(prisma, bu.id, 'NAO_OFICIAL', 'inst-retirement-fence', { requiresWindow: false });
    await prisma.userBuRole.create({ data: { userId: A.owner.id, buId: bu.id, role: 'OWNER', grantedViaTipo: 'MANTENEDORA', grantedViaId: A.mantenedora.id } });
    const conversation = await criarConversaFixture(prisma, { buId: bu.id, contact: CONTATO, contactType: 'PESSOA', status: 'QUEUED' });
    await criarRotaConversaFixture(prisma, { conversationId: conversation.id, channelId: channel.id });
    await prisma.message.create({ data: { conversationId: conversation.id, buId: bu.id, direction: 'IN', messageType: 'TEXTO',
      content: 'pre-existing inbound conversation history', channelOrigin: 'NAO_OFICIAL', status: 'RECEBIDA' } });
    let entered!: () => void; let release!: () => void;
    const atProvider = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { entered = resolve; });
    const gate = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { release = resolve; });
    evolutionFake.sendHook = async () => { entered(); await gate; };
    const pending = (await responder(cookieOwner, conversation.id, 'request while channel is retired', 'NAO_OFICIAL')).request;
    const result = pending.then((response) => response);
    await atProvider;
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).delete(`/api/bff/business-units/${bu.id}/channels/NAO_OFICIAL`)
      .set('Cookie', cookieOwner).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').expect(204);
    release();
    evolutionFake.sendHook = null;
    const response = await result;
    expect(response.status).toBe(409);
    expect(evolutionFake.enviados).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(await prisma.message.count({ where: { conversationId: conversation.id, direction: 'OUT' } })).toBe(0);
    expect(await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: identity.id } })).toMatchObject({ currentChannelId: null, cleanupState: 'CLEANED' });
    expect(channel.evolutionInstanceName).toBe('inst-retirement-fence');
  });

  it('serializes verified inbound acceptance behind retirement on the same identity row', async () => {
    await contratarCanalNaoOficial(prisma, A.mantenedora.id);
    const { channel, identity } = await criarCanalVerificado(prisma, A.businessUnit.id, 'NAO_OFICIAL', 'inst-lock-order-retirement', { requiresWindow: false });
    let release!: () => void; let entered!: () => void;
    const released = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { release = resolve; });
    const locked = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { entered = resolve; });
    const blocker = prisma.$transaction(async (tx) => {
      await tx.$queryRaw`SELECT id FROM provider_resource_identities WHERE id = ${identity.id} FOR UPDATE`;
      entered();
      await released;
    });
    await locked;
    let lockWaiters = 0;
    const waitForWaiters = async (target: number) => {
      const deadline = Date.now() + 5_000;
      while (Date.now() < deadline) {
        const rows = await prisma.$queryRaw<Array<{ count: number }>>`SELECT count(*)::int AS count FROM pg_stat_activity WHERE wait_event_type = 'Lock' AND query ILIKE '%provider_resource_identities%' AND pid <> pg_backend_pid()`;
        lockWaiters = Number(rows[0]?.count ?? 0);
        if (lockWaiters >= target) return;
        await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 20));
      }
      throw new Error(`timed out waiting for ${target} identity-row waiters; saw ${lockWaiters}`);
    };
    const unlinkRequest = request(app.getHttpServer()).delete(`/api/bff/business-units/${A.businessUnit.id}/channels/NAO_OFICIAL`)
      .set('Cookie', cookieOwner).set('x-requested-with', 'teste');
    const unlinkResult = unlinkRequest.then((response) => response);
    try {
      await waitForWaiters(1);
      const webhook = { event: 'messages.upsert', instance: channel.evolutionInstanceName, data: {
        key: { remoteJid: `${CONTATO}@s.whatsapp.net`, fromMe: false, id: 'evo-retirement-lock-race' }, pushName: 'Cliente', message: { conversation: 'must not survive retirement' },
      } };
      const ingress = request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/webhooks/evolution').set('x-leadshug-webhook-secret', EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_SECRET).send(webhook);
      const ingressResult = ingress.then((response) => response);
      await waitForWaiters(2);
      release();
      await blocker;
      const [unlinked, accepted] = await Promise.all([unlinkResult, ingressResult]);
      expect(unlinked.status).toBe(204);
      expect([200, 409]).toContain(accepted.status);
      expect(await prisma.message.count({ where: { providerResourceIdentityId: identity.id, externalId: 'evo-retirement-lock-race' } })).toBe(0);
      expect(await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: identity.id } })).toMatchObject({ currentChannelId: null, cleanupState: 'CLEANED' });
    } finally {
      release();
      await blocker;
    }
  });

  it('serializes old-instance ingress against a real managed RENEW and rejects stale content', async () => {
    await contratarCanalNaoOficial(prisma, A.mantenedora.id);
    const { channel, identity } = await criarCanalVerificado(prisma, A.businessUnit.id, 'NAO_OFICIAL', 'inst-lock-order-renewal', { requiresWindow: false });
    let release!: () => void; let entered!: () => void;
    const released = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { release = resolve; });
    const locked = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { entered = resolve; });
    const blocker = prisma.$transaction(async (tx) => {
      await tx.$queryRaw`SELECT id FROM provider_resource_identities WHERE id = ${identity.id} FOR UPDATE`;
      entered(); await released;
    }, { timeout: 30_000 });
    await locked;
    let lockWaiters = 0;
    const waitForWaiters = async (target: number) => {
      const deadline = Date.now() + 3_000;
      while (Date.now() < deadline) {
        const rows = await prisma.$queryRaw<Array<{ count: number }>>`SELECT count(*)::int AS count FROM pg_stat_activity WHERE wait_event_type = 'Lock' AND query ILIKE '%provider_resource_identities%' AND pid <> pg_backend_pid()`;
        lockWaiters = Number(rows[0]?.count ?? 0);
        if (lockWaiters >= target) return;
        await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 20));
      }
      const activity = await prisma.$queryRaw<Array<{ query: string; wait_event_type: string | null; wait_event: string | null }>>`SELECT query, wait_event_type, wait_event FROM pg_stat_activity WHERE pid <> pg_backend_pid() AND (query ILIKE '%provider_resource_identities%' OR query ILIKE '%bu_channels%')`;
      throw new Error(`timed out waiting for ${target} identity-row waiters; saw ${lockWaiters}; activity=${JSON.stringify(activity)}`);
    };
    const renewal = request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/business-units/${A.businessUnit.id}/channels/nao-oficial/pair`)
      .set('Cookie', cookieOwner).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').send({ intent: 'RENEW', expectedGeneration: channel.generation });
    let renewalResponse: { status: number; body: Record<string, unknown> } | null = null;
    const renewalResult = renewal.then((response) => { renewalResponse = response; return response; });
    try {
      await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 100));
      if (renewalResponse) throw new Error(`RENEW returned before the identity fence: ${renewalResponse.status} ${JSON.stringify(renewalResponse.body)}`);
      await waitForWaiters(1);
      const webhook = { event: 'messages.upsert', instance: channel.evolutionInstanceName, data: {
        key: { remoteJid: `${CONTATO}@s.whatsapp.net`, fromMe: false, id: 'evo-renewal-lock-race' }, pushName: 'Cliente', message: { conversation: 'stale old-instance message' },
      } };
      const ingress = request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/webhooks/evolution').set('x-leadshug-webhook-secret', EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_SECRET).send(webhook);
      const ingressResult = ingress.then((response) => response);
      await waitForWaiters(2);
      release(); await blocker;
      const [renewed, accepted] = await Promise.all([renewalResult, ingressResult]);
      expect(renewed.status).toBe(200);
      expect(renewed.body.state).toBe('CLEANUP_COMPLETED_RETRY_PAIR');
      expect([200, 409]).toContain(accepted.status);
      const current = await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: channel.id } });
      expect(current.pairingEpoch).toBe(channel.pairingEpoch + 1);
      expect(current.evolutionInstanceName).not.toBe(channel.evolutionInstanceName);
      expect(await prisma.message.count({ where: { providerResourceIdentityId: identity.id, externalId: 'evo-renewal-lock-race' } })).toBe(0);
      expect(await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: identity.id } })).toMatchObject({ currentChannelId: null, cleanupState: 'CLEANED' });
    } finally {
      release(); await blocker.catch(() => undefined);
    }
  });

  describe('POST conversations/:id/messages — o trio (ADR-0012 §2)', () => {
    it('autorizado: responde, envia pelo canal e assume a conversa', async () => {
      const res = await (await responder(cookieOwner, conversaId)).request.expect(201);
      expect(res.body.canal).toBe('OFICIAL');
      expect(metaFake.enviados).toEqual([{ para: CONTATO, texto: 'oi, tudo bem?' }]);

      const conversa = await prisma.conversation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: conversaId } });
      expect(conversa.status).toBe('IN_SERVICE');
      expect(conversa.attendantOrigin).toBe('INTERNO');
    });

    // Os dois testes que aqui provavam CAPACIDADE_AUSENTE e "VIEWER lê mas não
    // responde" SUMIRAM com o ADR-0018: o eixo de capacidades foi dissolvido e o
    // papel VIEWER deixou de existir. Não é cobertura perdida — é comportamento
    // que o produto não tem mais. A dívida está escrita no ADR §Negativas: quem
    // precisa acompanhar sem responder passa a ser ATENDENTE, e nada o impede de
    // responder; o substituto é o histórico de atividades (ADR-0020).

    it('ATENDENTE no escopo responde — estar na BU BASTA (ADR-0018 §3)', async () => {
      await criarUsuario(prisma, A.mantenedora.id, A.businessUnit.id, 'atendente@alfa.test', 'ATENDENTE');
      const cookie = await login(app, 'atendente@alfa.test');

      await (await responder(cookie, conversaId)).request.expect(201);
      expect(metaFake.enviados).toHaveLength(1);
    });

    it('CROSS-TENANT: o dono de Beta recebe 404 na conversa da BU de Alfa', async () => {
      const cookieBeta = await login(app, 'dono@beta.test');
      const res = await request(app.getHttpServer()).post(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversaId}/messages`)
        .set('Cookie', cookieBeta).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').send({ texto: 'oi, tudo bem?' }).expect(404);
      expect(res.body.code).toBe('NAO_ENCONTRADO');
      expect(metaFake.enviados).toHaveLength(0);
      expect(B.businessUnit.id).toBeTruthy();
    });
  });

  describe('janela de 24h — atributo do CANAL (ADR-0009 §3)', () => {
    it('recusa com JANELA_FECHADA quando a última mensagem do contato passou de 24h', async () => {
      const expiredAt = new Date(Date.now() - 25 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
      await prisma.message.updateMany({
        where: { conversationId: conversaId, direction: 'IN' },
        data: { createdAt: expiredAt, officialInboundAt: expiredAt },
      });

      const res = await (await responder(cookieOwner, conversaId)).request.expect(409);
      expect(res.body.code).toBe('JANELA_FECHADA');
      expect(metaFake.enviados).toHaveLength(0);
    });

    it('a thread informa o estado da janela, para a tela saber que responder está indisponível', async () => {
      const expiredAt = new Date(Date.now() - 25 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
      await prisma.message.updateMany({
        where: { conversationId: conversaId, direction: 'IN' },
        data: { createdAt: expiredAt, officialInboundAt: expiredAt },
      });
      const res = await request(app.getHttpServer())
        .get(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversaId}/messages`).set('Cookie', cookieOwner).expect(200);

      expect(res.body.janela).toMatchObject({ exigida: true, aberta: false, canal: 'OFICIAL' });
    });

    it('no canal NÃO oficial não existe janela: responde mesmo com a conversa velha', async () => {
      const geral = await prisma.setor.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { mantenedoraId: A.mantenedora.id } });
      const bu2 = await prisma.businessUnit.create({
        data: { mantenedoraId: A.mantenedora.id, setorId: geral.id, name: 'Número não oficial' },
      });
      const evolution = await criarCanalVerificado(prisma, bu2.id, 'NAO_OFICIAL', 'inst-alfa', { requiresWindow: false });
      await prisma.userBuRole.create({
        data: {
          userId: A.owner.id,
          buId: bu2.id,
          role: 'OWNER',
          grantedViaTipo: 'MANTENEDORA',
          grantedViaId: A.mantenedora.id,
        },
      });
      const conversa = await criarConversaFixture(prisma, { buId: bu2.id, contact: '5584933334444', contactType: 'PESSOA', status: 'QUEUED' });
      await criarRotaConversaFixture(prisma, { conversationId: conversa.id, channelId: evolution.channel.id });
      await prisma.message.create({
        data: {
          conversationId: conversa.id, buId: bu2.id, direction: 'IN', messageType: 'TEXTO',
          content: 'antiga', channelOrigin: 'NAO_OFICIAL', status: 'RECEBIDA',
          createdAt: new Date(Date.now() - 72 * 60 * 60 * 1000),
        },
      });

      await (await responder(cookieOwner, conversa.id, 'oi, tudo bem?', 'NAO_OFICIAL')).request.expect(201);
      expect(evolutionFake.enviados).toHaveLength(1);
      expect(metaFake.enviados).toHaveLength(0);
    });

  });

  describe('fila', () => {
    it('assumir trava para outro agente (409), e finalizar libera', async () => {
      await criarUsuario(prisma, A.mantenedora.id, A.businessUnit.id, 'outro@alfa.test', 'ATENDENTE');
      const cookieOutro = await login(app, 'outro@alfa.test');

      await request(app.getHttpServer())
        .post(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversaId}/claim`)
        .set('Cookie', cookieOwner).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').expect(201);

      const conflito = await request(app.getHttpServer())
        .post(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversaId}/claim`)
        .set('Cookie', cookieOutro).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').expect(409);
      expect(conflito.body.code).toBe('JA_ATENDIDA');

      await request(app.getHttpServer())
        .post(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversaId}/close`)
        .set('Cookie', cookieOwner).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').expect(201);

      await request(app.getHttpServer())
        .post(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversaId}/claim`)
        .set('Cookie', cookieOutro).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').expect(201);
    });

    it('responder NÃO rouba conversa de quem já está atendendo', async () => {
      await request(app.getHttpServer())
        .post(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversaId}/claim`)
        .set('Cookie', cookieOwner).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').expect(201);

      await criarUsuario(prisma, A.mantenedora.id, A.businessUnit.id, 'outro2@alfa.test', 'ATENDENTE');
      const cookieOutro = await login(app, 'outro2@alfa.test');
      await (await responder(cookieOutro, conversaId, 'me intrometi')).request.expect(201);

      const conversa = await prisma.conversation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: conversaId } });
      expect(conversa.attendantUserId).toBe(A.owner.id);
    });

    it('abrir a thread zera as não lidas — abrir É atender', async () => {
      await receber('mais uma');
      // O beforeEach já deixou a primeira mensagem recebida como não lida.
      expect((await prisma.conversation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: conversaId } })).unreadCount).toBe(2);

      await request(app.getHttpServer())
        .get(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversaId}/messages`).set('Cookie', cookieOwner).expect(200);

      expect((await prisma.conversation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: conversaId } })).unreadCount).toBe(0);
    });

    it('a lista filtra por aba e só enxerga a BU pedida', async () => {
      const res = await request(app.getHttpServer())
        .get(`/api/bff/business-units/${A.businessUnit.id}/conversations?aba=QUEUED`)
        .set('Cookie', cookieOwner).expect(200);
      expect(res.body.conversations).toHaveLength(1);

      const emAtendimento = await request(app.getHttpServer())
        .get(`/api/bff/business-units/${A.businessUnit.id}/conversations?aba=IN_SERVICE`)
        .set('Cookie', cookieOwner).expect(200);
      expect(emAtendimento.body.conversations).toHaveLength(0);
    });
  });

  describe('a inbox pagina — teto silencioso é o que vira "sumiram conversas"', () => {
    it('devolve total e temMais, e a segunda página traz o resto', async () => {
      for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
        await criarConversaFixture(prisma, {
            buId: A.businessUnit.id,
            contact: `55119000000${i}0`,
            contactType: 'PESSOA',
            status: 'QUEUED',
            lastMessageAt: new Date(Date.now() - i * 1000),
          });
      }

      const p1 = await request(app.getHttpServer())
        .get(`/api/bff/business-units/${A.businessUnit.id}/conversations?limite=2`)
        .set('Cookie', cookieOwner)
        .expect(200);

      expect(p1.body.conversations).toHaveLength(2);
      expect(p1.body.total).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(5);
      // O que faltava: a resposta DIZ que há mais. Antes o take era 200 fixo e o
      // corte era invisível.
      expect(p1.body.temMais).toBe(true);

      const p2 = await request(app.getHttpServer())
        .get(`/api/bff/business-units/${A.businessUnit.id}/conversations?limite=2&pagina=2`)
        .set('Cookie', cookieOwner)
        .expect(200);

      expect(p2.body.pagina).toBe(2);
      const ids1 = p1.body.conversations.map((c: { id: string }) => c.id);
      const ids2 = p2.body.conversations.map((c: { id: string }) => c.id);
      expect(ids1.some((id: string) => ids2.includes(id))).toBe(false);
    });

    it('limite acima do teto é aparado, não obedecido', async () => {
      const r = await request(app.getHttpServer())
        .get(`/api/bff/business-units/${A.businessUnit.id}/conversations?limite=99999`)
        .set('Cookie', cookieOwner)
        .expect(200);
      // Um cliente não escolhe quanto o banco carrega de uma vez.
      expect(r.body.conversations.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(200);
    });
  });

  describe('timeline paginada do atendimento', () => {
    it('caminha por 251 mensagens na ordem do banco e mantém a página limitada', async () => {
      const base = new Date('2026-01-01T12:00:00.000Z');
      const mensagens = Array.from({ length: 251 }, (_, i) => ({
        conversationId: conversaId,
        buId: A.businessUnit.id,
        direction: 'IN' as const,
        messageType: 'TEXTO' as const,
        content: `mensagem ${i}`,
        channelOrigin: 'OFICIAL' as const,
        status: 'RECEBIDA' as const,
        createdAt: new Date(base.getTime() + Math.floor(i / 7) * 1000),
      }));
      await prisma.message.deleteMany({ where: { conversationId: conversaId } });
      await prisma.message.createMany({ data: mensagens });
      const esperados = await prisma.message.findMany({
        where: { conversationId: conversaId }, orderBy: [{ createdAt: 'asc' }, { id: 'asc' }], select: { id: true },
      });

      const recebidos: string[] = [];
      let antes: string | null = null;
      do {
        const query = antes ? `?limite=37&marcarLida=false&antes=${encodeURIComponent(antes)}` : '?limite=37&marcarLida=false';
        const pagina = await request(app.getHttpServer())
          .get(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversaId}/messages${query}`).set('Cookie', cookieOwner).expect(200);
        expect(pagina.body.messages.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(37);
        expect(pagina.body.paginacao.limite).toBe(37);
        recebidos.unshift(...pagina.body.messages.map((m: { id: string }) => m.id));
        antes = pagina.body.paginacao.cursorAnterior;
        expect(pagina.body.paginacao.temAnteriores).toBe(Boolean(antes));
      } while (antes);

      expect(recebidos).toEqual(esperados.map((m) => m.id));
      expect((await prisma.conversation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: conversaId } })).unreadCount).toBe(1);
    });

    it('valida parâmetros depois da autorização e rejeita cursor de outra conversa autorizada', async () => {
      await prisma.message.create({ data: {
        conversationId: conversaId, buId: A.businessUnit.id, direction: 'IN', messageType: 'TEXTO',
        content: 'página de cursor', channelOrigin: 'OFICIAL', status: 'RECEBIDA',
        createdAt: new Date(Date.now() - 1000),
      } });
      const segunda = await criarConversaFixture(prisma, {
        buId: A.businessUnit.id, contact: '5584999999999', contactType: 'PESSOA', status: 'QUEUED', unreadCount: 3,
      });
      const cursorFonte = await request(app.getHttpServer())
        .get(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversaId}/messages?limite=1`).set('Cookie', cookieOwner).expect(200);
      const cursor = cursorFonte.body.paginacao.cursorAnterior;
      if (!cursor) throw new Error('A fixture precisa produzir continuação.');

      await request(app.getHttpServer())
        .get(`/api/bff/conversations/${segunda.id}/messages?antes=${encodeURIComponent(cursor)}&marcarLida=false`)
        .set('Cookie', cookieOwner).expect(400).expect(({ body }) => expect(body.code).toBe('PAGINACAO_INVALIDA'));
      expect((await prisma.conversation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: segunda.id } })).unreadCount).toBe(3);
      await receber('antes de cursor inválido');
      const anchor = lerCursorTimeline(cursor);
      await prisma.message.delete({ where: { id: anchor.mensagemId } });
      await request(app.getHttpServer())
        .get(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversaId}/messages?antes=${encodeURIComponent(cursor)}&marcarLida=false`)
        .set('Cookie', cookieOwner).expect(400).expect(({ body }) => expect(body.code).toBe('PAGINACAO_INVALIDA'));
      expect((await prisma.conversation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: conversaId } })).unreadCount).toBe(1);
      const conversaOutraMantenedora = await criarConversaFixture(prisma, {
        buId: B.businessUnit.id, contact: '5584666666666', contactType: 'PESSOA', status: 'QUEUED',
      });
      await request(app.getHttpServer())
        .get(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversaOutraMantenedora.id}/messages?limite=0&antes=x`)
        .set('Cookie', cookieOwner).expect(404);
      const setor = await prisma.setor.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { mantenedoraId: A.mantenedora.id } });
      const buSemGrant = await prisma.businessUnit.create({ data: {
        mantenedoraId: A.mantenedora.id, setorId: setor.id, name: 'BU sem grant',
      } });
      const conversaSemGrant = await criarConversaFixture(prisma, {
        buId: buSemGrant.id, contact: '5584777777777', contactType: 'PESSOA', status: 'QUEUED', unreadCount: 3,
      });
      await request(app.getHttpServer())
        .get(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversaSemGrant.id}/messages?limite=0&antes=x`)
        .set('Cookie', cookieOwner).expect(404);
      expect((await prisma.conversation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: conversaSemGrant.id } })).unreadCount).toBe(3);
      await request(app.getHttpServer())
        .get(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversaId}/messages?limite=0`)
        .set('Cookie', cookieOwner).expect(400).expect(({ body }) => expect(body.code).toBe('PAGINACAO_INVALIDA'));
      expect((await prisma.conversation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: conversaId } })).unreadCount).toBe(1);
    });

    it('exige sessão antes de validar query e não altera unread', async () => {
      await request(app.getHttpServer())
        .get(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversaId}/messages?limite=0&antes=x`).expect(401);
      expect((await prisma.conversation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: conversaId } })).unreadCount).toBe(1);
    });

    it('rejeita cursor/limite repetidos e mantém refresh e backfill sem efeito de leitura', async () => {
      await receber('entrada extra');
      const invalid = await request(app.getHttpServer())
        .get(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversaId}/messages?limite=1&limite=2`)
        .set('Cookie', cookieOwner).expect(400);
      expect(invalid.body.code).toBe('PAGINACAO_INVALIDA');
      await request(app.getHttpServer())
        .get(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversaId}/messages?antes=a&antes=b`)
        .set('Cookie', cookieOwner).expect(400);
      expect((await prisma.conversation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: conversaId } })).unreadCount).toBe(2);

      const aberta = await request(app.getHttpServer())
        .get(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversaId}/messages?limite=1`).set('Cookie', cookieOwner).expect(200);
      expect((await prisma.conversation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: conversaId } })).unreadCount).toBe(0);
      const cursor = aberta.body.paginacao.cursorAnterior;
      if (cursor) {
        await request(app.getHttpServer())
          .get(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversaId}/messages?antes=${encodeURIComponent(cursor)}`)
          .set('Cookie', cookieOwner).expect(200);
      }
      await receber('nova durante leitura');
      await request(app.getHttpServer())
        .get(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversaId}/messages?marcarLida=false`)
        .set('Cookie', cookieOwner).expect(200);
      expect((await prisma.conversation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: conversaId } })).unreadCount).toBe(1);
    });

    it('serializa dez chegadas concorrentes com a fronteira de abertura em três lotes', async () => {
      for (let lote = 0; lote < 3; lote++) {
        await request(app.getHttpServer())
          .get(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversaId}/messages`).set('Cookie', cookieOwner).expect(200);
        const chegadas = Array.from({ length: 10 }, (_, i) => receber(`concorrente-${lote}-${i}`));
        const abertura = request(app.getHttpServer())
          .get(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversaId}/messages`).set('Cookie', cookieOwner).expect(200);
        await Promise.all([...chegadas, abertura]);

        const aposConcorrencia = (await prisma.conversation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: conversaId } })).unreadCount;
        expect(aposConcorrencia).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);
        expect(aposConcorrencia).toBeLessThanOrEqual(10);
        await receber(`apos-fronteira-${lote}`);
        expect((await prisma.conversation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: conversaId } })).unreadCount).toBe(aposConcorrencia + 1);
      }
    });

    it('preserva dez incrementos feitos após o ack e enquanto a página fica bloqueada, em três lotes', async () => {
      for (let lote = 0; lote < 3; lote++) {
        await prisma.conversation.update({ where: { id: conversaId }, data: { unreadCount: 0 } });
        let marcouLock!: () => void;
        let liberaLock!: () => void;
        const lockAdquirido = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { marcouLock = resolve; });
        const aguardaLiberação = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { liberaLock = resolve; });
        const transacaoLock = prisma.$transaction(async (tx) => {
          await tx.$executeRawUnsafe('LOCK TABLE "messages" IN ACCESS EXCLUSIVE MODE');
          marcouLock();
          await aguardaLiberação;
        }, { timeout: 30_000 });
        await lockAdquirido;

        const inicioLeitura = performance.now();
        const leitura = request(app.getHttpServer())
          .get(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversaId}/messages`)
          .set('Cookie', cookieOwner).then((res) => res);
        let esperaNoSelect = false;
        let consultas = 0;
        for (let tentativa = 0; tentativa < 100; tentativa++) {
          const bloqueadas = await prisma.$queryRaw<Array<{ count: number }>>`
            SELECT COUNT(*)::int AS count
            FROM pg_stat_activity
            WHERE datname = current_database()
              AND wait_event_type = 'Lock'
              AND query ILIKE '%messages%'
              AND query ILIKE '%SELECT%'
          `;
          consultas++;
          if (Number(bloqueadas[0]?.count ?? 0) > 0) {
            esperaNoSelect = true;
            break;
          }
          await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 20));
        }

        try {
          expect(esperaNoSelect, `GET precisa estar bloqueado no SELECT de messages no lote ${lote + 1}`).toBe(true);
          const inicioIncrementos = performance.now();
          await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: 10 }, () => prisma.conversation.updateMany({
            where: { id: conversaId }, data: { unreadCount: { increment: 1 } },
          })));
          const aposIncrementos = (await prisma.conversation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: conversaId } })).unreadCount;
          expect(aposIncrementos).toBe(10);
          liberaLock();
          const resposta = await leitura;
          expect(resposta.status).toBe(200);
          const persistido = (await prisma.conversation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: conversaId } })).unreadCount;
          expect(persistido).toBe(10);
          console.info(JSON.stringify({
            prova: 'BCI_ACK_COM_SELECT_BLOQUEADO', lote: lote + 1,
            bloqueioDetectado: esperaNoSelect, sondagens: consultas,
            ackAteSelectBloqueadoMs: Math.round(performance.now() - inicioLeitura),
            dezIncrementosMs: Math.round(performance.now() - inicioIncrementos),
            unreadEnquantoSelectBloqueado: aposIncrementos, unreadDepoisDaResposta: persistido,
          }));
        } finally {
          liberaLock();
          await leitura.catch(() => undefined);
          await transacaoLock;
        }
        await receber(`apos-ack-bloqueado-${lote}`);
        expect((await prisma.conversation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: conversaId } })).unreadCount).toBe(11);
      }
    });

    it('permite leituras de cursor concorrentes com close sem reescrever status', async () => {
      for (let lote = 0; lote < 3; lote++) {
        const mensagens = Array.from({ length: 10 }, (_, i) => prisma.message.create({ data: {
          conversationId: conversaId, buId: A.businessUnit.id, direction: 'IN', messageType: 'TEXTO',
          content: `corrida ${lote}-${i}`, channelOrigin: 'OFICIAL', status: 'RECEBIDA',
          createdAt: new Date(Date.now() + i),
        } }));
        await Promise.all(mensagens);
        const primeira = await request(app.getHttpServer())
          .get(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversaId}/messages?limite=1&marcarLida=false`)
          .set('Cookie', cookieOwner).expect(200);
        const cursor = primeira.body.paginacao.cursorAnterior;
        expect(cursor).toBeTruthy();
        const leituras = Array.from({ length: 10 }, () => request(app.getHttpServer())
          .get(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversaId}/messages?limite=1&marcarLida=false&antes=${encodeURIComponent(cursor)}`)
          .set('Cookie', cookieOwner).expect(200));
        const fechamento = request(app.getHttpServer())
          .post(`/api/bff/conversations/${conversaId}/close`).set('Cookie', cookieOwner).set('x-requested-with', 'teste').expect(201);
        const respostas = await Promise.all([...leituras, fechamento]);
        expect(respostas.slice(0, 10).every((r) => r.body.messages.length <= 1)).toBe(true);
        expect((await prisma.conversation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: conversaId } })).status).toBe('CLOSED');
      }
    });
  });
});
