import { INestApplication } from '@nestjs/common';
import { createHmac } from 'node:crypto';
import request from 'supertest';
import { afterAll, beforeAll, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { PrismaService } from '../src/infra/prisma/prisma.service';
import { EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_SECRET, META_APP_SECRET, criarCanalVerificado, criarMantenedora, limparBanco, metaFake, evolutionFake, subirApp, typebotFake } from './helpers';

describe('Typebot tenant, trigger and resumed session isolation (PostgreSQL)', () => {
  let app: INestApplication;
  let prisma: PrismaService;
  let a: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof criarMantenedora>>;
  let b: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof criarMantenedora>>;
  const phoneId = 'typebot-resource-a';
  const contact = '5584997770000';

  beforeAll(async () => { ({ app, prisma } = await subirApp()); });
  afterAll(async () => { if (prisma) await limparBanco(prisma); await app?.close(); });
  beforeEach(async () => {
    await limparBanco(prisma);
    a = await criarMantenedora(app, 'Typebot tenant A', 'typebot-a@example.test');
    b = await criarMantenedora(app, 'Typebot tenant B', 'typebot-b@example.test');
    await criarCanalVerificado(prisma, a.businessUnit.id, 'OFICIAL', phoneId, { provedWabaId: 'synthetic-typebot-current-waba' });
    typebotFake.enabled = true;
    metaFake.enviados = [];
  });

  function trigger(id: string) {
    const webhook = { entry: [{ changes: [{ field: 'messages', value: { metadata: { phone_number_id: phoneId }, messages: [{ id, from: contact, timestamp: String(Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000)), type: 'text', text: { body: 'ajuda' } }] } }] }] };
    const signature = `sha256=${createHmac('sha256', META_APP_SECRET).update(JSON.stringify(webhook)).digest('hex')}`;
    return request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/webhooks/meta').set('x-hub-signature-256', signature).send(webhook);
  }

  it('does not resume a session stored under another tenant and ignores a foreign global trigger', async () => {
    const botA = await prisma.typebotBot.create({ data: { mantenedoraId: a.mantenedora.id, typebotId: 'bot-a', publishedTypebotId: 'published-a', status: 'ATIVO', name: 'A', editorUrl: 'https://typebot.invalid/a' } });
    const botB = await prisma.typebotBot.create({ data: { mantenedoraId: b.mantenedora.id, typebotId: 'bot-b', publishedTypebotId: 'published-b', name: 'B', editorUrl: 'https://typebot.invalid/b' } });
    await prisma.typebotTrigger.create({ data: { botId: botA.id, kind: 'NOVA_CONVERSA', value: '*', buId: a.businessUnit.id } });
    await prisma.typebotTrigger.create({ data: { botId: botB.id, kind: 'NOVA_CONVERSA', value: '*', buId: null } });
    await prisma.typebotSession.create({ data: { botId: botA.id, mantenedoraId: b.mantenedora.id, buId: a.businessUnit.id, contato: contact, sessionId: 'foreign-session', ativo: true } });

    await trigger('typebot-foreign-session').expect(200);
    expect(typebotFake.continues).toBe(0);
    expect(typebotFake.starts).toBe(0);
    expect(metaFake.enviados).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(await prisma.message.count({ where: { buId: a.businessUnit.id } })).toBe(1);
  });

  it('uses only the same tenant trigger, derives its session owner from the BU, and accepts the bot reply through the keyed writer', async () => {
    const botA = await prisma.typebotBot.create({ data: { mantenedoraId: a.mantenedora.id, typebotId: 'bot-a', publishedTypebotId: 'published-a', status: 'ATIVO', name: 'A', editorUrl: 'https://typebot.invalid/a' } });
    await prisma.typebotTrigger.create({ data: { botId: botA.id, kind: 'NOVA_CONVERSA', value: '*', buId: a.businessUnit.id } });
    await trigger('typebot-tenant-a').expect(200);
    const session = await prisma.typebotSession.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { botId_buId_contato: { botId: botA.id, buId: a.businessUnit.id, contato: contact } } });
    expect(session.mantenedoraId).toBe(a.mantenedora.id);
    expect(typebotFake.starts).toBe(1);
    expect(metaFake.enviados).toHaveLength(1);
    const outbound = await prisma.message.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { buId: a.businessUnit.id, direction: 'OUT', externalId: 'wamid-enviado-1' } });
    expect(outbound).toMatchObject({ authorName: 'Typebot', status: 'ENVIADA', providerResourceIdentityId: expect.any(String) });
  });

  it('pins a held bot reply to its verified ingress and preserves the live human queue state', async () => {
    const evolution = await criarCanalVerificado(prisma, a.businessUnit.id, 'NAO_OFICIAL', 'typebot-later-origin', { requiresWindow: false });
    const bot = await prisma.typebotBot.create({ data: { mantenedoraId: a.mantenedora.id, typebotId: 'bot-held-origin', publishedTypebotId: 'published-held-origin', status: 'ATIVO', name: 'A', editorUrl: 'https://typebot.invalid/a' } });
    await prisma.typebotTrigger.create({ data: { botId: bot.id, kind: 'NOVA_CONVERSA', value: '*', buId: a.businessUnit.id } });
    let markEntered!: () => void;
    let release!: () => void;
    const entered = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { markEntered = resolve; });
    const gate = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { release = resolve; });
    typebotFake.beforeReply = async () => {
      markEntered();
      await gate;
      const conversation = await prisma.conversation.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { buId: a.businessUnit.id, contact } });
      await prisma.message.create({ data: {
        conversationId: conversation.id, buId: a.businessUnit.id, direction: 'IN', messageType: 'TEXTO', content: 'later different origin',
        channelOrigin: 'NAO_OFICIAL', status: 'RECEBIDA', providerResourceIdentityId: evolution.identity.id, externalId: 'typebot-later-origin-event',
      } });
      await prisma.conversation.update({ where: { id: conversation.id }, data: {
        status: 'QUEUED', unreadCount: 7, attendantUserId: a.owner.id, attendantName: 'Human owner', attendantOrigin: 'INTERNO',
      } });
    };
    const pending = trigger('typebot-held-origin').then((response) => response);
    await entered;
    release();
    expect((await pending).status).toBe(200);
    expect(metaFake.enviados).toEqual([{ para: contact, texto: 'resposta do bot' }]);
    expect(evolutionFake.enviados).toHaveLength(0);
    const conversation = await prisma.conversation.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { buId: a.businessUnit.id, contact } });
    expect(conversation).toMatchObject({ status: 'QUEUED', unreadCount: 7, attendantUserId: a.owner.id, attendantName: 'Human owner', attendantOrigin: 'INTERNO' });
  });

  it('does not dispatch a held bot reply after its required 24-hour window expires', async () => {
    const bot = await prisma.typebotBot.create({ data: { mantenedoraId: a.mantenedora.id, typebotId: 'bot-held-window', publishedTypebotId: 'published-held-window', status: 'ATIVO', name: 'A', editorUrl: 'https://typebot.invalid/a' } });
    await prisma.typebotTrigger.create({ data: { botId: bot.id, kind: 'NOVA_CONVERSA', value: '*', buId: a.businessUnit.id } });
    let markEntered!: () => void;
    let release!: () => void;
    const entered = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { markEntered = resolve; });
    const gate = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { release = resolve; });
    typebotFake.beforeReply = async () => {
      markEntered();
      await gate;
      const inbound = await prisma.message.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { buId: a.businessUnit.id, externalId: 'typebot-held-window' } });
      await prisma.message.update({ where: { id: inbound.id }, data: {
        createdAt: new Date(Date.now() - 25 * 60 * 60 * 1000),
        officialInboundAt: new Date(Date.now() - 25 * 60 * 60 * 1000),
      } });
    };
    const pending = trigger('typebot-held-window').then((response) => response);
    await entered;
    release();
    expect((await pending).status).toBe(200);
    expect(metaFake.enviados).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(await prisma.message.count({ where: { buId: a.businessUnit.id, direction: 'OUT' } })).toBe(0);
  });

  it('does not rebind a held bot reply after its ingress identity is retired', async () => {
    const binding = await prisma.buChannel.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { buId: a.businessUnit.id, type: 'OFICIAL' }, include: { providerIdentities: true } });
    const identity = binding.providerIdentities.find((row) => row.currentChannelId === binding.id)!;
    const bot = await prisma.typebotBot.create({ data: { mantenedoraId: a.mantenedora.id, typebotId: 'bot-held-retire', publishedTypebotId: 'published-held-retire', status: 'ATIVO', name: 'A', editorUrl: 'https://typebot.invalid/a' } });
    await prisma.typebotTrigger.create({ data: { botId: bot.id, kind: 'NOVA_CONVERSA', value: '*', buId: a.businessUnit.id } });
    let markEntered!: () => void;
    let release!: () => void;
    const entered = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { markEntered = resolve; });
    const gate = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { release = resolve; });
    typebotFake.beforeReply = async () => { markEntered(); await gate; await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.update({ where: { id: identity.id }, data: { currentChannelId: null, retiredAt: new Date() } }); };
    const pending = trigger('typebot-held-retire').then((response) => response);
    await entered;
    release();
    expect((await pending).status).toBe(200);
    expect(metaFake.enviados).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(await prisma.message.count({ where: { buId: a.businessUnit.id, direction: 'OUT' } })).toBe(0);
  });

  it('selects the matching trigger from the current tenant when an earlier foreign global trigger also matches', async () => {
    const botB = await prisma.typebotBot.create({ data: { mantenedoraId: b.mantenedora.id, typebotId: 'foreign-global-bot', publishedTypebotId: 'foreign-global-published', status: 'ATIVO', name: 'Foreign', editorUrl: 'https://typebot.invalid/foreign' } });
    const botA = await prisma.typebotBot.create({ data: { mantenedoraId: a.mantenedora.id, typebotId: 'tenant-global-bot', publishedTypebotId: 'tenant-global-published', status: 'ATIVO', name: 'Tenant', editorUrl: 'https://typebot.invalid/tenant' } });
    const foreignTrigger = await prisma.typebotTrigger.create({ data: { botId: botB.id, kind: 'NOVA_CONVERSA', value: '*', buId: null, createdAt: new Date(Date.now() - 60_000) } });
    await prisma.typebotTrigger.create({ data: { botId: botA.id, kind: 'NOVA_CONVERSA', value: '*', buId: null } });
    expect(foreignTrigger.createdAt.getTime()).toBeLessThan(Date.now() - 1_000);

    await trigger('typebot-global-trigger-tenant-order').expect(200);

    expect(typebotFake.startedBots).toEqual([{ botId: 'tenant-global-published', contact, text: 'ajuda' }]);
    const session = await prisma.typebotSession.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { botId_buId_contato: { botId: botA.id, buId: a.businessUnit.id, contato: contact } } });
    expect(session).toMatchObject({ mantenedoraId: a.mantenedora.id, buId: a.businessUnit.id, botId: botA.id });
    expect(await prisma.typebotSession.count({ where: { botId: botB.id } })).toBe(0);
    expect(metaFake.credentialCalls).toEqual([{ phoneNumberId: phoneId, accessToken: 'fixture-scoped-token' }]);
    expect(metaFake.enviados).toEqual([{ para: contact, texto: 'resposta do bot' }]);
    const outbound = await prisma.message.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { buId: a.businessUnit.id, direction: 'OUT', authorName: 'Typebot' }, include: { providerResourceIdentity: true } });
    expect(outbound.providerResourceIdentity).toMatchObject({ buId: a.businessUnit.id, externalResourceId: phoneId });
    expect(outbound.content).not.toContain('foreign');
  });

  it('records a Typebot send without a provider ID as an ambiguous outcome and does not resend', async () => {
    const botA = await prisma.typebotBot.create({ data: { mantenedoraId: a.mantenedora.id, typebotId: 'bot-null-id', publishedTypebotId: 'published-null-id', status: 'ATIVO', name: 'A', editorUrl: 'https://typebot.invalid/a' } });
    await prisma.typebotTrigger.create({ data: { botId: botA.id, kind: 'NOVA_CONVERSA', value: '*', buId: a.businessUnit.id } });
    metaFake.sendIdNull = true;
    await trigger('typebot-null-id').expect(200);
    expect(metaFake.enviados).toHaveLength(1);
    const failed = await prisma.message.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { buId: a.businessUnit.id, direction: 'OUT', externalId: null } });
    expect(failed).toMatchObject({ status: 'FALHOU', authorName: 'Typebot', providerResourceIdentityId: expect.any(String) });
    expect(failed.content).toBe('resposta do bot');
  });

  it('stops a two-text Typebot reply after the first UNKNOWN outbound and contains the webhook round', async () => {
    const bot = await prisma.typebotBot.create({ data: { mantenedoraId: a.mantenedora.id, typebotId: 'bot-two-text-unknown', publishedTypebotId: 'published-two-text-unknown', status: 'ATIVO', name: 'A', editorUrl: 'https://typebot.invalid/a' } });
    await prisma.typebotTrigger.create({ data: { botId: bot.id, kind: 'NOVA_CONVERSA', value: '*', buId: a.businessUnit.id } });
    const firstText = 'primeira saída incerta';
    const secondText = 'segunda saída não pode sair';
    const start = vi.spyOn(typebotFake, 'iniciar').mockResolvedValue({ sessionId: 'two-text-session', messages: [
      { type: 'text', content: { text: firstText } },
      { type: 'text', content: { text: secondText } },
    ] });
    const providerCallsBefore = metaFake.providerCalls;
    metaFake.sendIdNull = true;
    try {
      await trigger('typebot-two-text-unknown').expect(200);
      expect(metaFake.enviados).toEqual([{ para: contact, texto: firstText }]);
      expect(metaFake.providerCalls - providerCallsBefore).toBe(1);

      const conversation = await prisma.conversation.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { buId: a.businessUnit.id, contact } });
      const inbound = await prisma.message.findMany({ where: { conversationId: conversation.id, direction: 'IN' } });
      const outbound = await prisma.message.findMany({ where: { conversationId: conversation.id, direction: 'OUT' } });
      expect(inbound).toHaveLength(1);
      expect(outbound).toHaveLength(1);
      expect(outbound[0]).toMatchObject({ content: firstText, externalId: null, status: 'FALHOU', authorName: 'Typebot' });
      expect(outbound[0].content).not.toBe(secondText);
      expect(conversation).toMatchObject({ status: 'QUEUED', unreadCount: 1, attendantUserId: null, attendantName: null });
      expect(start).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
    } finally {
      start.mockRestore();
      metaFake.sendIdNull = false;
    }
  });

  it('reconciles a Typebot Evolution echo delivered inside send and ignores a later duplicate echo', async () => {
    const setor = await prisma.businessUnit.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: a.businessUnit.id }, select: { setorId: true } });
    const bu = await prisma.businessUnit.create({ data: { mantenedoraId: a.mantenedora.id, setorId: setor.setorId, name: 'Typebot Evolution BU' } });
    const instance = 'typebot-evolution-resource';
    await criarCanalVerificado(prisma, bu.id, 'NAO_OFICIAL', instance, { requiresWindow: false });
    const bot = await prisma.typebotBot.create({ data: {
      mantenedoraId: a.mantenedora.id, typebotId: 'evolution-bot', publishedTypebotId: 'evolution-published',
      name: 'Evolution bot', editorUrl: 'https://typebot.invalid/evolution',
    } });
    await prisma.typebotTrigger.create({ data: { botId: bot.id, kind: 'NOVA_CONVERSA', value: '*', buId: bu.id } });
    await prisma.typebotTrigger.create({ data: { botId: bot.id, kind: 'PALAVRA_CHAVE', value: 'ajuda', buId: bu.id } });
    let echoRow: { id: string; createdAt: Date } | null = null;
    evolutionFake.sendHook = async (id, para, texto) => {
      const webhook = { event: 'messages.upsert', instance, data: {
        key: { remoteJid: `${para}@s.whatsapp.net`, fromMe: true, id }, message: { conversation: `${texto} provider echo` },
      } };
      await request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/webhooks/evolution').set('x-leadshug-webhook-secret', EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_SECRET).send(webhook).expect(200);
      echoRow = await prisma.message.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { providerResourceIdentity: { externalResourceId: instance }, externalId: id }, select: { id: true, createdAt: true } });
    };
    const inbound = { event: 'messages.upsert', instance, data: {
      key: { remoteJid: `${contact}@s.whatsapp.net`, fromMe: false, id: 'evo-typebot-trigger' },
      pushName: 'Cliente', message: { conversation: 'ajuda' },
    } };
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/webhooks/evolution').set('x-leadshug-webhook-secret', EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_SECRET).send(inbound).expect(200);
    evolutionFake.sendHook = null;
    const outbound = await prisma.message.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { providerResourceIdentity: { externalResourceId: instance }, externalId: 'evo-enviado-1' } });
    expect(outbound.id).toBe(echoRow?.id);
    expect(outbound.createdAt).toEqual(echoRow?.createdAt);
    expect(outbound).toMatchObject({ direction: 'OUT', content: 'resposta do bot', authorName: 'Typebot' });
    expect(await prisma.message.count({ where: { providerResourceIdentity: { externalResourceId: instance }, externalId: 'evo-enviado-1' } })).toBe(1);
    expect(await prisma.message.count({ where: { buId: bu.id, direction: 'IN' } })).toBe(1);
    const conversation = await prisma.conversation.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { buId: bu.id, contact } });
    expect(conversation.unreadCount).toBe(1);
    expect(typebotFake.starts).toBe(1);
    expect(evolutionFake.enviados).toHaveLength(1);

    const laterEcho = { event: 'messages.upsert', instance, data: {
      key: { remoteJid: `${contact}@s.whatsapp.net`, fromMe: true, id: 'evo-enviado-1' }, message: { conversation: 'untrusted duplicate echo' },
    } };
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/webhooks/evolution').set('x-leadshug-webhook-secret', EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_SECRET).send(laterEcho).expect(200);
    const afterEcho = await prisma.message.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { externalId: 'evo-enviado-1' } });
    expect(afterEcho).toMatchObject({ id: outbound.id, createdAt: outbound.createdAt, content: 'resposta do bot', authorName: 'Typebot' });
    expect(await prisma.conversation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: conversation.id } })).toMatchObject({ unreadCount: 1 });
    expect(typebotFake.starts).toBe(1);

    await prisma.typebotSession.updateMany({ where: { botId: bot.id, buId: bu.id, contato: contact }, data: { ativo: false } });
    const secondInbound = { event: 'messages.upsert', instance, data: {
      key: { remoteJid: `${contact}@s.whatsapp.net`, fromMe: false, id: 'evo-typebot-sender-first-trigger' },
      pushName: 'Cliente', message: { conversation: 'ajuda' },
    } };
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/webhooks/evolution').set('x-leadshug-webhook-secret', EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_SECRET).send(secondInbound).expect(200);
    const senderFirst = await prisma.message.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { providerResourceIdentity: { externalResourceId: instance }, externalId: 'evo-enviado-2' } });
    const lateEcho = { event: 'messages.upsert', instance, data: {
      key: { remoteJid: `${contact}@s.whatsapp.net`, fromMe: true, id: 'evo-enviado-2' }, message: { conversation: 'late untrusted Typebot echo' },
    } };
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/webhooks/evolution').set('x-leadshug-webhook-secret', EVOLUTION_WEBHOOK_SECRET).send(lateEcho).expect(200);
    const senderFirstAfterEcho = await prisma.message.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: senderFirst.id } });
    expect(senderFirstAfterEcho).toMatchObject({ createdAt: senderFirst.createdAt, content: 'resposta do bot', authorName: 'Typebot' });
    expect(await prisma.message.count({ where: { providerResourceIdentity: { externalResourceId: instance }, externalId: 'evo-enviado-2' } })).toBe(1);
    expect(typebotFake.starts).toBe(2);
    expect(evolutionFake.enviados).toHaveLength(2);
  });
});

import { createHash, createHmac, randomBytes, randomUUID, timingSafeEqual } from 'node:crypto';

import { AccessService } from '../access/access.service';

import { PrismaService } from '../../infra/prisma/prisma.service';

import { loadChannelCredentialKey, openChannelCredential, sealChannelCredential } from './channel-credential-crypto';
import { SessionService } from '../../infra/session/session.service';
import { RegistroDeAtividade } from '../historico/registro.service';

const TTL = 10 * 60_000;
const BUDGET_WINDOW_MS = 60 * 60_000;
const KEY = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-f]{3}-[89ab][0-9a-f]{3}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i;
const RECEIPT_CONFIRMED = Symbol('INSTAGRAM_RECEIPT_CONFIRMED');
type SubscriptionClockFacts = { deadlineAt: Date; attemptExpiresAt: Date; credentialExpiresAt: Date | null; bindingLeaseUntil: Date; attemptLeaseUntil: Date };

export interface InstagramActor { id: string; mantenedoraId: string; sessionToken: string; nome: string; email: string; ip?: string; }

@Injectable()
export class InstagramAuthorizationService {
  constructor(private readonly prisma: PrismaService, private readonly access: AccessService, private readonly http: InstagramHttpClient, private readonly sessions: SessionService, private readonly activity: RegistroDeAtividade) {}

  capabilities() {
    const configured = Boolean(instagramConfig());
    const cryptoReady = (() => { try { loadChannelCredentialKey(process.env); return true; } catch { return false; } })();
    return { instagram: { available: configured && cryptoReady, reason: !configured ? 'NOT_CONFIGURED' : cryptoReady ? 'AVAILABLE' : 'CREDENTIAL_ENCRYPTION_UNAVAILABLE', apiVersion: 'v26.0' } };
  }

  async create(actor: InstagramActor, buId: string, idempotencyKey: string, body: { operation: 'CONNECT' | 'REAUTHORIZE'; channelId?: string; expectedGeneration?: number }) {
    const config = instagramConfig();
    if (!config || !this.capabilities().instagram.available) throw new ServiceUnavailableException({ code: 'INSTAGRAM_UNAVAILABLE' });
    if (!KEY.test(idempotencyKey)) throw new BadRequestException({ code: 'IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_INVALID' });
    const sessionHash = hash(actor.sessionToken);
    const requestHash = hash(JSON.stringify({ buId, ...body }));
    let expectedCredentialVersion: number | null = null;
    const { attempt, replay } = await this.actorTransaction(actor, async (tx) => {
      await this.requireActorTx(tx, actor, buId);
      await tx.$queryRaw`SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended(${'instagram-attempt-actor:' + actor.id}, 0))::text AS locked`;
      await tx.$queryRaw`SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended(${'instagram-attempt-bu:' + buId}, 0))::text AS locked`;
      const prior = await tx.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.findUnique({ where: { mantenedoraId_idempotencyKey: { mantenedoraId: actor.mantenedoraId, idempotencyKey } } });
      if (prior) {
        if (prior.userId !== actor.id || prior.sessionHash !== sessionHash) throw new ConflictException({ code: 'ATTEMPT_SESSION_MISMATCH' });
        if (prior.requestHash !== requestHash) throw new ConflictException({ code: 'IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT' });
        return { attempt: prior, replay: true };
      }
      const bu = await tx.businessUnit.findFirst({ where: { id: buId, mantenedoraId: actor.mantenedoraId }, select: { id: true } });
      if (!bu) throw new NotFoundException({ code: 'NAO_ENCONTRADO' });
      if (body.operation === 'REAUTHORIZE') {
        const initial = await tx.buChannel.findFirst({ where: { id: body.channelId, buId, type: 'INSTAGRAM' }, select: { id: true, businessIdentity: { select: { externalResourceId: true } } } });
        if (!initial) throw new NotFoundException({ code: 'NAO_ENCONTRADO' });
        if (!initial.businessIdentity) throw new ConflictException({ code: 'BUSINESS_RESOURCE_OWNER_CONFLICT' });
        const accountId = initial.businessIdentity.externalResourceId;
        await this.lockMappedResource(tx, accountId);
        await tx.$queryRaw`SELECT id FROM bu_channels WHERE id = ${initial.id} FOR UPDATE`;
        await this.requireActorTx(tx, actor, buId);
        const channel = await tx.buChannel.findFirst({ where: { id: initial.id, buId, type: 'INSTAGRAM' } });
        if (!channel || channel.generation !== body.expectedGeneration || channel.businessIdentityId === null) {
          throw new ConflictException({ code: 'CHANNEL_GENERATION_STALE' });
        }
        const identity = await tx.businessChannelIdentity.findFirst({ where: { id: channel.businessIdentityId, buId,
          platform: 'INSTAGRAM', namespace: 'INSTAGRAM_LOGIN', externalResourceId: accountId } });
        const owner = await tx.providerResourceIdentity.findFirst({ where: { provider: 'INSTAGRAM', externalResourceId: accountId,
          buId, currentChannelId: channel.id, businessIdentityId: identity?.id, retiredAt: null } });
        if (!identity || !owner) throw new ConflictException({ code: 'BUSINESS_RESOURCE_OWNER_CONFLICT' });
        const credential = await tx.channelCredential.findUnique({ where: { channelId: channel.id }, select: { provider: true, generation: true, version: true } });
        if (credential) {
          if (credential.provider !== 'INSTAGRAM' || credential.generation !== channel.generation) throw new ConflictException({ code: 'CREDENTIAL_UNAVAILABLE' });
          expectedCredentialVersion = credential.version;
        } else if (channel.status !== 'DESCONECTADO' || owner.currentChannelId !== channel.id) {

      const [actorCount, buCount] = await Promise.all([
        tx.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.count({ where: { userId: actor.id, createdAt: { gte: since } } }),
        tx.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.count({ where: { buId, createdAt: { gte: since } } }),
      ]);
      if (actorCount >= 10 || buCount >= 30) {
        const [actorOldest, buOldest] = await Promise.all([
          actorCount >= 10 ? tx.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.findFirst({ where: { userId: actor.id, createdAt: { gte: since } }, orderBy: { createdAt: 'asc' }, select: { createdAt: true } }) : null,
          buCount >= 30 ? tx.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.findFirst({ where: { buId, createdAt: { gte: since } }, orderBy: { createdAt: 'asc' }, select: { createdAt: true } }) : null,
        ]);
        const resetTimes = [actorOldest, buOldest].filter((row): row is { createdAt: Date } => row !== null)
          .map(({ createdAt }) => createdAt.getTime() + BUDGET_WINDOW_MS);

      }

        id, mantenedoraId: actor.mantenedoraId, buId, userId: actor.id, sessionHash,
        idempotencyKey, requestHash, operation: body.operation, channelId: body.channelId ?? null,
        expectedGeneration: body.expectedGeneration ?? null, stateHash: hash(state), expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() + TTL),
        expectedCredentialVersion,
      } });
      await this.activity.registrar(tx, actor.mantenedoraId, 'instagram.autorizacao.iniciada', this.auditActor(actor), { buId, entidadeId: id });
      return { attempt: created, replay: false };
    });
    if (replay) return this.authorizationDto(attempt, config);
    return this.authorizationDto(attempt, config);
  }

  async authorizeBusinessUnit(actor: InstagramActor, buId: string) {
    await this.requireAdmin(actor, buId);
  }

  async authorizeLiveBusinessUnit(actor: InstagramActor, buId: string) {
    await this.requireLiveActor(actor, buId);
  }

  async authorizeBusinessUnitInTransaction(actor: InstagramActor, buId: string, tx: Prisma.TransactionClient) {
    await this.requireActorTx(tx, actor, buId);
  }

  async authorizeAttempt(actor: InstagramActor, id: string) {
    await this.requireAttempt(actor, id);
  }

  /** Explicit, read-only verification for a durable current Instagram binding. */
  async verifyConnection(actor: InstagramActor, buId: string, channelId: string, expectedGeneration: number): Promise<{ observation: 'UNKNOWN' }> {

    const config = instagramConfig();
    if (!config) throw new ServiceUnavailableException({ code: 'INSTAGRAM_UNAVAILABLE' });
    const lease = randomBytes(24).toString('hex');
    const deadlineAt = new Date(Date.now() + 40_000);
    let leaseAccountId: string | null = null;
    try {
      const binding = await this.actorTransaction(actor, async (tx) => {
        await this.requireActorTx(tx, actor, buId);
        const initial = await tx.buChannel.findFirst({ where: { id: channelId, buId, type: 'INSTAGRAM' },
          select: { id: true, businessIdentity: { select: { externalResourceId: true } } } });
        if (!initial) throw new NotFoundException({ code: 'NAO_ENCONTRADO' });
        if (!initial.businessIdentity?.externalResourceId) throw new ConflictException({ code: 'BUSINESS_RESOURCE_OWNER_CONFLICT' });
        const accountId = initial.businessIdentity.externalResourceId;
        await this.lockMappedResource(tx, accountId);
        await tx.$queryRaw`SELECT id FROM business_units WHERE id = ${buId} FOR UPDATE`;
        await tx.$queryRaw`SELECT id FROM bu_channels WHERE id = ${channelId} FOR UPDATE`;
        await this.requireActorTx(tx, actor, buId);
        const channel = await tx.buChannel.findFirst({ where: { id: channelId, buId, type: 'INSTAGRAM' } });
        const business = await tx.businessChannelIdentity.findFirst({ where: { id: channel?.businessIdentityId ?? '', buId, platform: 'INSTAGRAM', namespace: 'INSTAGRAM_LOGIN', externalResourceId: accountId } });
        const owner = await tx.providerResourceIdentity.findFirst({ where: { provider: 'INSTAGRAM', externalResourceId: accountId, buId, currentChannelId: channelId, businessIdentityId: business?.id, retiredAt: null } });
        const credential = await tx.channelCredential.findFirst({ where: { channelId, buId, provider: 'INSTAGRAM', generation: expectedGeneration } });
        if (!channel || !business || !owner || !credential) throw new ConflictException({ code: 'CHANNEL_GENERATION_STALE' });
        if (channel.generation !== expectedGeneration) throw new ConflictException({ code: 'CHANNEL_GENERATION_STALE' });
        if (!channel.ownNumberVerified || channel.verifiedEpoch !== channel.pairingEpoch || channel.status === 'DESCONECTADO') {

        }

        const now = new Date();
        if (owner.instagramOperationLeaseUntil && owner.instagramOperationLeaseUntil > now) throw new ConflictException({ code: 'OPERATION_IN_PROGRESS' });
        if (owner.instagramLastSubscriptionAttemptAt && now.getTime() - owner.instagramLastSubscriptionAttemptAt.getTime() < 10_000) {

            instagram_last_subscription_attempt_at = clock_timestamp()
          WHERE id = ${owner.id} AND current_channel_id = ${channelId}

        return { channelId, accountId, generation: channel.generation, version: credential.version, identityId: owner.id,

        ciphertext: Buffer.from(credential.ciphertext), tag: Buffer.from(credential.tag) }, new Map([[key.keyId, key.key]]), {

  async disconnectConnection(actor: InstagramActor, buId: string, channelId: string, expectedGeneration: number) {

    return this.actorTransaction(actor, async (tx) => {
      await this.requireActorTx(tx, actor, buId);
      const initial = await tx.buChannel.findFirst({ where: { id: channelId, buId, type: 'INSTAGRAM' },
        select: { businessIdentity: { select: { externalResourceId: true } } } });
      if (!initial) throw new NotFoundException({ code: 'NAO_ENCONTRADO' });
      const accountId = initial.businessIdentity?.externalResourceId;

      await this.lockMappedResource(tx, accountId);

      await tx.$queryRaw`SELECT id FROM bu_channels WHERE id = ${channelId} FOR UPDATE`;
